\textbf{Executive status: P versus NP remains OPEN.}
The single active research spine is ARC/FLMV simultaneous time--space
contraction.  No all-input or typed favorable successor route is proved.
The strongest current endpoint is one fixed locally indexed complete/hard
SAT image, but every structural promise, recursive type and charged
transport into a contractor remains a separate obligation.
}}
\end{center}

\section*{Canonical model and strict endpoint}
The all-input achievable set $E$ contains $(t,s)$ exactly when one fixed
uniform deterministic total exact ordinary-SAT decider, correct on every
ordinary $N$-bit input, jointly uses time $N^{t+o(1)}$ and writable/index
space $N^{s+o(1)}$ in the FLMV indexed read-only-input random-access
Turing-machine model.  Under $\mathrm P=\mathrm{NP}$, some finite diagonal
$(K,K)$ lies in $E$.  Write
\[
 c_\star=\frac{241}{200},\qquad
 d_\star=\frac{11642479}{23232400}
 =\frac12\left(\frac{c_\star+2}{c_\star^2}-c_\star\right).
\]
A finite charged route from cofinally many achievable diagonals into
$t<c_\star,s<d_\star$ would contradict FLMV.  It also suffices to build one
fixed uniform program that is exact and has those joint bounds on every
member of one fixed locally indexed FLMV-hard image.  The latter program
need not recognize the image or be correct off-image.

\[
\boxed{\mathrm P=\mathrm{NP}}
\Longrightarrow\boxed{(K,K)\in E}
\mathrel{\mathop{\Longrightarrow}^{\text{\scriptsize OPEN charged route}}}
\boxed{\text{strict-box solver on all inputs or one fixed hard image}}
\Longrightarrow\boxed{\text{contradiction}}.
\]
The illustrative map
$(t,s)\mapsto(\max\{11/10,3/5+t/2\},\max\{1/2,s/2\})$ has verified
finite-entry arithmetic only.  Its machine-producing edge is \OPEN.  The
universal $t+s<2\cos(\pi/7)$ endpoint is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}.

\section*{Typed composition and the current promise gate}
For fixed ordinary-input promises $\mathcal H_0,\ldots,\mathcal H_d$, exact
fixed-depth composition is valid when every level-$j$ contractor handles
every parent in $\mathcal H_j$, emits only children proved to lie in
$\mathcal H_{j-1}$, and has an exact both-label join.  After all eager/lazy
access, caches, ancestors and adapters are included locally,
\[
 t_j=\max\{r_j,a_j+\lambda_jt_{j-1}\},\qquad
 s_j=\max\{h_j,\lambda_js_{j-1}\}.
\]
This is \textbf{PROVED/EXACT}.  Automatic inheritance of a reduction-image
promise under ordinary restriction is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}.  A fixed
locally complete image $\mathcal H_{\rm loc}$ can repair type membership by a
paid near-linear local re-entry map, with exponent-scale
$E_{\mathcal H_{\rm loc}}=E$; the adapter preserves rather than creates a
power shrink.

The current alignment gate is therefore not only semantic membership:
\[
\boxed{\text{chosen endpoint image}}
\xrightarrow{\text{paid structural normalization}}
\boxed{\text{contractor promise with hereditary two-level types}}.
\]

\section*{Newest independent result: scope is not an image invariant}
Starting from the fixed locally complete map $J$, append a disjoint public
satisfiable grid CNF $G_q$ whose clauses are
$(u\vee v\vee z_{uv})$ for grid edges, with
$q=\lfloor\sqrt{M/\log_2(M+2)}\rfloor$ and $M=|J(F)|$.  The new fixed image
$K(F)=J(F)\wedge G_q$ preserves both labels, has length $n^{1+o(1)}$, retains
polylogarithmic addressed-bit access and still satisfies
$E_{\operatorname{im}K}=E$.

Nevertheless every OPEN/CLAUSE/CLOSE presentation of the clauses as written,
under every clause order, has live-name width
\[
 w(K(F))\ge q=|K(F)|^{1/2-o(1)}.
\]
Live-name bags form a path decomposition of the primal graph; the
row--column cross bramble of the $q\times q$ grid forces a bag of size at
least $q$.  This is \textbf{PROVED/EXACT}.  Hence
\[
\boxed{\text{local indexed completeness}}
\not\Longrightarrow
\boxed{\text{low raw scope width}}.
\]
The grid is dual-Horn, independently satisfiable and cheaply removable, so
this is not a normalizer lower bound or decision-work claim.  It inserts a
necessary explicit structural-alignment theorem between the fixed-image
endpoint and the restricted scoped contractor.  The finite audit checked
exact vertex separation through $4\times4$, direct access through
$32\times32$, and SAT/UNSAT roots with two real Shannon levels: 942 checks,
zero failures.

\section*{Current restricted firewalls}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Scoped-width contractor.}  On an already validated
$O(\log N)$-width scoped stream, fixed-depth block-and-pin recursion has exact
named joins and favorable promised-source bounds.  Endpoint alignment and
normalizer acquisition remain \OPEN.
\item \textbf{Scoped-normalizer toll.}  An unconditional normalizer on the
fixed hard image cannot simultaneously have extraction/output and
scoped-DP resources already inside the strict FLMV box; an assumed-$P=NP$
$A$-dependent favorable normalizer remains \OPEN.
\item \textbf{Nested virtual access.}  Two genuinely short ordinary
children can still incur a large repeated source-read tax in a literal lazy
emulator.  Paid caching/fusion can remove the tax on easy affine/2SAT
sources, so short length alone is neither sufficient nor compulsory work.
\item \textbf{Branch/selector and guest ledgers.}  Polynomial complete raw
fanout stays parent-scale; strict-short extensional SAT-bit selectors
eliminate; actual guest calls obey their full count--length slope.  These are
restricted no-go theorems, not a universal intensional-contractor exclusion.
\end{itemize}

\section*{Charged-edge and hostile-control ledger}
Every proposed edge must separately charge source/workspace reads, unchanged
writes, indexing, arithmetic, adaptive rule choice, failed trials, repeated
probes, guest instructions, child headers/bodies, grammar adapters, buffers,
caches, live ancestors, compatibility, reconstruction, rereads and program
size.  Eager/lazy envelopes remain
\[
 t'\le\max\{r,a+\lambda+g,a+\lambda t\},\quad
 s'\le\max\{\lambda,\lambda s,\eta,\kappa\},
\]
\[
 t'\le\max\{r,a+\lambda t+g\},\quad
 s'\le\max\{\lambda s,\eta,\kappa\}.
\]
At two lazy levels, nested bit generation must compose every source probe;
no write-only body is a free readable input.  Actual flips, physical
switches, gates/wires, cumulative bit-time, bit work and occupied storage
remain distinct.

Required controls include SAT and UNSAT image members, malformed inputs when
totality is claimed, off-image descendants, Horn/dual-Horn, SCC/2SAT,
affine/Gaussian, bounded width, graph factors, adaptive DPLL/learning, direct
recognition, source rereading, cache fusion, public padding deletion and
parent fallback.  Pathwidth transfers exactly to raw representation width,
but fails as a SAT-work transfer on the removable grid control.

\paragraph{ARC-SOURCE-INJECTIVITY-FIREWALL163 (25 October 2026, 01:55 PDT): complete-source retention cannot be a standalone power-short child.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT elementary counting, independent peer audit pending:} for each $n$, if an encoded standalone child $C_n(x)$ has a fixed decoder recovering every $x\in\{0,1\}^n$ from that child alone, then $C_n$ must be injective. The $2^n$ distinct sources require maximum child length at least $n$ binary bits (even with variable-length encoding). For the fixed tagged FLMV-hard parent of $N=n\log^{O(1)}n=n^{1+o(1)}$ bits, this rules out any worst-case bound $|C_n(x)|\le N^{\lambda+o(1)}$ with a fixed $\lambda<1$. The condition is \emph{full standalone source recovery}; a decoder which silently probes its parent is outside the theorem and all parent access/ancestor state must be paid. It does NOT constrain an arbitrary one-bit SAT decider.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT elementary multi-child budget:} if a full source is reconstructible only from the complete self-delimited transcript of $q$ children each of length at most $m$, auxiliary metadata $b$ and framing, then
$n\le b+q(m+O(\log(m+2)))+O(\log(q+2))$. Thus $a+\lambda<1$ together with sublinear metadata rules out lossless bundles at fanout $q\le N^{a+o(1)}$, size $m\le N^{\lambda+o(1)}$. At the illustrative ARC pair $(a,\lambda)=(3/5,1/2)$, $a+\lambda=11/10>1$, so this exact counting bound does \emph{not} exclude the desired ARC budget. Disjoint source chunks are a positive representation control but supply no SAT semantic join. No all-input machine bound, unconditional ARC contraction, or complexity separation follows.

\textbf{NEXT ARC-EDGE-90 requirement, OPEN:} create genuine $A$-dependent semantically sufficient \emph{lossy} ordinary-SAT child descriptions, or a fully accounted many-child compatible join, with two certified levels, both SAT/UNSAT labels, strict power-size contraction, fully charged source and guest resources, and one simultaneous FLMV pair. Roles 2--5 maintain their existing respective construction, semantic join, machine audit and independent synthesis allocations. Five enabled task identities, :00/:12/:24/:36/:48 schedules and peers are untouched. \textbf{$P$ versus $NP$ and ARC-EDGE-90 remain OPEN.}

\paragraph{ARC-IMAGE-REENTRY163 (25 October 2026, 01:55 PDT): locally complete fixed SAT image and two-level charged promise re-entry.}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM CONSEQUENCE plus elementary proof, independent red-team pending:} Rather than use an arbitrary fixed FLMV-hard witness image $\mathcal H^\dagger$, choose a single fixed nondeterministic linear-time clocked padded SAT verifier $L_V$ and its bit-local FLMV Cook reduction. The uniform normalizer
\[
J(F)=R_{L_V}(P(F)),\qquad
\mathcal H_{\rm loc}=\{J(F): F\text{ is an ordinary SAT encoding}\}
\]
satisfies $\SAT(J(F))=\SAT(F)$ for SAT and UNSAT, encoded size $|J(F)|=|F|^{1+o(1)}$, and polylogarithmic per-output-bit time with $O(\log|F|)$ extra writable/index space. FLMV Section 3.1 supplies the virtual-input simulation in the matched model, so the jointly achievable exponent sets satisfy
\[
\boxed{E_{\mathcal H_{\rm loc}}=E}.
\]
This exponent-scale equivalence does \emph{not} hold automatically for the preceding arbitrary hard image $\mathcal H^\dagger$; no near-linear SAT-to-$\mathcal H^\dagger$ normalizer has been proved. A solver promised only on $\mathcal H_{\rm loc}$ can be invoked on every ordinary recursive child $G$ after generating $J(G)$, preserving the child's exponent $\lambda$ and its original virtual-bit access exponent $g$ up to $o(1)$, including two genuine recursive levels, but all guest reads, index operations, buffers, ancestor state, adaptive selection and failed routes remain paid. \textbf{Normalization repairs promise membership only; it does not produce any fixed-power semantic contraction.}

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL, not a FLMV machine trace:} A finite, exactly bit-indexed toy guard normalizer was tested on all $1{,}024$ subsets of ten clauses (317 SAT, 707 UNSAT), with $118{,}784$ indexed-bit comparisons, $9{,}217$ assertions, $2{,}048$ repaired image calls and two real recursively re-normalized levels, zero failures. All first/second ordinary restrictions left the toy promise, proving that automatic inheritance remains false; re-entry restored both labels. The toy has constant-scale, not power-short, descendants.

\textbf{OPEN:} the actual $P=NP$-dependent all-input or locally complete-image solver transformation achieving favorable \emph{simultaneous} time and index/writable-space exponents in the strict FLMV box $t<241/200,\ s<11642479/23232400$. The illustrative ARC-EDGE-90 recurrence remains a conditional ledger, not a machine. Neither the retired universal cosine endpoint nor cross-machine resource borrowing is revived. P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.

\paragraph{ARC-COOK-TAG-RECOVERY162 (25 October 2026, 01:50 PDT): source-readable fixed FLMV-hard image, without a child contraction.}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM CONSEQUENCE / PROVED elementary canonical encoding, independent peer review pending:} Choose the single fixed $L^\dagger\in\operatorname{NTIME}[n]$ violating the published FLMV simultaneous box at $c_\star=241/200$, $d_\star=11642479/23232400$. Choose its uniform near-linear, locally indexed ordinary-SAT Cook reduction, then add a fresh marker and $n$ independent fresh 3CNF tag clauses recording the signs of all source bits; rename Cook variables above the tag range. This preserves both SAT and UNSAT, keeps output size $n\log^{O(1)}n$, and retains $\log^{O(1)}n$ charged per-bit generation with $O(\log n)$ extra counted workspace/index bits. On the exact tagged image promise, each source bit can be recovered by a charged polylogarithmic indexed probe into one tag clause, with $O(\log n)$ extra workspace. If one fixed total uniform solver achieved the strict FLMV forbidden joint pair on every member of this fixed image, the locally indexed virtual-Cook simulation would decide $L^\dagger$ in the FLMV forbidden simultaneous class, contradiction. Image recognition is not needed. Recovering the source is not recovering a SAT witness, a label, a short semantic child, or an image-self-reduction.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT elementary restricted typed control, peer review pending; COMPUTATIONAL:} On an easy independent-component tagged ordinary-CNF family, a genuine two-level recursive AND join preserves SAT and UNSAT; all 16 four-bit parents, 32 first-level children, 64 grandchildren and all 510 parity-source image controls were independently checked with a standard-library exact-DPLL audit. Total $2,890$ assertion-counted checks, zero failures, including a shared-name contradiction $(u)\land(\neg u)$ where independent child SAT answers do NOT suffice. Balanced half-component children have $\Theta(N)$ encoded length, $\lambda=1$, even though their byte count is approximately halved. This is an explicit negative control showing source-tag recovery plus type closure does not constitute the missing fractional-power contraction. No original FLMV hard instance, genuine FLMV TM peak-space trace, hardware bit flips or bit-time is measured.

\textbf{ONE active ARC DAG:} KNOWN THEOREM CONSEQUENCE hard-image forbidden endpoint; PROVED elementary source tags and easy typed recursion (peer review pending); COMPUTATIONAL two-level controls; CONDITIONAL fully justified finite-entry exponent arithmetic; OPEN an actual assumed-$P=NP$, fixed uniform $A$-dependent, two-real-level, both-label, power-short hard-image semantic contractor with paid source probes, indexing, selection, failed paths, ordinary child access, ancestors and simultaneously-live workspace. \textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 and $P$ versus $NP$ remain OPEN.} The retired cosine endpoint, free source-only/extensional SAT answers, uncharged parent-scale guest execution and silent restricted-to-all-input transport remain retired. \paragraph{ARC-SINGLE-HARD-IMAGE161 (25 October 2026, 01:30 PDT): one fixed FLMV-hard image suffices at final landing.}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM CONSEQUENCE, source-matched:} FLMV Corollary~5.8 at $c_\star=241/200$ and $d_\star=11642479/23232400$ gives a fixed $L^\dagger\in\operatorname{NTIME}[n]$ outside $\operatorname{DTISP}[n^{c_\star},o(n^{d_\star})]$. FLMV's Theorem~3.1 and Section~3.1 provide a fixed uniform locally indexed SAT reduction $R^\dagger(x)$ of $n\operatorname{polylog}n$ encoded bits, each accessible in polylogarithmic time and $O(\log n)$ extra writable/index space. Hence a \emph{single} uniform machine with time $N^{t+o(1)}$, space $N^{s+o(1)}$, $t<c_\star$, $s<d_\star$, correct on \emph{every} formula in the fixed image $\mathcal H^\dagger=\operatorname{im}R^\dagger$ would contradict FLMV. No correctness, recognition, or resource bound is required off-image. This is the published endpoint re-expressed on a smaller \emph{promise} domain, not a new lower bound or an ARC contraction. The global all-ordinary-SAT achievable set $E$ retains its original definition; image-only promised exponents $E_{\mathcal H}$ are \emph{not} automatically in $E$.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT elementary typed-composition lemma, independent review pending:} a fixed finite chain $\mathcal H_0,\ldots,\mathcal H_d$ may be assembled from uniform exact contractors if every $\mathcal H_j$ parent on both SAT/UNSAT labels issues children \emph{proved} to belong to $\mathcal H_{j-1}$ and all input bits, policy selection, child building/access, gluing, caches, failures and simultaneously live storage are paid. This permits an \emph{alternative sufficient} ARC route beginning under $P=NP$ with one all-input $(K,K)\in E$ and ending on $\mathcal H^\dagger$, without all-input improvements at intermediate levels. The original all-input finite-hit theorem remains sufficient but is \emph{not necessary as a final coverage condition}. The candidate successor $(t,s)\mapsto(\max\{11/10,3/5+t/2\},\max\{1/2,s/2\})$ is \textbf{OPEN} as an actual machine transformation, including typed-child eligibility.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL falsifier:} the toy even-parity ordinary 2CNF image has a correct image-only decider that deliberately rejects off-image SAT formulas. Two genuine SAT-preserving restrictions of $R_\oplus(0000)$ produce SAT children outside the image, so the image-only decider answers both incorrectly. The independently executed JavaScript audit checked $1,022$ source image formulas, $232,320$ individual indexed bits, $254$ brute SAT controls and both pin levels, all PASS. These finite checks do not establish FLMV-hardness or actual model-machine cycles. This identifies \emph{child promise closure} as a new explicit receiving gap rather than assuming unrestricted descendant inheritance.

\paragraph{ARC-IMAGE-PROMISE-BRIDGE160 (25 October 2026, 01:27 Los Angeles): fixed reduction-image alternative to all-input ARC.}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM / DERIVED PROVED/EXACT (independent review pending):} FLMV Theorem~3.1 supplies near-linear-length, polylogarithmic-indexed-bit-access SAT reductions from each fixed nondeterministic linear-time language, in the same counted writable/index random-access Turing model. Pad arbitrary ordinary SAT inputs by a fixed polylogarithmic factor to one fixed language $L_{\mathrm{pad}}\in\NTIME[n]$ (or equivalently the standard nondeterministic quasi-linear SAT verifier padded to linear time). Let $Q$ be the fixed FLMV reduction after that padding and $\mathcal H_{\circ}=\{Q(F):F\in\{0,1\}^*\}$. Then $|Q(F)|=N^{1+o(1)}$, each addressed bit has $\log^{O(1)}N$ charged work and $O(\log N)$ extra writable/index space, and $\SAT(Q(F))=\SAT(F)$ for \emph{every} bitstring, including invalid encodings as NO. A \emph{single uniform total promise machine} $B$ jointly bounded by $(t,s)$ on \emph{every} image input in $\mathcal H_{\circ}$, with $t\ge1,s\ge0$, can be simulated on $Q(F)$ without full materialization, giving one \emph{all-input} ordinary-SAT machine with the \emph{same simultaneous polynomial exponents} $(t,s)$, after polylog time/additive logarithmic space overhead. Conversely every all-input SAT machine works on this image. Thus the fixed-image promise-achievable set and ordinary achievable set agree at exponent precision; this is a derived consequence of FLMV, not a new separation.
\textbf{TWO sufficient targets, BOTH OPEN:} ARC-EDGE-90A is the original all-input cofinal fully charged favorable route. ARC-EDGE-90B requires favorable cofinal machine routes only for the \emph{fixed reduction-image} promise $\mathcal H_{\circ}$; all necessary source outputs in that family require both SAT/UNSAT correctness, uniform time and same-machine peak indexed/workspace. Because the image is fine-grained complete, entering $t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$ still contradicts FLMV, not merely a special-family theorem. The illustrative successor and its finite-entry arithmetic remain conditional, and the universal cosine endpoint remains retired. \emph{Typed recursive eligibility} is indispensable: SAT-preserving children need not belong to the parent's image promise. Two real tautological-guard-deletion levels on 128 Horn cores (56 SAT, 72 UNSAT; 642 finite assertions) preserve all labels but leave the promised grammar; a promise-only solver returns a wrong SAT label on all 72 UNSAT cores after either level. Actual FLMV tableau reduction and a favorable A-dependent contraction were \emph{not} implemented. Only declared bit-serialization was counted in the toy audit; not a matched FLMV machine trace.
\textbf{OPEN next implication:} build an $A$-dependent uniform typed contractor on $\mathcal H_{\circ}$ with two verified eligible ordinary child levels, original-name compatible joins, fully charged per-probe index/accessor work and \emph{simultaneous} counted memory, then a cofinal finite route. Off-image arbitrary behavior is allowed only where never invoked. Root proof status stays $P$ versus $NP$ \textbf{OPEN}; ARC-EDGE-90A/B \textbf{OPEN}. \paragraph{ARC-M22-PIN-FANOUT158 (25 October 2026, 00:23 PDT): power-short raw pinning requires exponential independent fanout.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent review pending; COMPUTATIONAL 202 deterministic two-level assertions.}
For every proper clausewise-monotone exact-(2,2) three-CNF source with $k$ active names, $m=4k/3$ and dense indexed bit length $N=\Theta(k\log(k+2))$, any \emph{raw literal pin-and-copy} descendant fixing $q$ original names but retaining every source clause disjoint from those names contains at least $m-4q$ \emph{unchanged ternary clauses}. Thus if $q=O(\log N)$, its fully encoded ordinary CNF length remains $\Theta(N)$, even after injective dense renaming, while an $O(N^\lambda)$-bit child for fixed $\lambda<1$ requires $q\ge k/3-o(k)$. A \emph{complete independent} Cartesian enumeration of these pinned settings then has $B\ge 2^{k/3-o(k)}=N^{\omega(1)}$ leaves. Two genuinely serialized consecutive restriction levels were checked. This excludes \emph{only} a raw pin-and-copy polynomial-fanout source of the illustrative $\lambda=1/2$ ARC edge; no unrestricted decider is constrained to this architecture. Direct symbolic solving, learning, propagation, caching, source recognition, A-dependent selection and early exits remain available. \paragraph{ARC-POLICY-SLOPE156 (24 October 2026, 22:58 PDT): paid state-dependent selector channel and parent-sized black-box query barrier.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted query-ledger algebra and mandatory black-box padding lemma, mathematical peer review pending; COMPUTATIONAL 57 exact-rational recurrence checks.}
All five active staggered roles have been reallocated to this \emph{single} ARC/FLMV simultaneous time--space contraction spine; previously valid UWI, M22, wrappers, selectors and proof-system results remain supporting evidence only. The current target is the upward-closed set $\mathcal E$ of \emph{jointly} achieved exponents for one uniform total ordinary-SAT exact decider on all inputs in the FLMV indexed read-only-input random-access model. Under $P=NP$ some $(K,K)\in\mathcal E$ exists. The strict matched FLMV forbidden box is
\[
t<\frac{241}{200},\qquad s<\frac{11642479}{23232400}.
\]
The old universal cosine endpoint remains \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}. The finite-hit lemma from FINAL-LINK-ARC is a \emph{conditional} existential proof template, not an all-input machine-producing edge.

For a \emph{genuine} total all-input uniform wrapper of fixed supplied $A$ with joint exponents $(t,s)$, suppose a paid adaptive rule/selector channel makes at most $N^{b+o(1)}$ sequential solver calls on ordinary virtual queries of length $N^{\rho+o(1)}$, every per-step input probe costing at most $N^{g+o(1)}$ bit operations. With the proposed but still \textbf{OPEN} recursive base channel $(a,\lambda)=(3/5,1/2)$, nonchild time exponent $11/10$, and fully paid parent/ancestor/generator space exponent $1/2$, the \emph{conditional} same-machine envelope is
\[
t'\le\max\{11/10,\,3/5+t/2,\,b+g+\rho t\},\qquad
s'\le\max\{1/2,\,s/2,\,\rho s\}.
\]
For $\rho<1$, the numerical time fixed point is
\[
T_*=\max\{6/5,(b+g)/(1-\rho)\}.
\]
A strict endpoint via \emph{this envelope} requires $b+g<(241/200)(1-\rho)$. For $\rho=1/2$ this is $b+g<241/400$, leaving only $1/400$ exponent of slack beyond $3/5$. Eager materialization of a policy input of length $N^\rho$ places the entire child buffer in counted workspace; $\rho=3/5$ or $3/4$ therefore misses the space target even when the \emph{lazy numerical} fixed point remains $6/5$. Actual bit-probe decoding, live ancestry, failure and replay costs remain charged.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted padding no-go:} assuming $P=NP$, artificially delay a fixed correct polynomial-time SAT decider by $\Theta(M^K)$ steps to obtain a uniform total exact polynomial-time decider $A_K$. Any wrapper that must \emph{execute} the supplied $A_K$ on a $\Theta(N)$-bit SAT query for infinitely many parent lengths has worst-case time $\Omega(N^K)$, and so cannot promise a strict speedup for \emph{every} supplied solver by compulsory full-length lookahead. This does not rule out an existential cofinal route or a wrapper that skips the query.

\paragraph{M22-PORT-CROSSING154 (24 October 2026, 21:55 PDT): necessary endpoint-witness crossings in strict pure-sign port gadgets.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT within a declared static existential port-gadget model, independent mathematical review pending; COMPUTATIONAL LP controls.}
For any pure-sign Boolean OR-CNF gadget $G(p,y)$ with $r\ge2$ port names and
$\operatorname{Proj}_pG=\{0^r,1^r\}$, fix arbitrary extending auxiliary
witnesses $y^0,y^1$. For \emph{each} port $p_i$, the unsatisfied neighboring
mixed port words force distinct \emph{single-port} positive and negative
clauses; both use some auxiliary $u$ with $(y^0_u,y^1_u)=(1,0)$.
Writing $D=\{u:y^0_u=1,y^1_u=0\}$ and assuming each auxiliary has
at most $d_+$ positive and $d_-$ negative gadget occurrences gives
\[
r\le \min\{d_+,d_-\}|D|,\qquad
|D|\ge\left\lceil\frac{r}{\min\{d_+,d_-\}}\right\rceil.
\]
This is a \emph{static difference between two distinct existential
extension witnesses}, \textbf{not} a temporal computational bit flip,
live-memory lower bound or SAT decision-time obstruction.

With $a=9$ the endpoints must satisfy

Independent numerical LP checking of these $81$ signatures after forced

\paragraph{M22-NAE-PSD-CLIQUE150: exact local-marginal PSD obstruction (24 October 2026, 20:04 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the fixed uniform six-row clause-law model,
independent review pending; COMPUTATIONAL full six-name controls.
ARC-EDGE-90 and DIRECT-POST OPEN.}
The earlier M22-NAE-PAIR149 theorem gives pairwise overlap-compatible
clause-local distributions $\mu_S$ uniform over the six nonconstant
three-bit rows, including partially complemented source supports.
The new theorem proves these \emph{same} local laws do not, in general,
admit a \emph{global joint distribution or PSD degree-two moment matrix}.
For spins $Z_i=2X_i-1$ the law enforces
$\mathbb E Z_i=0$ and $\mathbb E Z_iZ_j=-1/3$ for every cooccurrence
edge. On any $r$-clique of the variable cooccurrence graph the moment
matrix is $\frac43I_r-\frac13J_r$, with eigenvalue $(4-r)/3$.
For $r\ge5$ it violates positive semidefiniteness, equivalently
\[
\mathbb E\left[\left(\sum_{i\in Q}Z_i\right)^2\right]
=\frac{r(4-r)}3<0.
\]
This is a strict \emph{restricted model} obstruction, not a general
SoS feasibility result, an UNSAT certificate, or an all-algorithm
decision-time lower bound. In fact the canonical valid $\mathcal C_\star$
SAT fixture $Y$ (twenty models) contains a five-clique

has a six-clique, twenty-two satisfying assignments and witness
$(0,0,1,1,0,0)$, with proposed moment $-4$.

$\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}$ instances SAT: there are eight clauses
with average random violated count one, but the all-zero assignment
violates four positive clauses, ruling out UNSAT.
Independent complete six-name audit enumerated $75^2=5625$
signed regular sources, $5550$ in $\mathcal C_\star$,
all SAT; clique histogram $\omega=3:915$, $\omega=5:3420$,
$\omega=6:1290$, zero failures. Among nonpaired sources,
$4710$ trigger the fixed-law PSD obstruction.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED} is any inference that mutual clause-marginal
consistency automatically supplies this global PSD law or that its
failure requires expensive unrestricted SAT decision.
Neither the fixed-law inconsistency nor its cheap five-clique detection
is a complete-state noncircular post-acquisition potential.

\paragraph{M22-NAE-PAIR149: full-partner-compatible local relaxation and sharp correlation firewall (24 October 2026, 18:58 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted local-marginal theorems, independent review

distributions were exact only for \emph{pair-free} sources.

 \sum_{i<j}\operatorname{Corr}(X_i,X_j)=-1;

a high-level Sherali--Adams or PSD construction, or a forced amount
of computation. A post-acquisition potential initialized solely from

\paragraph{Current LSPV-1 archive and spine reconciliation, M22-CLONE-SHARPNESS148 (24 October 2026, 17:58 PDT).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent review pending; COMPUTATIONAL finite controls only.}
Role~5's M22-CLONE-EQUALITY-FLOOR147 proves that a proper ternary
clone-only CNF defining exactly $\{0^r,1^r\}$ needs at least $2r$
clauses. The new explicit sharp construction, for $r\ge3$, is
\[
 G_r=\bigwedge_{i=0}^{r-1}
  [(\neg z_i\vee z_{i+1}\vee z_{i+2})\wedge
   (\neg z_i\vee z_{i+1}\vee\neg z_{i+2})],
\]
with indices modulo $r$. The paired clauses reduce to
$(\neg z_i\vee z_{i+1})$, so $G_r$ is the directed equality
implication cycle, has exactly $2r$ unique proper clauses,
models precisely $0^r,1^r$, and gives each clone $(3^+,3^-)$
occurrences. The peer $2r$ floor is therefore \emph{sharp in
mixed-sign proper CNF}. Separately, \emph{no} clone-only
clausewise-monotone CNF can admit both constants and enforce
their equality: any positive clause fails all-zero, any
negative clause fails all-one, and the empty conjunction is
not equality for $r\ge2$. Thus a standalone monotone equality
normalizer needs auxiliaries or explicit contextual semantics.
The sharp gadget is \textbf{OUTSIDE} canonical $L_\star$ and does
\emph{not} fill LOCAL-COOK-TAGGED-SIGNED22-1.
Exhaustive verification for $r=3,\ldots,12$: 8,184 total
assignments, 4,290 mixed proper ternary boundary clauses,
1,430 pure-sign control clauses, zero discrepancies; $r=20,50,100$
only structural checks. This is \textbf{not} measured FLMV
machine work, nor two legal SAT-preserving recursive levels.
Prior peer-compatible results: Role~2 PIN-ERASURE147 excludes
ordinary type-hereditary connected-UNSAT pinning; Role~3
MAXDEFECT-FACTOR101 exhibits high-frontier easy target SAT;
Role~4 DP-TWOLEVEL103 projects exact SAT and UNSAT answers
through two ordinary mixed-width CNF stages, but not target
children and not compulsory enumeration; Role~5's proper
clone-only floor is now quantitatively tight. The Role~1
LAZY-ACCESS-BRIDGE147 simulation bounds remain conditional.
Canonical target $\mathcal C_\star$ excludes \emph{only fully}
complement-paired clausewise-monotone exact-$(2,2)$ sources,
allows partial pairs and easy inputs, and has
$N=\Theta(k\log(k+2))$ in dense indexed encoding.
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 remains OPEN}: all-input uniform joint
resource pairs of one FLMV-matched ordinary SAT machine must
reach the strict box $t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$

\paragraph{M22-PARITY-LOCAL142 (24 October 2026): exact two-local pseudomarginal ceiling with matched target SAT/UNSAT controls.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in a restricted source model, independent review pending; COMPUTATIONAL finite original-source checks; ARC-EDGE-90 and DIRECT-POST OPEN.}
For every proper three-variable OR clause $C$ on distinct unsigned source
names, let $z_C$ be its unique falsifying three-bit row.  The uniform
distribution $\mu_C$ on the four rows of XOR parity
$1-\bigoplus_i(z_C)_i$ is supported on assignments satisfying $C$.
Every one- or two-name projection is perfectly uniform.  Therefore,
if distinct clauses have distinct \emph{unsigned} triple supports,
all pairwise satisfying clause marginals agree on every shared name.
This applies to the \emph{pair-free subfamily} of the canonical target
$\mathcal C_\star$, not to every partially paired target input.
Even a globally UNSAT formula admits these pairwise-compatible local
satisfying marginals.  They do not imply one globally consistent model.

The original D\"ocker 198-variable 264-clause pair-free monotone UNSAT
source $U$ and a valid same-size 198-variable monotone SAT mutant $V$
(two negative source identifiers swapped, a 198-bit witness independently
replayed) both have $q=132$ and exactly the same unsigned clause-pair
intersection histogram $(33579,1086,51,0)$ over $34716$ pairs.  Both
have zero local-marginal conflicts.  The globally strengthened system
of opposite-parity XOR equations is \emph{inconsistent for both},
with GF(2) coefficient rank 198.  Thus directly imposing the parity
clauses is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as an exact SAT normalizer}: it
rejects the SAT source $V$.  The UNSAT label for $U$ relies on the
published D\"ocker theorem and Role 2's prior certificate replay,
not a fresh full UNSAT proof in this run.  The six-variable target SAT
anchor $Y$ has 20 models, while the fully paired six-variable SAT
control lies outside $\mathcal C_\star$ and has 18 models.

For every full $\mathcal C_{\rm M22}$ source there are two exact original
source-name-labeled loopless cubic multigraphs $G_+,G_-$ whose edge labels
are the $k$ variables: the TRUE edge set must cover all positive-clause
vertices and its complement must cover all negative-clause vertices.
This is source-invertible, \emph{not} a shrinking ordinary SAT child.
The fractional inequalities have the uniform strictly feasible point
$z_x=1/2$ even on the UNSAT control, ruling out fractional feasibility
or two-local consistency alone as a decision-hardness obstruction.
Higher-order compatibility and integral global reasoning remain open.
The reported finite checks involved $69488$ cross-formula clause-pair
comparisons and explicit SAT witness replay, not physical indexed-TM
bit steps or true live-bit-time measurements.

\paragraph{M22-TOTAL-ANCHOR138 (24 October 2026): exact target-scoped, invalid-input-reflecting bit-local anchor.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT for one declared dense indexed clause-list grammar; independent review pending.}
The canonical $\mathcal C_{\rm M22}$ consists of distinct proper clausewise-monotone
3-CNFs with exactly two positive and two negative occurrences of each active
source variable. Let $\bar C$ flip signs \emph{inside OR clauses} and let
$\mathcal C_\star=\mathcal C_{\rm M22}\setminus
\{F:\forall C\in F,\bar C\in F\}$.
Only fully complementary-clause-paired formulas are excluded; partial pairs,
disconnected and easy formulas remain valid. The target language accepts
satisfiable members and rejects malformed encodings. For valid sources,
$3m=4k$, $m_+=m_-=2k/3$ and $N=\Theta(k\log(k+2))$.
D\"ocker's $\mathcal C_{\rm M22}$ NP-completeness and fully paired SAT
result establish target NP-completeness but no time lower bound.
The previously validated six-variable fixture $Y$, with positive supports
$123,145,246,356$ and negative supports $124,136,235,456$, has
20 models and no complementary partner.
\texttt{M22-TOTAL-ANCHOR138} strengthens Role~4's previously
\emph{valid-source} no-test anchor with a \emph{total} reduction for a
declared dense encoding: a paid header-only framing gate checks
$k,m,w,N$, copies all original signed literal fields, maps any
old out-of-range variable ID to illegal target ID zero, and appends
a fresh, renamed copy of $Y$.
This preserves and reflects every original grammar violation
without a quadratic full-input validity scan:
\[
\operatorname{Valid}_{\rm M22}(x)\iff
\operatorname{Valid}_{\rm M22}(A_{\rm total}(x)),\qquad
\#\operatorname{SAT}(A_{\rm total}(x))=20\#\operatorname{SAT}(x)
\quad\text{on valid }x.
\]
All target bit accesses require $\operatorname{polylog}N$ paid
elementary bit work and $O(\log N)$ extra live writable/index bits.
Thus a single uniform exact $\mathcal C_\star$ decider with joint
$(t,s)$ exponent upper bounds transports to $\mathcal C_{\rm M22}$
with the \emph{same} upper exponents for $t\ge1,s\ge0$,
in this declared machine/encoding, not to ordinary unrestricted SAT.
The output is \emph{longer}, and illegal pinned children are not
repaired by extra $Y$ copies. This supplies no ARC favorable successor.

\paragraph{PHASE-SANDWICH136 (25 October 2026): paid scan-first and deferred-last-bit normal forms.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT for the declared indexed-bit-machine simulation;
independent review pending. POST-POTENTIAL-136 and ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN.}
For \emph{any} fixed total uniform polynomial-time exact decider $A$
with joint time $T_A(N)$ and writable/index space $S_A(N)$, one uniform
scan-first decider $A_{\rm early}$ reads every physical source bit before
simulating $A$. Another uniform decider $A_{\rm late}$ reads every bit
\emph{except} the first, simulates $A$ on both fully charged virtual
completions $x^{(0)}$ and $x^{(1)}$, then reads the first physical bit
last and selects the already-computed correct answer. Both machines
are all-input exact and have simultaneous bounds
\[
T_{\rm early},T_{\rm late}
=O((N+T_A)\operatorname{polylog}N),\qquad
S_{\rm early},S_{\rm late}=O(S_A+\log N).
\]
For $t\ge1$, $s\ge0$, neither changes the achievable exponent
\emph{upper bounds} $(t,s)$ or gives an ARC contraction. In the late
machine only $O(\log N)$ charged bit work follows the first state
$C_{\rm full}$ at which every physical input bit has been acquired.
By the exact telescoping lemma, any terminal-zero complete-state
potential with local drop $\Phi(C)-\Phi(C')\le L(N)u(C,C')$
satisfies $\Phi(C_{\rm full}^{\rm late})\le O(L(N)\log N)$.
Thus a claimed \emph{every-decider} large structural potential
initialized solely at the final input acquisition is
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED} when it exceeds that budget. This
does \emph{not} refute a potential initiated at a \emph{paid clean
scan-first state}: the early machine is the correct normalization
repair, but an independently large initial value and universally
bounded decrease remain \textbf{OPEN: POST-POTENTIAL-136}.
Input-observation hitting $\tau\le N$ is not post-read computation.
A compact fixed proper-3CNF verifier checked 606 sources
(478 SAT, 128 UNSAT, 257 pivotal two-completion pairs), zero
failures; the separate expanded interpreter tested 619
bounded CNFs with affine/Horn/2-SAT and both-label controls.
These are \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL} checks, not physical FLMV traces.
Both constructions charge source/work reads, all writes including
unchanged writes, address/control/selection, virtual input access,
saved answer bits, up to two simulated workspaces, peak live bits
and cumulative bit-time. The all-input favorable ARC-EDGE-90
and P versus NP remain \OPEN.

handoff:

occurring more than twice, and

Pin all $B_v$ names, branch on $k_v$ internal names and solve each

T\le \operatorname{poly}(N)\bigl(1+\sum_v2^{|B_v|+k_v}\bigr),
\qquad

\paragraph{INTERVAL-FACTOR134 (25 October 2026): signed degree-two interval-cardinality sources have a polynomial graph-factor algorithm.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT as a restricted source-to-(g,f)-factor equivalence; independent review pending.}
Extend SIGNED-DEGREE2-MATCH133 from exact-one demands to arbitrary
per-clause intervals $L_j\le\#\{\mathrm{true\ literal\ occurrences}\}\le U_j$.
Under the \emph{source} occurrence-degree-at-most-two promise,
make each clause a graph vertex of degree interval $[L_j,U_j]$;
a singleton variable becomes a clause--private-leaf edge;
two same-polarity occurrences become one clause--clause edge;
two opposite-polarity occurrences use a choice vertex of mandatory
degree one with alternative edges to the two clauses.
Every feasible factor induces one source-consistent Boolean assignment,
and conversely. Classical polynomial $(g,f)$-factor feasibility thus gives
a deterministic polynomial source-language decision procedure.
With independently defined $k(H)=|\{v:\deg_H(v)\ge3\}|$, branching first on
those source variables yields $2^{k(H)}\operatorname{poly}(|H|)$ time and
polynomial space, including all branches and reconstruction costs.
When every source variable occurs exactly twice and all demands are exact,
SAT requires $\sum_j b_j\equiv h\pmod 2$, where $h$ counts opposite-sign
variables. In the connected $K_4$ four-clause source, all-positive exact-one
and exact-two demands are SAT; flipping one literal-sign occurrence makes
both UNSAT with the same unsigned incidence.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} $3395$ sources ($2388$ SAT, $1007$ UNSAT),
$22196$ assertion-accounted independent source/factor checks and zero failures;
these are not physical FLMV-machine bit-work or workspace measurements.
Role~2 OVERLAP-CUT128, Role~3 SCOPE-ALIAS-COMPLETENESS96, Role~4
PARITY-MARGINAL-COUNT92 and Role~5 STRICT-SUBJOIN-STATUS131 remain exact
restricted interface and compatibility controls, not all-input improvements.
This graph-factor shortcut defeats raw occurrence/cycle/sign multiplicity as
a universal post-acquisition hardness proxy; on arbitrary ordinary SAT,
$k(H)$ may be linear and exact fallback preserves worst-case exponents.
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain OPEN.}

\paragraph{SIGNED-DEGREE2-MATCH133 (25 October 2026): signed occurrence-two exact-one SAT reduces to weighted matching.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT within a restricted signed exact-one source grammar, independent review pending.}
For signed exact-one clauses on pairwise distinct named source variables, each
variable with occurrence at most two has one of three representations in a
polynomial weighted-matching instance: a degree-one literal makes a
clause--leaf edge; two same-polarity occurrences make one clause--clause
edge; two opposite-polarity occurrences make a \emph{mandatory choice node}
with alternative edges to both clause nodes.  All clause nodes are mandatory.
Weight each graph edge by the number of mandatory endpoints and find a
maximum-weight matching.  All mandatory nodes are covered if and only if
the original signed exact-one instance is SAT; a matching reconstructs
one globally coherent assignment.  This extends
\texttt{DEGREE-MATCH-LADDER132}'s positive-only theorem and differs from
Role~2's ordinary-OR-clause \texttt{BLOSSOM-FACTOR60}.
With source-defined $k=|\{x:\deg_H(x)\ge3\}|$,
conditioning all $k$ source variables and matching each residual yields
$2^k\operatorname{poly}(|H|)$ time and polynomial writable space.  Moore--Robson's
planar cubic positive one-in-three SAT is NP-complete at source degree~3.
This is a sharp \emph{source-language tractability boundary}, neither an
unrestricted deterministic SAT time lower bound nor an ARC successor.
A connected, equal-unsigned-support four-clause, six-variable $K_4$
control has opposite SAT/UNSAT labels under two signed orientations,
both correctly handled by matching.  \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:}
4096 exhaustive source signings, 1200 randomized degree-two sources and
240 degree-three controls, all cross-checked by complete Boolean enumeration:
5536 sources, 2992 SAT, 2544 UNSAT, 11078 assertion-accounted checks
with zero failures.  Python matching counts are not physical FLMV-machine
bit work, complete-state flips or peak writable space.
Role~3's \texttt{EXACTONE-JOIN-CYCLE95}, Role~4's
\texttt{SCOPE-FRESHNESS-VALIDATION91}, and Role~5's
\texttt{THREE-STATE-JOIN-FIREWALL130} remain complementary
source-semantics checks, not all-machine resource transfers.
A grammar recognizer on ordinary inputs must retain an exact
paid fallback, which can preserve the original exponent.
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain OPEN.}

\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-25_0026_LSPV-1_SIGNED_DEGREE2_MATCH133.tex}
and files with matching timestamp and run identifier in that directory.



\paragraph{NONAFFINE-WINDOW131 (24 October 2026): nonlinear overlapping interfaces admit charged four-state joining.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT for a restricted canonical proper-3CNF grammar; independent review pending.}
For each ordered triple-window constraint on original source variables
$(x_i,x_{i+1},x_{i+2})$, extract its actual signed forbidden-three-bit
mask by charged source reads.  With pins $x_0=x_1=1$ and
$x_{r+1}=b$, the four-state recurrence
\[
 D_0=\{(1,1)\},\qquad
 D_{i+1}=\{(v,w):\exists u,\ (u,v)\in D_i,\ (u,v,w)\notin Q_i\}
\]
is an exact, source-name-consistent prefix projection.  Validation plus
decision on this restricted ordered grammar uses
$O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)$ bit work and $O(\log N)$ writable bits.
Its explicit nonlinear AND recurrence $x_{i+2}=x_i\wedge x_{i+1}$
gives matched connected proper-3CNF SAT/UNSAT instances with
$n=r+8$, $m=4r+12$, primal cycle rank $r+3$,
and every UNSAT subformula meeting all $r$ gate blocks.
The fixed solver recognizes both labels and bypasses off-tree
$2^{\Theta(r)}$ enumeration, without a label oracle.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 584 finite inputs, 526 SAT and 58 UNSAT,
1397 assertion-accounted groups with zero failures.
Large cycle rank, local nonaffinity, and growing UNSAT core span
are \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as standalone universal SAT runtime
obstructions}.  This does not supersede the CYCLE-CUTSET-AFFINE130
restricted upper bound or establish an all-input contractor.
Recognition failure needs exact fallback, so \textbf{ARC-EDGE-90
and P versus NP remain OPEN}.  Full report, reproducible executable,
raw aggregate and source/gap handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-24_2300_LSPV-1_NONAFFINE_WINDOW131.tex}
and adjoining \texttt{NONAFFINE\_WINDOW131} files.


\paragraph{ROOT-HARD-PROJECTION129 (24 October 2026): a source-hard root with exponentially many correlated pinned answers.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted construction; independent review pending.}
For every ordinary proper ternary source CNF $F$, integers $d\ge1$ and
$1\le h\le2^d$, a uniform connected proper-three-CNF $H_{F,d,h}$
retains all original source literals, enforces a functional comparator
$C_{d,h}(b)=[\operatorname{unsigned}(b)<h]$ through exact Tseitin
constraints, and uses fresh-output three-clause-width XOR connectors
to join every source and boundary variable without restricting them.
Exact existential elimination yields
\[
 \boxed{\operatorname{SAT}(H_{F,d,h}[b])
   = C_{d,h}(b)\wedge\operatorname{SAT}(F),\qquad
   \operatorname{SAT}(H_{F,d,h})=\operatorname{SAT}(F).}
\]
Unlike earlier OR-guard counterexamples, the \emph{unpinned parent
decision} is NP-complete under the polynomial ordinary-input
reduction $F\mapsto H_{F,1,1}$, and there are matched SAT/UNSAT
sources of identical clause shape and encoding length.  Nevertheless,
for fixed $d,h$, all $2^d$ pinned-query outcomes are determined by
\emph{one} paid source SAT bit and a public $O(d)$-gate comparator;
the complete answer-vector quotient has only two classes.
Thus connectedness, root NP-completeness and arbitrarily many
individually source-complete projected memberships do not justify
additive repeated SAT-work charges for a one-bit root decider.
This construction does \emph{not} make general SAT easy,
nor does it provide an all-input simultaneous time-space successor.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} the expanded exact DPLL audit checked
285 sources (274 SAT, 11 UNSAT), 903 compiled roots and
3996 pinned controls, 6705 assertion-accounted checks, zero failures;
the compact independent GitHub reproducer additionally passed
1536 checks on all 256 ternary source-clause subsets.
The typed bit-resource vector records charges, but actual
FLMV-machine bit flips and cumulative bit-time were not measured.
The root-hard variant supersedes any claim that the prior
easy-root guard was the \emph{only} obstruction to an interface-density
lower bound.  Both independent source-answer acquisition and the
all-input ARC-EDGE-90 route remain \textbf{OPEN}.
Report, script, raw summary, and additive five-role handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-24_2105_LSPV-1_ROOT-HARD-PROJECTION129.tex}
and neighboring \texttt{2026-10-24\_2105\_ROOT-HARD-PROJECTION129} files.
\textbf{P versus NP remains OPEN.}


\paragraph{DENSE-BOUNDARY-BYPASS128 (24 October 2026): densely SAT-dependent projected interfaces do not compel root decision work.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted; independent review pending.}
For every ordered proper ternary CNF $F$ and integer $d\ge1$, an explicit
connected proper-3CNF guard-and-OR construction $D_{F,d}$ of ordinary size
$O((m+d)\log(n+m+d))$ preserves source variable identities and has
\[
Q_{F,d}(b)=[b=0^d]\lor\operatorname{SAT}(F)
\quad(b\in\{0,1\}^d).
\]
Thus for UNSAT $F$, $2^d-1$ of $2^d$ projected membership answers require
the same source SAT label; nevertheless every unpinned parent is SAT,
verified immediately by the all-zero witness. The entire projected relation
has an $O(d)$-gate representation given just one shared source decision
bit, so density of hard boundary queries does not force independent
repeated source work. A uniform exact, state-adaptive decider can first
verify the all-zero witness in $O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)$ bit operations
and $O(\log N)$ writable bits on this family, then invoke fully charged
exact fallback elsewhere. This is \emph{not} an unrestricted polynomial
SAT algorithm or a lower bound, and does not provide an achievable-pair
contraction. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: $317$ independent SAT/UNSAT source
controls, $1{,}268$ constructed parents, $9{,}510$ pinned checks
($8{,}990$ YES, $520$ NO) and $14{,}899$ assertion-accounted checks
with no failures. The source gadget is credited to LSPV-3 GUARDED90,
complementing LSPV-5 GUARD126's single-hard-corner construction.
Root ARC-EDGE-90 remains \textbf{OPEN}; $P$ versus $NP$ remains \textbf{OPEN}.
Full report, compact reproducibility script, numeric results and additive
peer handoff are under
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-24_2024_LSPV-1_DENSE_BOUNDARY_BYPASS128.tex}
and adjacent \texttt{2026-10-24\_2024\_DENSE\_BOUNDARY128} files.

\paragraph{SPARSE-FRONTIER-SCHEDULE127 (24 October 2026): paid trajectory-weighted virtual-frontier accounting.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only under a declared total all-input, source-coherent scoped-stream generation contract; independent peer audit pending.}
For a virtual $\mathsf{OPEN}/\mathsf{CLAUSE}/\mathsf{CLOSE}$ stream of encoded size
$N^{\mu+o(1)}$, per-bit decoding time $N^{a+o(1)}$, source
validation/construction time $N^{r+o(1)}$, peak generator and decoder
live space exponents $\zeta,\sigma$, and maximum active width
$\kappa\log_2 N+o(\log N)$, define the independently structural
trajectory mass
\[
 \Psi(F)=\sum_{i=1}^{L(F)}2^{\max(w_{i-1},w_i)}
   \le N^{\chi+o(1)}.
\]
A uniform exact online frontier DP decodes each event once, then performs
its own row updates.  One jointly resource-bounded FLMV-model exact SAT
decider would therefore have
\[
 \boxed{t'\le\max\{r,\mu+a,\chi\},\qquad
 s'\le\max\{\zeta,\sigma,\kappa\}.}
\]
All source/index work, switching, failed trials, record buffering, ancestor
state, and peak live bits are charged to the one implementation.  This
sharpens the previous coarser maximum-width simulation
$t'\le\max\{r,\mu+\max(a,\kappa)\}$ because high-width steps need not
dominate a long low-width trajectory; it does \emph{not} construct any
all-input virtual normalizer.  In fact a putative generator with
$(\mu,a,\kappa,\chi,r,\sigma,\zeta)
=(1,1/20,43/100,1,1,43/100,43/100)$
would yield $(t',s')=(21/20,43/100)$,
forbidden by FLMV at $c=53/50$ since
$\beta(c)=233623/280900>43/100$.  The old upper envelope
$(143/100,43/100)$ fails that same two-level exclusion.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: a standard-library adaptive SCC/Horn/affine/frontier
audit tested $225$ sources ($174$ SAT, $51$ UNSAT), $20{,}171$ assertions,
zero failures; these are finite semantic/profile checks and not
physical FLMV bit-time or workspace measurements.  The original
LSPV-1 LOCALPARITY1 and LSPV-4 BLOCK-CLOSED-ORACLE85 show a
paid source-aware parity bypass, so cyclic parity gluing is
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as a pointwise universal cost obstruction}.
LSPV-2's SCC-one-parity algorithm is a positive restricted shortcut;
LSPV-3's anonymous profile collision and LSPV-5's named-profile
invertibility leave the arbitrary-input compatibility arrow \textbf{OPEN}.
The code, numeric ledger, additive source/coverage handoff and theorem:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-24_2000_LSPV-1_SPARSE_FRONTIER_SCHEDULE127.tex}
and adjacent same-stem files.
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 remains OPEN; P versus NP remains OPEN.}



\paragraph{ADAPTIVE-LOCAL-DEPTH126 (24 October 2026): unlimited adaptive clause-local querying remains insufficient.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted; independent review pending.}
For every \(k\ge0\), a connected, duplicate-free, proper ordered 3CNF
opposite-label pair \(U_k,V_k\) has the same indexed
\emph{source-anonymized} eight-row local clause profiles and
\(n_k=51+2k,\ m_k=36+k\).
Consequently any deterministic or randomized, arbitrarily adaptive
finite-depth \emph{clause-local} pin/query protocol with no original
cross-clause variable-ID channel has coupled identical transcripts and
cannot decide both SAT/UNSAT parents with error strictly below \(1/2\).
This extends Role~3 PIN89 and Role~5 NAMED125 beyond two levels;
adaptive switching, local caching and local oracle model-count access do
not repair the information loss. \emph{The result is not a lower bound on
ordinary source-readable SAT deciders}: reading original IDs distinguishes
the pair, while retaining IDs makes the full named profiles an invertible
re-encoding of the original Boolean CSP join. The guarded connected family
adds \(k\) satisfiable fresh-variable clauses; finite DPLL/pin audits checked
\(k=0,1,4,32\), matched \(3744\) first-level pin-child clause profiles and
\(512\) adaptive local queries per control without failures.
\textbf{OPEN:} an all-input, fully charged, source-coherent,
same-machine favorable ARC successor or finite cofinal route into the
verified FLMV endpoint. No universal SAT lower bound, \(P=NP\) proof,
or \(P\ne NP\) proof follows. Report:

\paragraph{ARC-ENDPOINT-GUARD125 (24 October 2026): strict FLMV two-level endpoint audit.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT algebraic reformulation, independent review pending.}
For jointly achievable uniform all-input exact SAT time/space pairs in the
published FLMV indexed-read-only-input random-access Turing-machine model,
the two-level forbidden criterion
$\exists c>\max\{1,t\}:\ s<\beta(c)$, where
$\beta(c)=\frac12((c+2)/c^2-c)$, is equivalent for $t\ge1$ to
the strict inequality $s<\beta(t)$ because $\beta$ is continuous and
strictly decreasing.  This restates a \emph{known published} endpoint, not a
new SAT lower bound.  The previously proposed universal landing
$t+s<2\cos(\pi/7)$ is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED} as a substitute:
$(t,s)=(3/2,1/10)$ passes that cosine test but fails FLMV2
($\beta(3/2)=1/36$), whereas $(21/20,4/5)$ satisfies FLMV2
($\beta(21/20)=15139/17640$) but fails the cosine test.  Failing the
two-level sufficient certificate does not establish achievability or
escape from higher-level FLMV bounds.  \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:}
1,271 exactly classified rational grid points, including 369 passing
both diagnostics, 189 legacy-only, 20 FLMV2-only, and 693 neither,
plus five exact rational controls; zero failures.  This is an offline
endpoint audit, not a SAT-algorithm runtime measurement.
\textbf{OPEN:} ARC-EDGE-90, an all-input fully charged uniform
SAME-REALIZATION favorable successor or cofinal finite route;
source-coherent parent gluing and the original HEREDITARY-SCOPED10
executable remain unresolved.  Source report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-24_1800_LSPV-1_ARC_ENDPOINT_GUARD125.tex}.
Reproducer and peer handoff are in the same role directory.

\paragraph{VIRTUAL-FRONTIER-FENCE124 (24 October 2026): lazy virtual-normalization FLMV firewall.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT under a declared all-input source-queryable scoped-stream
contract; independent review pending.} Extending the earlier
ARC-STREAM-FENCE117 from sequentially emitted events to lazily addressable
event bits, suppose one fixed uniform total source-dependent virtualizer
provides an SAT-equivalent scoped event stream of length
$M\le N^{\mu+o(1)}$ and live width
$w\le\kappa\log_2N+o(\log N)$ on \emph{every} ordinary SAT source.
Each event-bit request, including original-source reads, control/index,
all scratch initialization and cleanup, costs
$N^{a+o(1)}$ bit work and $N^{\sigma+o(1)}$ writable space.
Length/preflight costs have exponents $(r,\zeta)$.
An exact DP buffers each event \emph{once} and reuses it across every
live assignment row, so a single uniform all-input SAT machine has
\[
 (t',s')\le
 \left(\max\{r,\mu+\max(a,\kappa)\},
 \max\{\zeta,\sigma,\kappa\}\right)
\]
simultaneously. The per-row multiplicative decoder exponent $a+\kappa$
is \emph{not compulsory}; primitive work is the sum of decoded bits and
table updates with all repeated queries and replay fully charged.
By the verified matched FLMV endpoint, no such \emph{all-input} virtualizer
exists when there is a fixed $c>\max\{1,t'\}$ and
$s'<\beta(c)=((c+2)/c^2-c)/2$.
The hypothetical profile
$(\mu,a,\kappa,r,\sigma,\zeta)=(1,1/10,1/10,11/10,2/5,2/5)$
would yield the forbidden $(11/10,2/5)$ at $c=241/200$.
This restricts only the virtual-normalizer service; arbitrary exact
deciders may bypass it, and the all-input ARC successor remains \OPEN{}.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 430 deterministic SAT/UNSAT controls
(324 SAT, 106 UNSAT) passed 1,689 checks, zero failures.
The paid-grammar hybrid selected Horn, SCC, or virtual frontier DP on
observed source structure, and independently matched materialized DP
and exhaustive truth tables. Python source/event counters are not
physical FLMV bit-tape measurements. The source-handling claims are
theoretical under the explicitly charged subroutine contract, not a

\paragraph{BIT-CHANNEL-TYPE122 (24 October 2026): corrected typed bit-resource ledger.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only in the declared bit-event model; independent review pending.}
The peer \texttt{BIT-LEDGER-CEILING120} displays
$\Delta_{\mathrm{total}}\le W_{\mathrm{work}}\le U$ while
$\Delta_{\mathrm{total}}$ includes mutable source-head, index/address and control
coordinates and $W_{\mathrm{work}}$ counts work-tape writes only.
This first inequality is false as typed.  For coordinate-disjoint complete states
the correct identity and inequality are
\[
 \Delta_{\mathrm{total}}=\Delta_{\mathrm{work}}+
 \Delta_{\mathrm{head}}+\Delta_{\mathrm{addr}}+
 \Delta_{\mathrm{ctrl}}+\Delta_{\mathrm{other}},
 \qquad
 \Delta_{\mathrm{work}}\le W_{\mathrm{work}}\le U.
\]
If each billed primitive event flips at most one complete-state bit, then
$\Delta_{\mathrm{total}}\le U$; otherwise sum explicit per-event bit-change caps,
especially for FLMV index-tape erase operations.  A proper-3CNF parity promise
with $n=3q+5$ variables and $m=4q+8$ clauses is SAT iff its $q$ orientation
bits have even parity.  A finite-control read-only promise scanner uses zero
work-tape writes but has $\Delta_{\mathrm{total}}\ge q$ from source-head movement
on both SAT and UNSAT members.  An independent DPLL audit covered all
$510$ orientation instances through $q=8$, with $3{,}594$ assertions and no
failures.  This repairs only the untyped inequality: Role-5's separate global
Hamming-Lipschitz range ceiling and log-word RAM write-shadow theorem remain
scoped valid, and Role-1's FLIPGAUGE121 autonomous nonstuttering theorem is
unaffected.  It is not an all-input SAT solver, a new FLMV endpoint, or an ARC
successor.  Standalone report, executable and JSON:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-24_1630_LSPV-1_BIT_CHANNEL_TYPE122.tex},
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-24_1630_BIT_CHANNEL_TYPE122_minirepro.py},
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-24_1630_BIT_CHANNEL_TYPE122_results.json};
the additive peer coverage handoff is in the same role directory.
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN; P versus NP remains OPEN.}


\paragraph{ARC-CHANNEL-CURVE119 (24 October 2026): repaired multichannel endpoint and an explicit FLMV feasibility firewall.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT from KNOWN THEOREM in a declared solver-independent self-reducer model; not an ARC successor.}
Recovering the original 4 October \texttt{MULTICHANNEL1}, each complete
uniform all-input exact adaptive SAT self-reduction with finitely many
explicit smaller-instance channels has simultaneous recursion exponents
\[
 C=\max\{r,\max_j\eta_j/(1-\theta_j)\},\qquad
 \rho=\max\{\sigma,\max_j\theta_j\},
\]
provided every channel has $\theta_j<1$, local work, rule discovery,
failed attempts, switching, ordinary child construction, buffers,
indexes, parent/ancestor state and peak space are charged, and no
uncharged nonrecursive use of a supplied SAT solver remains.
The source recurrence proof is valid; its historical
$C+\rho<2\cos(\pi/7)$ \emph{unrestricted polynomial-space landing}
is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}. FLMV Theorem~3.1 and Corollary~5.8
instead forbid any such complete all-input self-reducer if
some $c>\max\{1,C\}$ has
\[
 \rho<\beta_2(c)=\tfrac12((c+2)/c^2-c).
\]
For the canonical fractional channel
$(r,\eta,\theta,\sigma)=(11/10,3/5,1/2,1/2)$,
$(C,\rho)=(6/5,1/2)$ is impossible, with a strict witness
$c=241/200$. The full indexed FLMV Theorem~5.7 additionally
excludes $(C,\rho)=(3/2,1/20)$ using
$c=151/100$, index~4 and
$\beta_4(c)=13827784249/207954240400>1/20$,
although the two-level curve does not exclude it.
Role~3 FORBIDDEN-COMPUTATION84 had already supplied the general
indexed curve and observed the multichannel application;
the current contribution is quantitative old-source reconciliation,
explicit parameter profiles and hostile controls.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 237 assertions with zero failures:
six rational channel profiles, finite recursion event accounting, ten
opposite-label named SAT controls and 150 randomized controls.
These are abstract checks, not physical FLMV time/space measurements.
A full correct all-input \emph{solver-independent} favorable fractional
reduction is precisely what this firewall excludes at the stated cost.
An ARC construction \emph{depending on a hypothetical polynomial-time
base decider} is not covered without charging each dependence.
The standalone, code, raw summary and shared handoff were published

\paragraph{ARC-STREAM-FENCE117 (24 October 2026): all-input streaming-normalizer FLMV firewall and paid pure-literal escape.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT within a declared typed output contract, NOT an ARC successor; independent review pending.}
Suppose one uniform, total, same-machine producer transforms every ordinary
encoded SAT input of $N$ bits into a valid equisatisfiable one-way
active-slot source-scoped 3CNF stream, with at most $N^{\mu+o(1)}$ emitted
bits, $\kappa\log_2 N+o(\log N)$ simultaneously live source variables,
producer time $N^{r+o(1)}$ and counted work/index space $N^{\sigma+o(1)}$.
A source-identity-preserving exact existential frontier DP consumes its
output \emph{online}, without storing the free write-only output as
a readable buffer.  The resulting \emph{one fixed} uniform, all-input
SAT decider has simultaneous exponent pair
\[
 (t',s')=
 \bigl(\max\{r,\mu+\kappa\},\max\{\sigma,\kappa\}\bigr).
\]
Therefore, by FLMV Theorem~3.1 and Corollary~5.8, no such producer
can exist if some constant $c>\max\{1,r,\mu+\kappa\}$ satisfies
\[
 \max\{\sigma,\kappa\}<
 \beta(c)=\tfrac12\bigl((c+2)/c^2-c\bigr).
\]
For example $(\mu,\kappa,r,\sigma)=(1,0,11/10,1/2)$ is impossible in
this contract; the curved forbidden region also excludes
$(11/10,3/20,11/10,3/20)$ at $c=13/10$.
This is an unconditional \emph{typed normalizer impossibility}, not
a theorem that all SAT deciders build such streams, nor a universal
algorithm time lower bound.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT family-relative counterexample.}
Role~2's proper-3CNF complete-edge family has raw unchanged-clause
frontier $\Omega(h)$ for every clause order, yet each edge clause
$(x_i\vee x_j\vee z_{ij})$ has a private pure-positive
$z_{ij}$.  Paid pure-literal elimination, a generic state-adaptive rule,
removes these clauses and the pure bridge/filler variable, leaving
exactly seven SAT or eight UNSAT core clauses, with residual width
at most three.  This preserves Role~2's ordering theorem but
\emph{falsifies} mandatory decision cost inferred from its raw width.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL.} The expanded deterministic Python audit
used seed 202610240600, checked 254 source controls (186 SAT, 68 UNSAT),
18,519 assertions with zero failures, and a portfolio of paid
pure-literal elimination, simple witness recognition, complementary-pair
2SAT-SCC, and exact frontier DP.  Finite checks do not prove the
all-input normalization or exclusion theorem.
A standalone compact reproducer and machine-readable summary are
available with the report and cumulative handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-24_1400_LSPV-1_ARC_STREAM_FENCE117.tex},
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-24_1400_ARC_STREAM_FENCE117_COVERAGE_HANDOFF.md}.
ARC-EP-90 remains FILLED by FLMV; ARC-FH-90 remains a separation-equivalent
meta-implication; \textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 remains OPEN}.

\paragraph{ARCARCHIVECURVE90 (24 October 2026): corrected FLMV curved endpoint, cofinal finite-hit archive recovery, and universal-witness firewall.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT as a meta-level implication, not as a SAT closure theorem.}
Let $\mathcal E$ contain the pairs $(t,s)$ simultaneously achieved by
one uniform total all-input exact SAT decider in the
matched FLMV random-access Turing-machine model.
FLMV Theorem~3.1 and Corollary~5.8 forbid the entire downward-closed region
\[
\mathcal B_{\rm FLMV}
=\left\{(t,s):\exists c>\max\{1,t\},\
s<\tfrac12\bigl((c+2)/c^2-c\bigr)\right\}.
\]
At $c=241/200$ the endpoint is
$\beta(c)=11642479/23232400>1/2$, yielding the familiar strict
box $t<241/200$, $s\le1/2$. The \emph{curved} region is strictly
larger: $(t,s)=(121/100,9/20)$ is forbidden using $c=61/50$,
and $(13/10,3/10)$ using $c=131/100$.
The old unrestricted product interpretation of $2\cos(\pi/7)$
is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}; only its abstract finite-hit argument
is retained. Recovering the 8 October FINAL-LINK-ARC and 27 September
ARC ancestor establishes that an unbounded cofinal integer set of
diagonal $(K,K)$ starting points, each with a \emph{finite} route of
same-realization achievable pairs into $\mathcal B_{\rm FLMV}$,
would contradict $P=NP$. Exponent detection, a universal compiler,
and invariant code identity are \emph{not logically compulsory} for this
existential implication. Every actual all-input route edge with
fully charged simultaneous resources remains \OPEN{}.

The recovered corrected 14 October UWI-1 PDF gives the exact
polynomial-witness-budget equivalence to $P=NP$ and constructive
affine/nonlinear easy-family counterexamples to mandatory
DNNF/search-trace hardness. In particular $H(F)\le2^{O(N)}$
on SAT inputs; a purported $2^{\Omega(N\log N)}$
UWI lower bound is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}.
ARC-FRONTIER21/HEREDITARY-SCOPED10 yield only
\textbf{PARTIAL/RESTRICTED} certified-width contractors; they do
not cover arbitrary ordinary SAT. The current roles 2--5 corroborate
source-supported proof extraction, paid changing boundary parameters,
high-order syndrome easy escapes, and the meta-level closure quantifiers,
respectively. None closes \textbf{ARC-EDGE-90}.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} exact-rational regression checked
10,000 recurrence-envelope cases and seven finite diagonal routes;
an independently evaluated 107-input adaptive SCC/pair-SCC/fallback
control produced 46 SAT and 61 UNSAT correct answers.
Neither finite test is an unrestricted SAT time bound.
The independently stated strongest remaining implication is
one genuine uniform all-input joint-time/space ARC edge,
or cofinal family of finite such routes, permitting every
adaptive algorithm and all paid source/representation switches.

\paragraph{HALFRANDOM89 (24 October 2026): stronger source-computable backdoor upper bound and connected all-decider firewall (PROVED/EXACT in declared models; independent review pending).}
On proper duplicate-free signed exact-$(2,2)$ 3-CNF, perform the prior
unique complementary-pair contractions to $F\equiv B\wedge T$.
Let $\delta=m-2p$ be the number of unmatched ternaries and $r_2$
the number of residual source variables occurring in precisely two
ternaries (all others occur in four). A paid deterministic
conditional-expectation rule selecting variables with potential
$\#\mathrm{selected}+\mathbb E[\#\mathrm{unhit\ clauses}]$ constructs
a strong 2-CNF backdoor $X$ in $O(n+m)$ word time after source validation:
\[
\boxed{|X|\le\left\lfloor\delta/2+r_2/4\right\rfloor.}
\]
Indeed $2r_2+4r_4=3\delta$ and the expectation under independent
half-selection is $(r_2+r_4)/2+\delta/8=\delta/2+r_2/4$.
A constant-incidence local potential rule derandomizes the bound.
The solver may compare this cover with the earlier
TUTTECOVER79 matching cover and use the smaller before exact
binary-SCC continuation; all recognition, discovery, optional
matching, switching, child attempts and source replay are charged.
The certified upper bound is
\[
O\!\left(m\log(m+2)+\delta^4+
2^{h_*}(n+m+1)\right),\quad
h_*=\min\{\lfloor3\delta/5\rfloor,\lfloor\delta/2+r_2/4\rfloor\},
\]
with $O(n+m)$ reusable peak work words. In the nonpaired case
$p=0$, this improves the prior exponent $3m/5$ to $m/2$.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 9,410 checks with zero failures on a
standard-library audit, including connected both-direction source
controls. On the actual $198$-variable, $264$-clause
D\"ocker signed exact-$(2,2)$ source, an independent source scan
found no pairs and produced a 96-variable covering set
(84 local selections plus 12 uncovered-clause repairs), compared
with new generic cap 132 and prior cap 158.
The original UNSAT certificate is an imported
DOECKER-CORE-CERT61 dependency; the two-sign SAT repair and its
published witness were independently clause-replayed.
\par\noindent
\textbf{All-decider firewall, PROVED/EXACT restricted.}
The connected octahedral-chain family $G_t$ has $n=6t,m=8t$,
no complementary pair and exact \emph{minimum} ternary-hitting
set size $2t$, yet a canonical exact recognizer gives a verified
SAT witness in $O(t)$ word time; a general bounded-width
DP shortcut also exists. Thus large minimum backdoor size is
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED} as a pointwise universal decision-time
lower-bound parameter. It does not rule out distinct algorithm-dependent
hard inputs elsewhere. The only new unrestricted proof target is a
noncircular superpolynomial \emph{post-acquisition}
cost bridge covering all complete state-adaptive policies,
not compulsory $2^{|X|}$ branching. Neither $P=NP$ nor $P\ne NP$
has been proved; the universal bridge remains \OPEN{}.

\paragraph{PLANEFRACTION88: fractional adaptive source-word obstruction and source-readable projective-plane control (PROVED/EXACT in query model; independent review pending).}
For fixed legal source words, define the analysis-only fractional distinguishing
cover $\rho_c^*(F)$ as the minimum total positive read-cost weights hitting
the differing-word set of every valid opposite-SAT-label source.
Any bounded-error randomized \emph{complete state-adaptive} exact-input
decider with per-input error at most $\epsilon<1/2$ obeys
\[
\mathbb E\,C_{\rm distinct\ source\ reads}(F)
\ge(1-2\epsilon)\rho_c^*(F).
\]
This is a direct same-random-tape coupling and a feasible fractional-cover
construction, independent of the solver's representation changes. A
deterministic exact decider obeys the integer cover. Conditional
transcript potentials telescope and vanish after full source acquisition;
they cannot be added at each step to manufacture superlinear work.

A canonical proper duplicate-free 3CNF source from $\operatorname{PG}(2,p)$
has $q=p^2+p+1$ projective points and $q$ lines, $n=q(p+1)$,
$m=q(p+3)$ and encoded bit length
$N=(4m+2)\lceil\log_2(64(n+m+1))\rceil$.
The source sign vector $b$ is SAT precisely when no projective line has
all its point orientations equal to one. On the all-zero SAT source,
all opposite-label orientation changes intersect: the
\emph{promise-family} disjoint sensitivity is one, while its exact
fractional distinguishing cover is $q/(p+1)$ and integer cover $p+1$.
For every randomized decider of the \emph{full} proper-3CNF language,
the projective-line opposite-label neighbors therefore force
$(1-2\epsilon)q/(p+1)$ expected source-word reads. However full-source
geometry recognition and direct witness replay decide the whole
canonical family in polynomial time; no rich backward-selector relation
is compulsory. This construction is \emph{not} signed exact-$(2,2)$
and does not transport that budget automatically.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} seeded independent full audit with 5,152
assertion-accounted checks and zero failures for $p=2,3,5,7$,
including all 128 Fano orientations, independent DPLL and
SAT/UNSAT controls. Report, code and raw-summary records verified at
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1900_LSPV-1_PLANEFRACTION88.tex}
and same-stem files. Existing RANDOMSENS87, MAIN108 and fractional
adversary literature are adopted, not rebranded as global novelty.
\textbf{OPEN:} a representation-neutral post-acquisition
parameterized lower bound for every adaptive total uniform exact
decider, sufficiently growing on algorithm-dependent hard inputs.
P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.



\paragraph{Latest RANDOMSENS87: sharp bounded-error adaptive source-word lower bound (PROVED/EXACT, declared model; COMPUTATIONAL controls).}
For any legal input $F$ of a fixed source-word encoding, define
$\beta_w(F)$ as the maximum number of pairwise disjoint sets of changed
source-word positions reaching legal \emph{opposite-label} inputs.
This is a source/label parameter, not defined by a solver's runtime;
computing it is not supplied free.  Coupling the same random coins on
$F$ and each opposite-label neighbor proves that \emph{every} adaptive
randomized total exact-input decider with per-input error at most
$\epsilon<1/2$ pays
\[
 \mathbb E\,Q_A(F)\ge(1-2\epsilon)\beta_w(F).
\]
The proof allows arbitrary representation changes, Horn/SCC/affine
reasoning, DPLL, learning, restarts, caches and rereads.  It
lower-bounds source-word queries, \emph{not} post-acquisition SAT work.
Using the certified 198-variable 264-clause signed exact-$(2,2)$
UNSAT source $U$ and the two-sign same-support SAT repair $V$ from
TWO-SIGN-SWAP64/DOECKER-CORE-CERT61, an all-$V$ $p$-block product has
$p$ disjoint opposite-label two-word perturbations.  Thus
\[
\mathbb E Q_A(B_{p,1^p})\ge(1-2\epsilon)p,\qquad
N_p=(1056p+2)\lceil\log_2(64(462p+1))\rceil .
\]
The $(1-2\epsilon)$ factor is sharp for the abstract orientation-word
AND promise.  Complete source validation and fixed-template recognition
provide an $O(p)$-word SAT decider on the recognized family; unsupported
inputs may use exact fallback.  Since always
$\beta_w(F)\le2+4m=O(N/\log N)$, this family and measure cannot yield
superlinear, let alone superpolynomial, unrestricted SAT time.
The earlier $U$ UNSAT certificate is an explicit imported dependency,
not independently replayed during this run.  A standard-library
audit made 30,609 checks without failure, including 254 chain inputs,
10 hybrid SAT/UNSAT controls and 16 randomized promise settings.
Full report and verified adjacent code/raw/handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1805_LSPV-1_RANDOMSENS87.tex}.
The unrestricted parameterized acquisition/compression/direct-decision
cost bridge remains \OPEN{}; P versus NP remains OPEN.



\paragraph{Prior TWOHEAD86: source-identical two-local-head escape (PROVED/EXACT restricted; COMPUTATIONAL controls).}
On the proper unique 3CNF equality-source family $E_k(u,v)$,
$\mathsf{SAT}(E_k(u,v))$ holds exactly when $u=v$.
Its immutable grouped source uses $n=5k$, $m=8k$, and $32k+2$
fixed-width words; it is \emph{not} strict signed exact-$(2,2)$.
A \emph{total uniform exact} solver equipped with two independent locally
moving read-only input heads, both initially at source word zero,
can pay for a complete syntax scan, align the heads with the $u$ and $v$
quartets, and compare corresponding signed inputs in $O(k)$ charged
head moves plus reads with $O(\log(k+2))$ complete portable work bits.
Observed disagreement terminates UNSAT; comparison of all $k$
pairs terminates SAT. Unsupported inputs use a total exact fallback.
For the \emph{same physically grouped words}, the one-local-head
CUTFLOW85 crossing-transcript theorem implies that every exact
one-head solver with at most $c_A\log(k+2)+O_A(1)$ portable bits
requires $\Omega_A(k^2/\log k)$ time on some diagonal SAT input.
Therefore the separation is $\Omega_A(k/\log k)$ between the declared
\emph{access models}, not between SAT algorithms in an unrestricted RAM.
The full source identity and Boolean constraints remain unchanged; a
second source cursor bypasses the first model's cut-splicing premise.
At $k=64$, a charged abstract SAT run used 9,316 two-head movements
and reads, versus 135,313 for one-head unit batches and 26,449 for
logarithmic one-head batches; the full audit passed 285 assertions,
zero failures, including both SAT/UNSAT and signed exact-$(2,2)$ controls.
Backward shared-input Circuit-SAT counts for this family under the
NOT1/OR2/AND2 evaluation basis are $29k-1$ gates and $53k-2$
gate-input incidences; these are not SAT-solving circuit lower bounds.

\noindent\textbf{Handoff and first OPEN implication.}
The new bounded-head theorem is model-specific and cannot force an
arbitrary random-access adaptive SAT solver to retain, transfer or
materialize that boundary information. A universal post-acquisition
decision-oriented parameterized lower bound, with full policy switching,
SCC/Horn/affine, resolution and clause learning, caching, direct decisions
and source replay legal, remains \OPEN{}. Different deciders may have
different hard inputs. Root SAT alone does not compel all-context state.
