\paragraph{Latest CUTFLOW85: arbitrary-turn, parameterized two-way source-transport bound (PROVED/EXACT for one local source head; COMPUTATIONAL controls).}
The existing proper unique 3CNF equality-source family $E_k(u,v)$ of
PASSMEM84 has $n=5k$, $m=8k$, $W=32k+2$ fixed-width source words and
$\mathsf{SAT}(E_k(u,v))\Leftrightarrow u=v$.
For an arbitrary \emph{complete deterministic adaptive policy} whose
read-only source-word head moves only one adjacent word cell per charged
step and whose \emph{entire} transferable work configuration at any input
cut has at most $2^{\sigma_A(k)}$ states, accepted crossing transcripts
at the $2(k-1)$ public quartet boundaries give the independent source
information parameter
\[
h_{u,j}=j,\quad h_{v,j}=k-j,\quad
\sum_{j=1}^{k-1}(h_{u,j}+h_{v,j})=k(k-1).
\]
The exact crossing-sequence/splicing/Kraft argument, averaged over
uniform satisfying diagonal sign strings, proves
\[
\boxed{
\mathbb E_X T_A(E_k(X,X))\ge
\frac{(k-1)(k-2)}{\sigma_A(k)+1},\qquad
T_A^{\max}(k)=
\Omega\!\left(k+\frac{k^2}{s_A(k)+c_A+1}\right).
}
\]
This is a parameterized \emph{polynomial} joint time--space lower
bound covering arbitrary symbolically adaptive deterministic policies
\emph{within the physical local-head model}, with all work storage,
learning, policy switches and timers charged. Unlike PASSMEM84 it
permits unlimited two-way head reversals. SEQUENTIAL-TAPE-001 had
already described the general crossing-sequence method; this is its
source-specific quantified SAT application. A canonical batch scanner
achieves $O(k+k^2/B)$ motion with $B+O(\log k)$ live bits. Crucial
hostile controls \emph{falsify} transport to all algorithms:
the same clauses interleaved as neighboring $u_i,v_i$ quartets are
decidable by a locally moving head in $O(k)$ time and $O(\log k)$
space, and an ordinary random-access RAM decides the grouped source
in $O(k)$ word probes. The source is proper 3CNF but not strict signed
exact-$(2,2)$; strict $H_g$ cases remain separate controls.
The independent Python audit made 353 assertions without failure,
including SAT/UNSAT mutations and observed-evidence policy switching.
Standalone report, compact verifier, raw summary and five-role handoff
were published and verified:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1555_LSPV-1_CUTFLOW85.tex},
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1555_CUTFLOW85_minirepro.py},
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1555_CUTFLOW85_results_summary.json}.
All-decider random-access time amplification and a noncircular
superpolynomial parameter-growth theorem remain \OPEN{}.
P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.



\paragraph{Latest PASSMEM84: parameterized multipass source-memory obstruction (PROVED/EXACT only for sequential-stream policies; COMPUTATIONAL audit).}
A concrete family of proper unique exactly-three-literal CNFs encodes
two independent input-sign strings $u,v\in\{0,1\}^k$ in separate
clause halves using the charged four-clause local identity
$\bigwedge_{\epsilon,\delta}(\ell\vee a^\epsilon\vee b^\delta)
\equiv\ell$. The source has $n=5k$ variables, $m=8k$ clauses and
$N=(32k+2)\lceil\log_2(64(13k+1))\rceil$ bits; its SAT label equals
$[u=v]$, with $2^{4k}$ models in the SAT case.
For \emph{every} total deterministic exact adaptive solver restricted
to $p$ sequential left-to-right source passes, with at most $s$ bits
of charged persistent state at the midstream boundary and no random
seek or uncharged outside storage, the deterministic equality
fooling-set argument yields the exact parameterized lower bound
\[
\boxed{(2p-1)s\ge k,\qquad
s_{\max}(2T_{\max}/(32k+2)+1)\ge k.}
\]
The second inequality uses source-word advance cost and the actual
worst-case number of passes. All adaptive representation changes,
caches, learning, SCC/Horn/affine rules and switches are covered
\emph{within the streaming machine model}; no prescribed DP is assumed.
This is the first declared pass--space $\Omega(k/p)$ application to
this particular proper-3CNF equality template, not a new general
communication theorem. Earlier project HASHLIT1 had exact streaming
equality, and ONEPASS66 had a different one-pass INDEX SAT-source
obstruction which collapsed with two passes; both are explicitly
credited. A complete-grammar-tested $B$-bit-batch policy solves the
canonical family with $\lceil k/B\rceil$ source scans, $B+O(\log k)$
bits of live state, and charged SAT/UNSAT switching. Direct random
access instead gives an $O(k)$-word, $O(\log k)$-space comparator,
so \emph{transport to all random-access deciders is FALSIFIED}.
The source is proper 3-CNF but \emph{not} strict signed exact-$(2,2)$;
strict balanced $H_g$ instances were separately checked as fallback
controls. The shared-input NOT/OR/AND evaluation circuit has
$29k-1$ gates and $53k-2$ wire incidences, with no claim of a
SAT-solving circuit lower bound.
The exact independent standard-library audit passed 594 assertions
with zero failures, including exhaustive small source model counts,
60 matched SAT/UNSAT adaptive traces, shared-gate evaluations,
and 10 controls.

covering \emph{all} complete uniform random-access adaptive SAT

\paragraph{Current LSPV-1 state-adaptive result, ADAPTIVEPROOFRACE83
(PROVED/EXACT only in the declared scheduler/circuit models).}
On parsed well-formed CNF, a single source-observed policy ranks four
\emph{total exact} methods (unit-propagating DPLL, signed
complementary-pair/SCC, Horn least-model, verified XOR-quartet/GF(2)).
For the stricter signed exact-$(2,2)$ language, a separately charged
grammar validator must reject invalid input before running these methods.
The policy then interleaves the
\emph{resumable} executions using stage-$r$ quantum
$2^{\max(0,r-i)}$ at source-selected position $i$.
For standalone event cost $t_i(F)$ and charged source-inspection
$C_{\rm obs}$, the resulting exact portfolio obeys
\[
T_{\rm race}(F)
\le C_{\rm obs}(F)+
8\cdot2^i(t_i(F)+1)+
s\!\left(i+\lceil\log_2(t_i(F)+1)\rceil+1\right)+O(1)
\]
for each $i$, in the \emph{specified logical-event scheduler basis},
with all failed recognizers and switches charged.  The point is an
\emph{upper} bound for a finite, state-adaptive policy, not coverage
of every exact decider.  The elementary finite-portfolio principle
and proof-certified universal races were already in MAIN101 and
UNIVERSAL-CERTIFIED-RACE1; no independent discovery of them is claimed.
For 3-CNF with shared source inputs and $n$ NOT gates, $2m$ OR2
gates and $m-1$ AND2 gates, functional gate-definition CNF preserves
SAT and the complete satisfying relation on original inputs with
unique gate extensions; its gate/wire counts are
$G=n+3m-1$, $W=n+6m-2$, and its clauses number $2n+9m-2$.
A backward AND/OR compatibility table is \emph{not} mandatory.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 9,966 finite checks, zero failures; 29 named
SAT/UNSAT controls plus 40 random CNFs (37 SAT, three UNSAT);
five fully evaluated source/circuit controls covering 4,800 source
assignments.  On same-support parity sources ($q=7$), SAT/UNSAT
adaptive race events were 918/818, while bare DPLL events were
718/25,062 and direct XOR events 252/152; all inspection,
dispatch and expert execution events are included in the race totals.
The parity graph formulas are \emph{not} strict signed exact-$(2,2)$
sources, while the connected $H_4$ control is and selects paired SCC.
The abstract events are not physical word-RAM instruction counts.
Source: \path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1419_LSPV-1_ADAPTIVEPROOFRACE83.tex};
GitHub compact verifier and summary in the same role directory.
The relevant peer synthesis is MAIN107, which already falsifies a

to \emph{every} total uniform deterministic exact adaptive decider
of one fixed NP-complete language, without imposing a predetermined

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted:} CRITICAL65 gives a clause-minimal odd-wheel
family with $\Omega(N/\log N)$ aligned-word acquisition but a charged linear
recognizer. ONEPASS66 gives a $q^2$-bit one-pass, source-closed late-selector
state bound; two charged passes use $O(\log N)$ workspace and $O(N/\log N)$
word reads. These bounds do not lift to arbitrary SAT decision time.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted:} PAIRHELLY67 shows that for any satisfiable
2-CNF $B$, forced literals are jointly extendible exactly when all singleton
and pair combinations are extendible. Coordinatewise majority closure gives a
direct proof. Consequently 2-CNF plus $k$ ternary clauses compiles in
$O((1+k^2)(n+m+1))$ word time and $O(n+m+k^2)$ peak words to an exact
three-choice independent-transversal instance, with at most
$1+3k+9\binom{k}{2}$ charged SCC tests. The graph-three-coloring endpoint is
NP-complete; this compilation is an upper bound, not a decision lower bound.

For two disjoint positive ternaries with nine cross-group negative binary
clauses, all nine independently encoded source fields are essential to
distinguish the all-active UNSAT formula from each one-field SAT repair.
The restricted probe potential drops by at most one per field read. A single
new negative binary clause can nevertheless exclude $3^{k-2}$ literal-choice
selections at constant source-record cost; per-candidate-work amortization
is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}. Unrestricted symbolic choice selection remains
uncovered, and no all-decider superpolynomial lower bound follows.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} CRITICAL65 checked 3,844 instances/assertions;
ONEPASS66 checked 33,996; PAIRHELLY67 checked 9,793 assertions, with
zero reported failures in each standalone finite audit.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, CUTVERTEX68:} For connected
odd-charge Tseitin CNFs, the exact number of individually critical
clauses is $\sum_{v\in V}2^{d_v-c_v}$, where $c_v$ counts components of
$G-v$.  This refines MAIN83's whole-vertex-gadget minimality:
2-vertex-connected cubic graphs have all $4|V|$ clauses critical,
while a cubic bridge example has only 36 of 40 critical clauses.
Flipping a designated literal sign changes UNSAT to SAT exactly for
these critical clauses; consequently every exact deterministic
aligned-word source decider must read $\sum_v2^{d_v-c_v}$ distinct
words.  The potential drops by at most one per read.  This is only
$\Omega(N/\log N)$ acquisition, not universal exponential time.
Known expander-Tseitin exponential \emph{resolution} refutation bounds
do not transfer to arbitrary deciders: a charged affine checksum
decides the same canonical family in polynomial time.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 74,836 finite assertions passed, zero failures.
Source: \path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1629_LSPV-1_CUTVERTEX68.tex}.
Independent peer review of the count refinement is pending.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, AFFINECORE69:} A source-complete
normalizer partitions untagged ordinary proper-3CNF by identical variable
triples into full four-clause XOR-3 parity gadgets and literal residual
clauses. With $\kappa_{\oplus}$ residual clause records, the extraction is
exact and uniform in $O(m\log(m+2))$ word time, and exhaustive literal
choices followed by GF(2) elimination decide SAT in
$O(m\log(m+2)+(1+3^{\kappa_{\oplus}})(n+m+1)^3)$ word time.
Thus $\kappa_{\oplus}=O(\log N)$ is a polynomial-time \emph{upper}-bound
subclass, not a decision lower bound. Under the separately declared
complete certified-extraction calculus, all $m=4g+\kappa_{\oplus}$
original records must be validated; the unvalidated-record potential
falls by at most one per record read. This is a restricted source
normalization amortization, not a mandatory process for all SAT deciders.

For graph-complete cubic Tseitin proper-3CNFs, a source-checked
$O(N\log N)$ word-time recognizer reconstructs arbitrary clause
permutations, variable renamings, and whole-variable sign complementations.
For $c$ recovered graph components, SAT holds exactly when every component
charge parity is even, and satisfiable sources have exactly
$2^{|E|-|V|+c}$ models. A spanning-forest backsolve supplies an explicit
witness. Hence even a large syntactic $r=n$, fully critical clauses,
and hard ordinary resolution on expander variants coexist with a near-linear
source-aware SAT shortcut.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, AFFINEQUOTIENT70:}
On the affine solution space of a source-checked XOR-quartet decomposition,
let $t$ be the rank of all nonempty residual-clause falsification rowspaces
and $\kappa$ the number of residual clauses.  Exact quotient enumeration
and inclusion--exclusion give uniform SAT decision in
$\operatorname{poly}(N)(1+\min\{2^t,2^\kappa\})$ word time,
with polynomial working space and all failed trials charged.
In a separately declared source-complete clause-insertion calculus,
a clause whose false-set is a nonempty affine coset of codimension $c$
removes at most $2^{D-c}$ affine assignments.  Thus an UNSAT cover by
clauses all of codimension at least $c$ requires at least $2^c$ charged
insertions; two individually compatible clauses contradict each other
exactly when their bad sets are complementary affine hyperplanes.
These are representation/coverage results, \emph{not} all-decider
time lower bounds.  Role-2's positive-one-in-three affine-plus-2CNF
NP-complete endpoint was already known; no novelty is claimed for it.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, CONNECTRANK71 (external red-team pending):}
For every $g\ge1$ the connected proper signed exact-(2,2) source
\[
H_g=\bigwedge_{i=1}^g
(x_i\vee\neg y_i\vee z_i)\land(x_i\vee\neg y_i\vee\neg z_i)
\land(\neg x_i\vee y_i\vee z_{i+1})
\land(\neg x_i\vee y_i\vee\neg z_{i+1}),\quad z_{g+1}=z_1,
\]
has $n=3g$, $m=4g$, two positive and two negative occurrences per variable,
distinct proper ternary clauses, and connected incidence graph.
Source-checked complementary pairing simplifies it exactly to
$\bigwedge_i(x_i\leftrightarrow y_i)$, with $2^{2g}$ models
and a charged polynomial-time SAT decision.
Yet the rich cut matrix $M[x,(y,z)]=[H_g(x,y,z)]$ has rank $2^g$
over every field: rows have disjoint nonempty supports indexed by $y=x$.
For a declared source-closed explicit rank-one rectangle sum,
at least $2^g$ charged rank-one insertions are necessary;
the potential $2^g-\operatorname{rank}(P_t)$ decreases by at most one
per insertion. A BDD with all $x$ variables before all $y,z$
has width at least $2^g$. Neither representation is required for
the one-bit SAT decision, and a paired 2-CNF normalizer avoids both.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT positive algorithm:}
On the polynomial-time recognizable class of proper signed exact-(2,2)
CNFs whose complementary-clause pairing graph has a perfect matching,
replace each pair
$(a\vee b\vee u),(a\vee b\vee\neg u)$ by $(a\vee b)$.
PAIRRIGID72 strengthens this: the complement-pair graph has
maximum degree one under exact signed-occurrence counts and unique
clauses, so the forced matching is obtained without general matching.
For a fully paired input, the contracted binary clauses have each
surviving variable once positive and once negative; their incidence
multigraph consists of degree-two cycles. Orienting each cycle
produces an original-source satisfying assignment in linear time
after validation. Source-aware SCC remains available for partial pairing.
The strict sign-monochromatic NP-complete (2,2) sublanguage has no
such complementary pairs, so this is not a full-language SAT algorithm.
The backward AND/OR Circuit-SAT independent-transversal formulation
and its variable-wise $K_{2,2}$ conflict blocks are exact, but do not
force relation materialization in arbitrary deciders.

\par\noindent\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:}
The independent standard-library audit checked $H_g$ for $g=1,\ldots,6$
against every assignment, plus occurrence balance, connected incidence,
exact complement-pair matching and 2-CNF equivalence;
it materialized GF(2) cut ranks $2,4,8,16,32$ for $g\le5$.
The monotone $K_{3,3}$ and an unpairable balanced six-variable source,
and matched SAT/UNSAT 2-SAT controls, were checked.
Exactly 599,571 assertions passed, zero failures.
Verified report and reproducibility:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1900_LSPV-1_CONNECTRANK71.tex};
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1900_CONNECTRANK71_minirepro.py};
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1900_CONNECTRANK71_results.json}.

\par\noindent\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:}
Exponential cut-matrix rank, fixed-order OBDD width, or a large
independent-transversal relation automatically requires exponential
time from every exact SAT decider. Those are conditional
representation-service lower bounds. Their transport to all deciders
fails on the connected example, and no $P=NP$ conclusion follows.

\par\noindent\textbf{OPEN:}
a representation-independent, source-aware, charged theorem
forcing superpolynomial acquisition, valid semantic compression or
direct continuation for every total uniform deterministic exact decider
of one fixed NP-complete language, allowing solver-dependent hard inputs,
source retention, algebra, SCC, Horn, component dynamic programming,
learning, caching, and direct decision. P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.


\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (PAIRRIGID72, independent review pending):}
For valid proper signed exact-$(2^+,2^-)$ 3-CNF, complementary-pair
clause graphs are matchings by a three-same-signed-occurrences
contradiction. With $p$ forced pairs and $\delta=m-2p$ unmatched
ternaries, complete all pair contractions, choose one source
variable from each unmatched ternary, and enumerate at most
$2^h$ assignments to their union ($h\le\delta$), solving the remaining
2-CNF by SCC. The source-coherent exact deterministic time is
\[
O\bigl(m\log(m+2)+\delta^2+2^h(n+m)\bigr)
\]
word operations and $O(n+m)$ reusable words, including source
validation, failed branches, witness construction and replay.
Consequently $\delta=O(\log N)$ lies in P, and $\delta=0$
guarantees SAT with an explicit cycle-oriented assignment.
A source-complete explicit-pair-contraction calculus requires exactly
$p=(m-\delta)/2$ binary record writes, but an arbitrary one-bit
SAT decider need not perform this normalization.
The strict sign-monochromatic NP-complete exact-$(2,2)$
subclass has $\delta=m$, and a distinct polynomial paired-monotone
support class also has $\delta=m$. Thus large $\delta$
does not establish intrinsic hardness.
\par\noindent\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} A standalone standard-library audit
(seed 202610232000) completed 249,113 assertions with zero failures,
including 247,176 original-versus-contracted assignment comparisons
and 140 fully paired witness constructions. No genuine strict
monotone exact-$(2,2)$ UNSAT specimen was reconstructed in this run.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (PAIRPATH73; external review pending):}
The PAIRRIGID72 forced-complementary-pair calculus has $p$ disjoint
ternary pairs and $\delta=m-2p$ unmatched ternaries. Signed exact-$(2,2)$
occurrence conservation gives
$t_x+2b_x^{\pm}+u_x^{\pm}=2$: every contracted binary variable
outside the unmatched support occurs once positively and once negatively,
while every boundary variable occurs at most once in the binary core.
The binary clause-incidence multigraph is therefore a disjoint union
of paths and cycles. Exact existential path elimination yields a
\emph{linear} source-coherent kernel with precisely $\delta$ unmatched
ternaries, at most $P\le\lfloor3\delta/4\rfloor$ pairwise variable-disjoint
binary clauses, and at most $3\delta$ boundary variables. Every kernel
assignment has a valid source extension exactly when it satisfies
the kernel. A uniform charged decider runs in
\[
O\bigl(m\log(m+2)+2^\delta(\delta+1)+m\bigr)
\]
word time and $O(n+m)$ words. Conditional expectations imply SAT
whenever $\delta+2P<8$, in particular for defects $0$ and $2$.
A source-fixed resolution-path normalizer with $c$ binary-cycle
components needs exactly $p-P-c$ explicit resolution inferences.
This is a representation-specific cost, \emph{not} compulsory work
for all exact SAT deciders.
\par\noindent\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL (PAIRPATH73):}
1,179 valid signed-balanced samples; 1,103 full existential
boundary-projection checks; 1,105,248 original assignment evaluations;
1,082,925 kernel assignments; 110 checked resolution inferences and
222 local identities. All passed. The samples were SAT;
an explicit strict-monotone exact-$(2,2)$ UNSAT instance was not
independently reconstructed. No asymptotic claim is inferred.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (XORWITNESS74; independent review pending):}
A new source-coherent positive result sharpens PAIRPATH73.
For valid proper unique signed exact-$(2,2)$ $3$-CNF, let
$p$ be its forced complementary-pair count and $\delta=m-2p$
its number of unmatched ternaries. After the prior exact path
projection, the projected binaries are variable-disjoint, and
every ternary occurrence of a projected-binary endpoint has
the \emph{opposite} sign. Choose independently exactly one true
literal from each projected binary, and fair bits on the other
boundary variables. Every residual ternary has a forbidden
sampler cylinder of measure either $0$ or $1/8$.
Every nonempty forbidden cylinder can be disjoint from at
most six other residual clauses, since each of its three
sampler coordinates has at most two opposite-sign occurrences.
Consequently for $\delta=8$ two nonempty forbidden cylinders
overlap with probability at least $1/64$, and for $\delta<8$
the union bound applies:
\[
\Pr_{\rm sample}[K_F=1]\ge
\begin{cases}1-\delta/8,&\delta\le7,\\
1/64,&\delta=8.\end{cases}
\]
Thus \emph{every} valid exact-$(2,2)$ source with
$\delta\le8$ is SAT; as $\delta$ is even, UNSAT requires
$\delta\ge10$. A uniform source-validating positive decider
runs in $O(m\log(m+2))$ word time on this class, and
the earlier $\le 2^\delta\le256$ source-coherent SCC
branches construct an optional verified witness.
The prior criterion $\delta+2P<8$ is still correct but
no longer the strongest project-level general low-defect
SAT guarantee.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT amortization restricted to the
explicit sampler relation-validation calculus:}
if $\Phi_j$ is the fraction of the fair sampler
not covered by the first $j$ validated forbidden cylinders,
one insertion reduces it by at most $1/8$, giving
$\Phi_j\ge1-j/8$ and $\Phi_8\ge1/64$.
This is not compulsory work for an arbitrary one-bit SAT
decider, which may decide directly without materializing
the kernel or the sample space.

\par\noindent\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL (XORWITNESS74):}
The stdlib verifier tested 1,591 valid signed-balanced sources
with 16,900 assertion-accounted checks and zero failures;
736 had $\delta=8$, with smallest observed sampler success
$10/64$. Exact sampler-cylinder semantics, selected source
existential projections, both-label implication-ladder,
eight-cube, Horn and 2-CNF controls were checked.
Finite tests are not asymptotic lower-bound evidence.

No theorem forces every total uniform deterministic exact decider

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, CUBICCOVER75 (independent review pending):}
For the fixed NP-complete signed exact-$(2,2)$ proper-3CNF language,
after forced complementary-pair contraction let $\delta$ denote unmatched
ternaries. Original signed-occurrence conservation gives each unmatched
variable exactly two or four ternary occurrences. Pair these occurrences
to form a loopless cubic clause-dual multigraph with $\delta$ vertices and
$3\delta/2$ edges. Shannon's four-edge-color ceiling, followed by a
polynomial-time maximum matching, yields a source-variable cover of size
$h\le\lfloor5\delta/8\rfloor$. Exhausting these $h$ source assignments,
then deciding each resulting 2CNF using SCC, gives a \emph{uniform exact
upper bound}
\[
 T(F)=O\!\left(m\log(m+2)+\delta^4+
       2^{\lfloor5\delta/8\rfloor}(n+m+1)\right)
\]
word operations and $O(n+m)$ live words, all source acquisition,
failed branches, matching, witness lifting and replay charged.
For bridgeless duals, Petersen's perfect-matching theorem improves
the exponent to $\delta/2$. A proper valid source with
$n=48,m=64,p=24,\delta=16$ has a three-bridge cubic dual,
maximum matching $7$, and exact ternary hitting number $9$,
so a \emph{universal} half-defect cover is
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}. The example itself is SAT.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 248 valid source instances, 253 audit assertions,
zero failures; original 198/264 true UNSAT source not retested.
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM:} Zhang--Peitl--Szeider (SAT 2024) give
a $20$-clause lower bound for unsatisfiable proper 3CNF with
at most two occurrences per signed literal, implying $m\ge20,n\ge15$
for unsatisfiable valid exact-$(2,2)$ sources.
\textbf{OPEN:} neither the matching cover nor its branching tree
is mandatory for every deterministic exact decider;
the universal cost-preserving acquisition/compression/continuation
implication and both $P=NP$, $P\ne NP$ remain unproved.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, SIMPLECOVER76 (independent review pending):}
For valid proper duplicate-free signed exact-$(2,2)$ 3CNF, complementary-pair
contraction gives $F\equiv B\wedge T$ with $\delta$ unmatched ternaries
and a source-defined loopless cubic clause-dual multigraph $H_F$.
If $H_F$ is \emph{simple}, Haxell--Scott's matching theorem
gives $\nu(H_F)\ge(4\delta-c)/9$ for $c$ components, hence a
charged $O(m\log(m+2)+\delta^4+
2^{\lfloor(5\delta+c)/9\rfloor}(n+m+1))$
exact SCC-branching upper bound. This improves CUBICCOVER75 only
on the independently checkable simple-dual subclass, not all inputs.
A source-valid connected 30-variable, 40-clause, 15-pair example
with ten unmatched clauses has a \emph{multigraph} dual with
$\nu=4$ and exact minimum variable cover $6$, whereas illicit
simple-graph transport would claim $5$: \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}
as an unrestricted exponent improvement.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT positive solver, Separated-Core theorem:}
If $B,T$ have disjoint source-variable sets after contraction
and each variable in $T$ occurs once positively and once negatively
there, then the binary dual decomposes into cycles and the ternary
dual into cyclic cubic multigraph components. Edge orientations
construct both source-coherent witnesses, so $F$ is SAT and complete
source-recognized witness construction costs $O(m\log(m+2))$ word
operations, despite possibly linear $\delta$ and
$\tau=\delta-\nu(H_F)$.
The parameter $\tau$ is an exact output-size obstruction only for an
explicitly emitted variable-cover service, \emph{not} mandatory decision work.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 4,449 assertions, zero failures; 19 cubic
source lifts, 57 isomorphic shuffled/renamed controls, and
80 independent orientation SAT/UNSAT tests (25 SAT and 55 UNSAT;
these orientation controls are outside the exact-$(2,2)$ promise).

\subsection*{SPLITINTERFACE77: NP-complete sharp pair/path boundary}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT reduction; KNOWN THEOREM for source NP-completeness; independent review pending.}
For every nonempty proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ $3$-CNF $F$ on $n$ variables
and $m=4n/3$ clauses, a uniform source-exact lift $G(F)$ uses
$4n$ target variables and $m+4n=16n/3$ proper ternary clauses.
It is connected, duplicate-free, and signed exact-$(2,2)$.
Its canonical complementary-pair contraction has exactly $2n$ pairs,
defect $\delta=m$, and $P=n=3\delta/4$ pairwise disjoint positive binary
constraints after elimination of the cyclic auxiliary variables.
The remaining ternaries are \emph{all negative}, with each boundary
variable occurring exactly twice and no ternary containing both
variables of a positive binary pair. The exact projected kernel is
\[
K(F)=\bigwedge_{i=1}^{n}(a_i\vee b_i)\ \land\
\bigwedge_{C\in F}\widehat C,\qquad
\widehat{x_i}=\neg a_i,\quad
\widehat{\neg x_i}=\neg b_i.
\]
An exclusive-one normalization of each $(a_i,b_i)$ proves
$F\in SAT\iff K(F)\in SAT\iff G(F)\in SAT$.
D\"ocker's source NP-completeness therefore transfers to the
image kernel: \textbf{NP-complete even at the sharp}
$P=3\delta/4$, $|U|=3\delta/2$, $|K|=7\delta/4$.
The fixed explicit-output projection calculus must write at least
$n$ binary records and $m+n$ total records, but \emph{does not}
apply to deciders that recover $F$ and decide its one-bit SAT label
without materializing the kernel. The new lift falsifies a conjectured
easy-region rule based merely on absence of four-occurrence
unmatched variables; it does not conflict with SIMPLECOVER76's
stronger separated-core sufficient condition.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 346,297 locally accounted checks, zero failures;
the independently accessible 198/264 D\"ocker source lifted to a
proper connected 792-variable, 1056-clause instance with 396 pairs,
$\delta=264$, and $P=198$. Source grammar was checked, but a new
792-variable UNSAT refutation was not executed.
\textbf{OPEN:} a source-aware all-decider superpolynomial
acquisition/compression/direct-decision lower bound; both $P=NP$
and $P\ne NP$ remain unproved. Source and mini reproducer:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_0832_LSPV-1_SPLITINTERFACE77.tex},
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_0832_SPLITINTERFACE77_minirepro.py},
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_0832_SPLITINTERFACE77_results.json}.


\par\noindent\textbf{EXCLUSIVEQUOTIENT78, PROVED/EXACT restricted (independent review pending):} A global source-exact normalizer for proper unique signed exact-$(2,2)$ 3-CNF replaces the disjoint projected binary clauses of PAIRPATH73 by exclusive-one coordinates, preserving SAT/UNSAT, and produces a proper at-most-$(3,2,2)$ all-ternary formula of at most $\delta=m-2p$ clauses. The external KNOWN THEOREM of Zhang--Peitl--Szeider (SAT 2024, Table 1) gives a twenty-clause minimum UNSAT instance in that class. Therefore every valid source with $\delta\le18$ is SAT, and every UNSAT source has $\delta\ge20$. This strengthens XORWITNESS74's $\delta\le8$ bound and generalizes SPLITINTERFACE77's special-image normalization. COMPUTATIONAL: 346 finite cases and 272268 checks, zero failures. The quotient need not preserve model counts, and no arbitrary one-bit SAT decider is required to construct it. The universal cost bridge, $P=NP$, and $P\ne NP$ remain OPEN. Verified standalone report:

\paragraph{ANTIPODAL79: exact two-witness sign repair, no decision transfer.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent review pending:}
Every strict monotone signed exact-$(2,2)$ proper unique 3-CNF with $m$ clauses
admits a same-unsigned-support signed-balanced repair at \emph{exactly $m$}
literal-sign flips so that both $0^n$ and $1^n$ satisfy the repaired formula.
This is optimal: every old monochromatic clause needs one edit.
A source-coherent fractional $1/3$ flow on the positive-clause--variable--
negative-clause incidence network has value $m/2$; integral max flow
selects one flipped incidence per old clause and preserves every variable's
$(2,2)$ signed quota by conservation. The result strengthens MAIN103's
earlier sharp \emph{single all-TRUE witness} edit theorem to simultaneous
antipodal witnesses without any increase in edit cost.
A further \textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted} minimum-cost bipartite flow
computes the closest legal antipodal signing of \emph{every} signed exact-$(2,2)$
input, with variable positive degree exactly two and clause positive
degree in $[1,2]$; half-valued incidence flow proves feasibility and
integrality proves polynomial solvability. There are $O(n+m)$ network
arcs, polynomial charged time and $O(n+m)$ peak words.
The edit-calculus potential, number of still-monochromatic original
clauses, decreases by at most one per sign change, so it forces $m$
explicit edits \emph{for this transformation objective only}.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 352 synthetic monotone cases, 284 min-cost checks,
280 small truth-table controls, three exhaustive small optimum checks,
3,243 counted assertions and zero failures. Separate source-exact
flow reconstruction on Role 2's original D\"ocker $n=198,m=264$
UNSAT-certificate-dependent source found $132$ units of flow,
$264$ sign flips, preserved all unsigned positions and $(2,2)$ quotas,
and verified both constant witnesses in the repaired SAT formula.
The old UNSAT classification was imported, not independently reproved.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as an all-decider bridge:} editing source
signs changes the SAT instance and is not required of an exact SAT
decider on the unchanged input. This gives a positive optimization
algorithm, not a general SAT solver or lower bound.
\textbf{OPEN:} universal superpolynomial acquisition/compression/direct
decision cost for arbitrary total uniform exact deterministic SAT deciders.
Report and reproducer:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1003_LSPV-1_ANTIPODAL79.tex}.


\par\noindent\textbf{TUTTECOVER79, PROVED/EXACT restricted (independent review pending):}
In the existing canonical complementary-pair contraction of proper unique signed
exact-$(2,2)$ 3-CNF, let $\delta=m-2p$ be unmatched ternaries.
The source-labelled clause dual $H_F$ is a loopless cubic \emph{multigraph},
including parallel edges. A sharp Tutte--Berge argument proves for every
such graph on $s$ vertices
$\nu(H)\ge\lceil2s/5\rceil$. Consequently a maximum matching gives
a source-variable hit set of size $h\le\lfloor3\delta/5\rfloor$,
and the exact uniform SCC-branching decider has the improved general bound
\[
O\!\left(m\log(m+2)+\delta^4+
2^{\lfloor3\delta/5\rfloor}(n+m+1)\right)
\]
word operations and $O(n+m)$ reusable words, including complete charged
source validation, pairing, matching, failed branches, reconstruction
and replay. This \emph{improves} CUBICCOVER75's all-multigraph
$5\delta/8$ exponent, without invalidating its earlier upper bound;
SIMPLECOVER76's $ (5\delta+c)/9$ exponent for the separately
certified \emph{simple} subclass is retained.

The previously documented ten-vertex three-lobe parallel-edge graph
(SIMPLECOVER76) attains $\nu=4=2s/5$ and has an exact source-valid
$n=30,m=40,p=15,\delta=10$ lift whose minimum one-shot
ternary-hitting set has six variables. This establishes sharpness
for the \emph{declared variable-hit calculus}, not a SAT-decision
lower bound: the lifted source has a charged polynomial-time graph
orientation witness. Its explicit-output potential of uncovered
ternaries decreases by at most four per source-variable identifier
write, but arbitrary SAT deciders are not required to emit such
identifiers. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 553 assertion-accounted checks,
76 randomized cubic multigraphs, named exact matching/edge-cover and
SAT/UNSAT hostile controls, zero failures.
The all-decider source-acquisition/compression/direct-continuation
superpolynomial bridge remains \OPEN{}; neither $P=NP$ nor $P\ne NP$
has been proved. Verified report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-1/2026-10-23_1030_LSPV-1_TUTTECOVER79.tex}.


\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (GLOBALQUOTA80, external review pending).}
For every fixed valid proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ source, the two positive
signed-occurrence positions per original variable uniquely identify its
entire same-support signing.  Hence even against \emph{all} legal
multi-field sign perturbations, not merely the one-swap star,
the fixed-input SAT-label sign-probe certificate complexity is at most
$2n=3m/2=O(N/\log N)$.  This is not a uniform SAT decision-tree bound.

On $t$ disjoint octahedral eight-clause supports, there are
$6^{6t}$ valid balanced signings on $n=6t$ variables.
A fixed source needs exactly $2n$ probes for full sign identification,
while a uniform deterministic \emph{source-identification} decision tree
needs exactly $3n$ worst-case probes, via the exact adversarial
potential $\Phi=\sum_v\lceil\log_2|K_v^{\rm consistent}|\rceil$.
Yet \emph{all} these signings are SAT: two disjoint-support clause
failures overlap with probability $1/64$, strengthening the eight-clause
union bound to $63/64<1$.  Thus SAT-root decision on this sign-fiber
promise uses zero sign probes and a source-recognizing total exact
algorithm constructs a witness in $O(N/\log N)$ word time on the family.
At least $2^t$ different full satisfying relations occur despite the
constant SAT label.  The complete standard-library audit exhaustively
checked all $6^6=46{,}656$ one-block legal signings (zero UNSAT, model
counts $4$--$22$), with $118$ separate brute-force checks and
SAT/UNSAT symbolic hostile controls; status \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}
for finite observations.  The first missing implication remains an
all-decider superpolynomial \emph{post-acquisition} cost theorem, not an
input-query or rich-relation enumeration bound.  $P$ versus $NP$
remains \OPEN{}.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, GAUGEFORK81 (independent review pending).}
Using LSPV-2's explicitly source-certified D\"ocker UNSAT source $U$
($n=198,m=264$) and its source-coherent two-sign-swap SAT neighbor $V$
(an independent 198-bit witness was replayed), take $t$ disjoint
$U/V$ blocks and independently complement all occurrences of any
source variable.  The $\mathbb Z_2^{198t}$ gauge action preserves the
proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ language and SAT labels.  The resulting
\emph{both-label} family contains exactly $2^{199t}$ distinct encodings
with $n=198t$, $m=264t$ and
$N=(1056t+2)\lceil\log_2(64(462t+1))\rceil$ bits; it is SAT exactly
when all $t$ blocks use $V$.  The quotient of legal four-occurrence
two-positive/two-negative sign patterns by whole-variable complementation
has exactly $3^n$ gauge classes, not $6^n$.

On every all-$V$ input, \emph{every} deterministic exact decider for
the full signed exact-$(2,2)$ language must inspect at least one
\emph{swapped} and one \emph{unchanged} sign field of $x_3$ in each
block.  The source-probe potential
$\Phi=2t-\sum_i \alpha_i$, with $\alpha_i$ the number of these two
types already queried in block $i$, decreases by at most one per
field read, and accepting correctly requires $\Phi=0$.  This proves
a sharp $2t$ distinct-field lower bound, attained by reading just
two $x_3$ sign fields per block and testing their XOR.  A uniform
full-source validator and template recognizer runs in
$O(N/\log N)$ word time, reverting to an exact general solver outside
the recognized promise. Thus exponentially many valid sources with
\emph{both labels} coexist with near-linear SAT decision.  The
restricted acquisition bound does \emph{not} imply a superlinear or
superpolynomial all-decider cost bridge.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} the actual 264-clause $U$ source, the sign
swap, the $V$ witness and all 64 projected-core rows were independently
checked, as were 390 deterministically gauged trials at
$t\in\{1,2,3\}$: $1{,}396{,}170$ recorded assertion checks,
zero failures.  The six local UNSAT projection certificates are an
explicit \emph{imported} ROLE-2 dependency, not freshly replayed.
\textbf{OPEN:} the all-uniform-decider superpolynomial post-acquisition
semantic compression/continuation theorem, and both $P=NP$ and
$P\ne NP$.  This supplements, rather than supersedes, the
GLOBALQUOTA80 sign-certificate bound.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, AFFINECUTDP82 (independent review pending):}
For every affine Boolean system $Ax=b$, every ordering of its source variables,
and every prefix/suffix cut $L\mid R$, let
$\eta=\operatorname{rank}A_L+\operatorname{rank}A_R-\operatorname{rank}A$.
If the system is consistent, the number of distinct complete suffix
residual functions induced by all prefix assignments is exactly
\[
 W=2^\eta+\mathbf1_{\{\operatorname{rank}A_L>\eta\}};
\]
for an inconsistent system $W=1$. The proof counts nonempty right-hand
sides in $(b+\operatorname{im}A_L)\cap\operatorname{im}A_R$ and the optional
constant-FALSE residual. All values are polynomial-time computable by
Gaussian elimination. The forward/reverse syndrome compatibility test
checks this same affine-space intersection in polynomial time
\emph{without enumerating} the potentially exponential residual set.

On CONNECTRANK71's connected signed exact-$(2,2)$ source $H_g$,
where $n=3g$, $m=4g$ and
$H_g\equiv\bigwedge_i(x_i\leftrightarrow y_i)$ with free $z_i$,
the ordered read-once dynamic-programming cut immediately after all
$x_i$ needs $2^g$ distinct residual states; interleaving
$x_i,y_i,z_i$ keeps the maximum at most three.
The shared-source NOT1/OR2/AND2 assignment-evaluation circuit has
$15g-1$ gates and $27g-2$ directed references, and existentially
eliminating its deterministic gate variables preserves exactly the
original source residual relation. Gate-variable Tseitin encoding need
not preserve signed exact-$(2,2)$ physical occurrence quotas.
Under an \emph{explicit residual-record} calculus, $J$ required cut
states cost at least $J$ append writes; this is a restricted
representation lower bound and \textbf{does not} force any arbitrary
one-bit SAT decider to build that table. Gaussian elimination, SCC,
source recognition, order selection, caching, and decide-first avoid it.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 12,285 seeded assertions, zero failures,
including 550 randomized affine systems and 2,829 random cut comparisons,
plus 4,680 original-CNF versus shared-circuit assignment checks.

\subsection*{LSPV-2: ARC-LP-INDEPENDENT-FRONTIER161, exact LP pin firewall}
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN; P versus NP OPEN.}
For ANY proper signed ordinary 3CNF source \(F\) and a
primal-independent set \(I\) of original source variables, EVERY
partial Boolean assignment \(\alpha\) on \(I\) extends to the basic
real clause LP by setting all other \(x_j=1/2\).
Every clause has at least two unpinned variables, so even a
false pinned literal leaves clause LP sum \(1/2+1/2=1\).
Hence no exactly validated Farkas UNSAT certificate or
source-valid LP nogood can use only pins from \(I\).
A root dual cut's nonzero pin support must contain a
co-clausal original-variable pair. At deeper levels the
ancestral pins count toward this support.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT scoped source consequence.}
For \(F\in C_{\mathrm{M22}}\), every name occurs four
times and primal maximum degree is at most eight,
so a greedy independent boundary has at least
\(\lceil k/9\rceil\) variables. On the retained connected
198-name, 264-clause canonical UNSAT source, the greedy
set contains 42 names and all \(2^{42}\) Boolean
conditionings retain an LP-feasible rational point,
with 96 entire source clauses untouched after all 42 pins.
The UNSAT label is \emph{imported} from the published source,
not independently rerun in this report.

\textbf{ALL-INPUT assumption-dependent but noncontracting wrapper.}
Given one fixed total ordinary SAT decider \(A\), eagerly
branching on two independent names, constructing actual
ordinary first/second-level CNF children, calling \(A\)
on up to four real final descendants and OR-joining their
answers is exact on all ordinary encodings (unsupported
inputs delegate to \(A\)). Under fully charged sequential
bit parsing/serialization/guest costs, this construction
gives only \(t'\le\max\{1,t\}\),
\(s'\le\max\{1,s\}\) for \(A\in E\).
On canonical M22, pinning \(d=o(k)\) names leaves at least
\(m-4d\) untouched ternaries and raw \(L=\Omega(N)\);
there is no demonstrated fixed \(\lambda<1\) power shrink.
Numerical LP agreement and enumerative residual budgets
are not universal machine lower bounds.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 31 sources, 16 SAT/15 UNSAT,
108 second-stage real ordinary-CNF encodings,
104 actual small exact-DPLL leaf decisions checked
against exhaustive truth tables, zero failures;
four additional large-U198 second-stage children were
structurally generated without rerunning their UNSAT proof.
Physical FLMV indexed work, flips, bit-time and peak
workspace were not measured by Python.
The strict FLMV endpoint remains
\(t<241/200,\ s<11642479/23232400\).
The old universal cosine endpoint remains RETIRED.
The remaining all-input favorable \(A\)-dependent
successor, or a cofinal finite typed route to the
FLMV hard-image box, is \textbf{OPEN}.

Role-owned report:
\url{https://github.com/j15marti/pnp/blob/main/research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-25_0312_LSPV-2_ARC_LP_INDEPENDENT_FRONTIER161.tex}.

\subsection*{LSPV-2: ARC-SCOPE-TOLL160, hard-image cheap-normalizer resource toll}
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
Against the one fixed FLMV-hard Cook-image promise of Role~1's
\texttt{ARC-SINGLE-HARD-IMAGE161}, consider a uniform exact streaming
source-to-scoped-CNF normalizer with charged time $N^{r+o(1)}$,
writable/index workspace $N^{\eta+o(1)}$, explicitly emitted
event/name bits $N^{\mu+o(1)}$, and live source-name width
$w\le\delta\log_2N+o(\log N)$ on \emph{every} hard-image input.
One-pass exact frontier DP takes $N^{\mu+\delta+o(1)}$ work and
$N^{\delta+o(1)}$ writable bits; FLMV's locally indexed SAT
reduction supplies the virtual input bits at polylogarithmic
overhead. Thus the \emph{same uniform machine} for the FLMV-hard
language would have
\[
(t',s')=
(\max\{r,\mu+\delta\},\ \max\{\eta,\delta\})
\]
at exponent scale. Therefore no \emph{unconditional} normalized-image
constructor can satisfy both strict inequalities
\[
\max\{r,\mu+\delta\}<\frac{241}{200},\qquad
\max\{\eta,\delta\}<
\frac{11642479}{23232400}.
\]
For near-linear streams $\mu=1$, any $\delta<41/200$ makes the
continuation itself endpoint-cheap, so normalization must pay
the excluded time or space toll. This is a \textbf{PROVED/EXACT
consequence of KNOWN FLMV} within the declared streaming model,
independent mathematical review pending, not an unrestricted
time lower bound against all SAT algorithms.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 24 independent roots, 12 SAT/12 UNSAT;
2,133 actually serialized, parsed and scoped-validated ordinary
children; 1,992 actual exact solver leaves; 190,254 scoped DP
table updates and 986,912 emitted child bits; zero mismatches.
Two real recursive levels preserve source-name pin compatibility
on these controls, but sometimes a child remains nearly parent
size or grows. Python counters are \emph{not} full FLMV
bit-machine work, peak writable/index bits or bit-time.

The positive all-hard-image \emph{$A$-dependent} semantic
normalizer that would meet the forbidden resource profile
is still \textbf{OPEN}. It cannot be replaced by a free
source-only reduction, by a complete parent-scale $A$ call
or by scoped-width promises satisfied only by easy sources.
The old universal cosine endpoint remains \textbf{RETIRED}.

source-pinned interface:} Given original clause/box rows and fixed boundary
pins $\alpha$, a nonnegative rational Farkas infeasibility certificate
using only the pin rows for $J\subseteq\operatorname{dom}(\alpha)$ proves

\[
  \bigvee_{i\in J}(x_i\ne \alpha_i).
\]
One verified clause excludes $2^{|B|-|J|}$ boundary assignments,
with source-coherent reuse and no unverified numerical claim.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} two ordinary restriction levels, 105 test groups
(96 random, evenly split SAT/UNSAT), 541 LP calls, 187 rationally checked
source-valid cuts, 94 executed exact fallback calls; zero failures.

its finite cofinal FLMV landing remain \textbf{OPEN}.

\subsection*{LSPV-2: ARC-PADDED-GUEST-GATE157, charged polynomial-guest query slope}
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN; P versus NP OPEN.}
For the single-machine ordinary-SAT achievable region \(E\) in the
FLMV indexed read-only-input random-access model, the strict
published endpoint remains
\(t<241/200,\ s<11642479/23232400\).
The numerical claimed all-decider successor
\((t,s)\mapsto(\max\{11/10,3/5+t/2\},\max\{1/2,s/2\})\)
is \textbf{CONDITIONAL/OPEN}, not established by arithmetic.
The universal cosine endpoint remains \textbf{RETIRED}.

\textbf{New PROVED/EXACT scoped theorem, independent mathematical review
pending.} Assume \(P=NP\) supplies one fixed total uniform correct
ordinary-SAT machine \(B\) of time/space exponent \(K_0\).
For any fixed integer \(D>K_0\), define \(\operatorname{Pad}_D(B)\)
to delay for \(\Omega(N^D)\) counted steps \emph{before} evaluating
the source and then execute \(B\). It is another polynomial SAT decider,
with achievable joint diagonal \((D,D)\). Any compiled machine which
\emph{actually executes and awaits} this guest on an ordinary query
of length \(N^{\theta+o(1)}\) spends at least
\(N^{\theta D+o(1)}\) work. For the proposed time bound
\(q(D)=\max\{11/10,3/5+D/2\}\), necessarily
\(\theta D\le q(D)\); \(N^{b+o(1)}\) sequential
executed queries each of that length require
\(b+\theta D\le q(D)\).
Hence no \emph{executed} padded guest query of length
\(N^{1/2+\varepsilon+o(1)}\), or full-size parent query,
can be hidden under this successor for arbitrarily large
fixed \(D\). These are \emph{real guest-execution costs}, not
unrestricted SAT lower bounds: a source-aware compiler may strip
the delay, use another algorithm, cache or restructure calls.

\textbf{Positive FILLED/RESTRICTED all-input program-syntax construction.}
For explicit finite source grammar \(\operatorname{Pad}_{D_2}
(\operatorname{Pad}_{D_1}(B))\), deleting each marked
semantically empty delay prefix is a uniform finite source transformation
and preserves SAT and UNSAT on every ordinary input. Two actual
\emph{compiler} iterations yield first \(\operatorname{Pad}_{D_1}(B)\),
then \(B\), with both numerical exponent improvements for
\(D_1\ge2K_0,D_2\ge2D_1\). It does not cover arbitrary solver
programs, produce fractional ordinary SAT children, or constitute
two recursive SAT solver levels. It stops at a possibly high-exponent
unpadding target and therefore \emph{does not close ARC-EDGE-90}.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL finite controls:} six published mini cases
with 24 real ordinary DIMACS two-stage pin/serialize/parse/solve leaf calls;
ten extended local both-label cases with 40 leaf calls, exact
two-level OR gluing and twice-peeled guest labels, zero failed assertions.
Maximum measured second-level child length ratio is \(1\).
Padding counts are symbolically charged, \emph{not} physical FLMV
instruction timing, complete-state flips, peak live work/index bits,
bit-time or representation/hardware size.
These finite observations assert no asymptotic power shrink.
All children are ordinary SAT and are not asserted to preserve
the narrower clausewise-monotone exact-\((2^+,2^-)\) \(C_\star\) grammar.

\textbf{OPEN receiving lemma:} a genuinely polynomial-promise-sensitive,
all-input correct arbitrary-SAT transformation applicable to \emph{every}
fixed polynomial correct solver, using semantically exact ordinary
short children with paid discovery, source/read/write/index traffic,
selection, switching, failed branches, caching, ancestor storage,
reconstruction and two \emph{real} recursive solver levels, deriving
a favorable same-machine edge. The original \texttt{FINAL-LINK-ARC}
finite-hit logic is retained with FLMV's corrected box, not its retired
cosine criterion. Previous source-only/extensional oracle elimination,
boundary-join promised contractors, and selector results stay scoped.

\paragraph{ARC-CHAIN-INTERFACE155 (24 October 2026, 23:41 PDT): connected minimally-UNSAT proper-3CNF strict-subset SAT-label obstruction and charged two-level boundary repair.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT RESTRICTED, independent mathematical review pending; COMPUTATIONAL 20 SAT/UNSAT roots; CONDITIONAL promised-family same-machine ledger.}
For every $n\ge3$, convert the Horn implication chain
$H_n^0=(x_1)\wedge\bigwedge_{i<n}(\neg x_i\vee x_{i+1})\wedge(\neg x_n)$
to proper ordinary ternary clauses, using two fresh three-literal clauses per binary original clause and four fresh clauses per unit. The result $U_n$ is a connected minimally UNSAT ordinary proper-3CNF with $2n+6$ unique ternary clauses; $Y_n$ instead ends in $(x_n)$ and is SAT of equal length. Every \emph{strict proper source-clause subset} of either is SAT. Hence adaptive, \emph{source-blind} controllers that see only SAT-status bits of such strict clause subsets cannot distinguish these two opposite-label parents. This is not an ordinary-SAT machine lower bound; both parent chains are Horn-recognizable and the gadgets are not certified monotone exact-$(2,2)$.
With original source names preserved and auxiliaries fresh, the complete ordinary child relation
$R_{a,b}(u,v)=\operatorname{SAT}(\operatorname{proper3}[(x_a=u)\wedge
\bigwedge_{i=a}^{b-1}(\neg x_i\vee x_{i+1})\wedge(x_b=v)])$
obeys the exact shared-boundary join
$R_{a,c}(u,w)=\bigvee_{v\in\{0,1\}}R_{a,b}(u,v)\wedge R_{b,c}(v,w)$.
Fully materialized parseable root$\to$group$\to$leaf ordinary children with a real generic total DPLL on every leaf give two genuine semantic call layers and both root labels. A declared restricted nested implementation has
$L_1=N^{1/2+o(1)}$, $L_2=N^{1/4+o(1)}$,
$B=N^{3/4+o(1)}$ leaf calls, and conditional
$t'_{\rm promise}\le\max\{1,3/4+t/4\}$,
$s'_{\rm promise}\le\max\{1/2,s/4\}$;
full source discovery, all ordinary header/body writes and child buffers are charged in the corresponding construction. A total recognizer with original-parent $A$ fallback instead retains
$t'_{\rm all}\le\max\{t,1,3/4+t/4\}$ and
$s'_{\rm all}\le\max\{s,1/2,s/4\}$, failing a favorable all-input successor for a mandatory DELAY-padded fallback. Reproducible finite tests used 20 roots, 328 serialized group calls, 2848 actually executed leaf calls, all single-clause deletions and four outside-promise SAT/UNSAT fallback controls, zero assertion failures; Python's materialization is not physical FLMV measurement.

\paragraph{ARC-ORAND-FUSION154 (24 October 2026, 23:12 PDT): two-level exact ordinary-SAT guarded OR--AND, fused child-buffer accounting and all-input fallback firewall.}

The latest user direction assigns all five existing roles exclusively to the ARC/FLMV all-ordinary-SAT \emph{same-machine} resource-contraction gap, superseding older UWI-only governance text without changing schedules, task counts or peer-owned regions. all source scans/replays, recognized tags, actual emitted header/body bits, failed group attempts, guest input probes, arithmetic/index control, caches, live ancestors and peak writable/index storage are charged. Extending the already established two-round Role~4 M22-DP-TWOLEVEL103 and Role~2 M22-LOWSPACE-DP-BATCH152, a fixed integer $h$ of independent-pivot Davis--Putnam rounds admits a deterministic uniform source-backed clause-membership and bit-output accessor with $O_h(\log N)$ peak writable/index bits, polynomial $N^{O_h(1)}$ bit work and $O_h(N)$ fully ordinary output bits. \]
With maximum total variable occurrence $d_j$, clause width $w_j$ and primal degree $D_j$, safe recurrences are $d_{j+1}\le d_j^2$, $w_{j+1}\le2(w_j-1)$, $D_j\le d_j(w_j-1)$, with first-round M22 sharpening $(d_1,w_1,D_1)\le(8,4,24)$.  The short-seed local-minimum selector pays every unsuccessful polynomial-hash seed, source probe and address operation.  Therefore $k_2\le64k/75$ and $k_3\le(64/75)(320/321)k$.  A separately declared single-residual exhaustive upper continuation has $\Psi=2^{k_h}$, $R_{\rm search}=k-k_h$, and time $2^{k_h}N^{O_h(1)}$; neither continuation nor its enumeration budget is mandatory for arbitrary algorithms.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} Three genuine preprocessing rounds on published eligible U198, with UNSAT label imported, yielded $(k,m,N)$ equal to $(198,264,7936)$, $(156,264,9448)$, $(132,330,14830)$ and $(119,615,39400)$ in one declared ordinary encoding.  Eligible SAT Y6, connected cyclic F5 SAT, and paired H6 SAT \emph{outside} $\mathcal C_\star$ passed exhaustive finite existential-projection checks; Horn, affine and contradictory 2SAT outside-target controls also passed.  These tests do not measure physical FLMV state flips, bit-time or actual writable-space peaks, and use materialized greedy test pivots rather than the theorem's seed selector.

\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as an ARC inference:} bounded-round $\Theta(k)$ variable removal and $O_h(\log N)$ projector space do not guarantee $N^\lambda$, $\lambda<1$, ordinary child size; the U198 encoded output actually expands.  The descendants leave the $\mathcal C_\star$ grammar, and these are not two recursive SAT solver levels. \textbf{OPEN:} ARC-EDGE-90 still requires a fully charged all-ordinary-input same-machine cofinal favorable route into the strict FLMV box $t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$; DIRECT-POST still requires a noncircular complete-state post-acquisition potential. The retired universal cosine product endpoint remains invalid. P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.

\paragraph{M22-LOWSPACE-DP-BATCH152 (25 October 2026, 13:12 KST): two-round linear-pivot DP admits a uniform source-replayed logarithmic-space \emph{projector}, but not an ARC successor.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent mathematical review pending; COMPUTATIONAL finite controls.}
Extending Role~4 M22-DP-TWOLEVEL103 (which already proved the two-round existential semantics and counts using $O(N)$ writable materialization), we prove that bounded-degree independent sets of size at least $\lceil n/(D+1)\rceil$ can be selected with $O_D(\log n)$ writable/index bits and polynomial work by exhaustive finite-field short-seed hashing.  In valid clausewise-monotone proper exact-$(2^+,2^-)$ M22 sources, the first primal degree is at most $8$ and after one independent-pivot Davis--Putnam batch the next is at most $24$. Two uniform selected batches satisfy
\[
F_2(z)\equiv\exists_{I_1\cup I_2}F(x,z),\qquad
|I_1|\ge\lceil k/9\rceil,\quad
|I_2|\ge\lceil v_1/25\rceil,\quad v_2\le64k/75,
\]
with maximum child widths $4,6$ and ordinary dense-indexed outputs both $O(N)$ bits. A fully charged, deterministic, polynomial-work, \emph{source-backed} output-bit replay provides $O(\log N)$ simultaneous writable/index bits \emph{for this projector alone}; explicit query-tape materialization instead costs $\Theta(N_i)$ live bits, and replay overhead can have a very high polynomial exponent. Children generally leave $\mathcal C_\star$, where only fully complement-paired source instances are excluded. The test implementation materializes Python clauses, uses finite seeded candidate searches, not the proof's full seed enumeration or a physical FLMV trace; pointwise finite checks: $Y$ $32+16$, paired outside-target $H$ $16+8$, connected eligible $F_5$ $8192+2048$, zero mismatches; published eligible $U_{198}$ UNSAT was source-validated through two actual algebraic stages (label imported), with coded output lengths $7936\to8725\to10627$. The projector is an exact low-workspace \emph{transformation}, neither an improved uniform all-input SAT decider nor a power-short child generator. Independent exhaustive residual enumeration was not performed, so $R_{\rm search}$ is undefined for the actual computation. Role~4's earlier $d\ge k/3-o(k)$ source-record coverage floor remains a \emph{necessary}, not sufficient, bare-DP power-shrink condition. \textbf{OPEN:} ARC-EDGE-90 same-machine cofinal route into strict FLMV $t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$; DIRECT-POST structural post-acquisition complete-state $\Phi$; P versus NP. \paragraph{M22-DP-LOCALITY151 (25 October 2026, 12:20 KST): fixed-depth resolution is exact but cannot power-shrink M22 ordinary clauses.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the declared bare-resolution model, independent peer review pending.}
For any valid $F\in\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}$, $k$ original active variables,
$m=4k/3$, and any fixed constant $d$ distinct original pivot variables,
$G_d=\operatorname{DP}_{x_d}\cdots\operatorname{DP}_{x_1}(F)$ obeys the
pointwise identity
\[
 G_d(z)=\exists x_1\cdots\exists x_d F(x_1,\ldots,x_d,z).
\]
Every original clause avoiding the pivots survives verbatim; hence
$m(G_d)\ge m-4d$.  If $a_0=0$ and $a_{j+1}=a_j+(a_j+2)^2$, then
$m(G_d)\le m+a_d$; the maximum generated clause width is at most
$2^d+2$.  For standard dense-indexed ordinary-CNF encoding and fixed
$d$, the explicit child length is
$N_d=N(1+O_d(1/\log k))=N^{1+o(1)}$.  Thus bare
constant-depth Davis--Putnam source pivots do not produce
$N^{\lambda+o(1)}$-bit ordinary children for any fixed
$\lambda<1$.  The residual is not asserted to belong to
$\mathcal C_\star$; no eligible recursive $\mathcal C_\star$ solver call follows.
A source-backed constant-size patch admits paid virtual-bit access,
but no FLMV resource contraction is claimed.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 3,984 finite pointwise projection comparisons,
nine both-label/control sources, zero mismatches; the published
198-variable UNSAT label is imported rather than solved anew.
\textbf{OPEN:} ARC-EDGE-90, DIRECT-POST and P versus NP.

\paragraph{ARC-STREAM-NORMALIZER150 (25 October 2026, 11:27 KST): exact source-order scoped-stream costs and FLMV normalization firewall.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent mathematical review pending; KNOWN FLMV endpoint; COMPUTATIONAL finite controls.}
For every properly serialized ordinary proper 3CNF $F$, the exact source-order normalizer emits one named \texttt{OPEN} immediately before each variable's first containing clause, every original \texttt{CLAUSE}, and a \texttt{CLOSE} immediately after its last containing clause. This yields $m+2k$ events, $\Theta(N)$ ordinary indexed encoded bits, and $\operatorname{SAT}(E(F))=\operatorname{SAT}(F)$, with an eager $O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)$-work, $O(k\log(m+2))$-space directory implementation or a replay $O(N^2\operatorname{polylog}N)$-work, $O(\log N)$-space \emph{streaming} implementation. The Python checker materializes output and is not a physical low-space RTM measurement.
For \emph{every} exact $(2^+,2^-)$ clausewise-monotone M22 source whose positive clauses precede all negatives, each original variable spans the positive/negative boundary, forcing \emph{this declared compiler's} live width
\[
\boxed{w=k=\Theta(N/\log N),}
\]
for both SAT and UNSAT. Reordering or alternative representations may avoid that width; no universal decider lower bound is inferred.
More generally, suppose one total, exact, uniform ordinary-SAT source-to-valid-stream transducer in the indexed read-only-input FLMV RTM has simultaneous work $N^{a+o(1)}$, writable/index bits $N^{s+o(1)}$, output length $N^{b+o(1)}$ and width $\gamma\log_2N+o(\log N)$. Immediate frontier DP composes into \emph{one fixed ordinary-SAT decider} with pair
\[
(t,s')\le\bigl(\max\{a,b+\gamma\},\max\{s,\gamma\}\bigr).
\]
The published FLMV transfer forbids \emph{both} inequalities $\max\{a,b+\gamma\}<241/200$ and $\max\{s,\gamma\}<11642479/23232400$ at once. This is a \textbf{derived conditional no-go}, not a favorable all-input successor nor a new SAT lower bound. A $\mathcal C_\star$-only result requires paid fine-grained SAT transport before the ordinary-SAT endpoint applies.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 9 deterministic SAT/UNSAT controls, including matched connected eligible SAT $Z_{33}$ and published eligible UNSAT $U$ with $k=198,m=264,N=11910$ source bits, $M=31050$ stream bits and $w=198$ each; 9 eager/replay stream equalities; two true \emph{restriction/properization} levels on both labels, not ARC recursion. Source code measures logical field reads, not temporal physical flips, peak FLMV bits or bit-time; original $U$ label imported from D\"ocker. All pin children tested leave $\mathcal C_\star$. Valid target grammar remains clausewise monotone, unique proper triples, exactly two occurrences per sign and only fully paired instances excluded.
\textbf{OPEN:} ARC-EDGE-90 favorable simultaneous all-input existential edge/cofinal finite route, DIRECT-POST noncircular independent large post-acquisition $\Phi$ whose drop is bounded for every adaptive paid operation, and P versus NP. The universal $t+s<2\cos(\pi/7)$ endpoint is retired. \paragraph{M22-TWO-PIN-LIFT148 (25 October 2026, 10:12 KST): mathematical two-variable robustness for the six-name M22 source class.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent mathematical review pending; COMPUTATIONAL complete 5,625-source audit.}
For \emph{every} valid clausewise-monotone, unique, proper $(2^+,2^-)$ 3-CNF source $F\in\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}$ on exactly six active original names, every assignment of either prescribed Boolean value to any two distinct names extends to a satisfying full source assignment. This symbolically strengthens the \emph{prior} computational pairwise-full projection of Role~3 MINIMAL-NAMED-JOIN102, while retaining Role~3 ANTIPODAL-SIX103's earlier $12+h$ balanced-witness theorem and full unary feasibility priority. For opposite prescribed values, at least two Hamming-weight-three extensions exist. For equal pinned values, a balanced extension exists except when $P$ and $Q$ both have two supports through the pinned pair and their third-vertex tip pairs are disjoint; in that exceptional case an explicit source-coherent weight-two (pins 00) or weight-four (pins 11) extension exists. The sharp connected, valid $\mathcal C_\star$ counterexample
$P=\{123,124,356,456\}$ and $Q=\{125,126,345,346\}$ has extension counts $(1,10,10,1)$ for pins $(00,01,10,11)$, while the equal-pin cases have \emph{no} balanced extension. Thus unrestricted \emph{balanced-only} two-pin continuation is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}, not SAT itself. A third prescribed variable can destroy feasibility by falsifying a positive source clause.
Full finite $75^2=5625$ source census (5,550 in $\mathcal C_\star$, 75 fully paired outside), all 15 source-variable pairs and all four values gave 337,500 positive extension tests, 180 balanced exceptions matching the theorem, 1,350,046 abstract assertion-accounted checks, zero failures. Two ordinary pin-and-simplify levels on connected SAT controls and the published connected in-target UNSAT source $U$ continue to leave the canonical grammar, with ordinary child lengths $\Theta(N)$, not power shrink. The UNSAT label for $U$ is imported from D\"ocker's published construction, not independently exhaustively decided. These are logical and serialized-size controls, \emph{not} physical FLMV bit transitions, peak working space, complete-state flips or cumulative bit-time.
On a recognized disjoint union of $r$ six-name components containing a nonpaired component, the direct source-aware component solver is polynomial even though an artificial all-four-way two-pin independent exhaustive list has $\Psi=4^r2^{4r}=2^{6r}$ and $R_{\rm search}=0$. Such enumeration is not necessary decision work. Retain $k$ original active variables, $m=4k/3$, $N=\Theta(k\log(k+2))$ actual bits, $T_A=C_A+T_{\mathrm{finish},A}$ and jointly charged peak $S_A$ in one fixed uniform adaptive machine. \textbf{OPEN:} source-consistent, fully charged all-ordinary-input successor ARC-EDGE-90 and cofinal finite-hit route into strict FLMV $t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$; complete-state post-acquisition structural $\Phi$ with independently large initial value and universally bounded decrease; P versus NP. \paragraph{M22-PIN-ERASURE147 (25 October 2026, 09:16 KST): ordinary-pin target-type rigidity with a sharp component-erasure budget.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only for the declared source-preserving pin-and-simplify grammar; independent mathematical review pending. COMPUTATIONAL finite controls.} For every valid clausewise-monotone exact-$(2,2)$ source $F\in\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}$ and arbitrary partial assignment $\rho$, if the ordinarily simplified residual $F\!\restriction\rho$ still lies in $\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}$, it is an unchanged union of \emph{complete original clause--variable incidence components}. Every component met by a pinned variable must disappear with every original clause satisfied already by the pins. Because each pinned source variable can satisfy at most two of its four original clauses, erasing a component with $k_i$ source variables and $m_i=4k_i/3$ clauses requires at least $m_i/2=2k_i/3$ pinned variables. This threshold is \emph{sharp} for the six-variable connected in-target SAT fixture $Y$, which is erased by four pins. In particular, for connected $F\in\mathcal C_\star$, every nonempty pin batch of fewer than $2k/3$ leaves $\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}$; if the connected source is UNSAT, \emph{every} nonempty ordinary pin batch leaves this grammar, since terminal TRUE would imply parent SAT. The whole-original-clause component-closure antecedent was \emph{already proved} by Role~4 COMPONENT-CLOSURE97; the specialized pin-corollary and numerical threshold are the new restricted claim. A charged type-restoring compiler or different algorithm representation is not excluded.
Exhaustive ordinary restrictions on $Y$ and fully paired $H$ each covered $3^6=729$ partial pins, with no source/child semantic discrepancies; $Y$ retains exactly one $\mathcal C_\star$ status (unrestricted root), has $500$ nonterminal out-of-grammar, $184$ FALSE-clause and $44$ TRUE residuals. Original D\"ocker $U$ (198 variables, 264 clauses, connected in-target UNSAT according to its published construction) passed $396$ one-pin and $264$ selected two-pin grammar checks; two genuine ordinary pin stages yielded $12{,}048\to11{,}986\to11{,}920$ serialized source-namespace bits, with neither child in $\mathcal C_\star$. The disconnected $Y\dot\cup H$ positive control returns to valid $\mathcal C_\star$ only \emph{after} an invalid intermediate recursive child. All cited bit counts are representation traffic proxies, not same-machine FLMV time/space measurements. At an adaptive checkpoint preserve $T_A=C_A+T_{\mathrm{finish},A}$ and peak $S_A$; for exhaustive residuals $R_{\mathrm{search}}=k-\log_2\sum_j2^{r_j}$ is \emph{not} mandatory decision work.

\paragraph{M22-DEGREE3-TRANSFER146 (24 October 2026, 16:15 PDT): recovered uniform bounded-occurrence SAT upper bound and typed degree-three bridge.}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM, restricted upper bound:} Jain, Neoh and Stephan (arXiv:2411.07389, 2024), Theorem~8.3 and Corollary~8.5, give an exact \(O^*(1.1199^{(d-2)n})\) algorithm for CNF-SAT with maximum \(d\) variable occurrences, improving the Peng--Xiao IJCAI 2023 \(O^*(1.1238^{(d-2)n})\) bound. Every canonical M22-NONPAIRED1 input \(F\in\mathcal C_\star\), with \(k\) active source variables, \(m=4k/3\) unique wholly signed proper ternary clauses, \(N=\Theta(k\log(k+2))\) \emph{actual encoded bits}, has \(d=4\), so a known exact \emph{available completion upper bound} is
\[
\boxed{O^*(1.1199^{2k})=O^*(1.25417601^k).}
\]
Against predecessor ONE-SIDED-FACTOR133's \(3^q\operatorname{poly}(N)\) upper budget, this is better when \(q/k>2\log_3(1.1199)=0.20614987976\ldots\), notably for maximal defect \(q=2k/3\). The strategy may adaptively compare both charged upper budgets; neither enumerator is necessary work. \textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted translation:} replace two positive occurrences of each \(x_i\) by fresh \(v_i\) and add \((\neg v_i\vee x_i)\); the new formula is equisatisfiable, with \(2k\) variables, \(7k/3\) clauses and every variable occurring exactly three times. Its mixed-sign binary clauses are \emph{outside} \(\mathcal C_\star\), but eligible for a generic degree-three CNF solver. Ordinary child serialization remains \(\Theta(N)\), not \(N^\alpha\) for fixed \(\alpha<1\). \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL finite controls:} canonical \(Y\) has 20 SAT models; 12-variable transformed \(G(Y)\) has 44 models all projecting correctly. Illustrative source/compiled bit lengths are \(129\to233\); two actual restrictions yield ordinary degree-at-most-three children of 169,174,174,187 bits. These are not physical FLMV machine measurements. The older PIN-DIVISIBILITY141 and restriction no-shrink observations remain prior results. An original in-target connected UNSAT two-level control and a full charged FLMV trace remain uncompleted. \textbf{OPEN:} favorable same-machine all-ordinary-input ARC-EDGE-90 successor and cofinal finite route to strict FLMV \(t<241/200,\ s<11642479/23232400\); independent post-acquisition complete-state lower-bound potential; P versus NP. The old \(t+s<2\cos(\pi/7)\) universal endpoint remains retired. \paragraph{M22-OVERLAP-BARRIER143 (24 October 2026, 14:39 PDT): typical defect versus the one-sided-factor branch budget.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only within the declared independent uniform degree-two 3-hypergraph sampling model; COMPUTATIONAL finite checks, independent review pending.}
For the canonical clausewise-monotone signed exact-(2,2) source class
$\mathcal C_\star=\mathcal C_{\rm M22}\setminus\mathcal P_{\rm pair}$,
write $k$ for original active source variables, $m=4k/3$ for clauses
and $N=\Theta(k\log(k+2))$ for complete indexed input bits.
Let $P,Q$ be the unsigned support sets, $H=|P\cap Q|$ and
$q=2k/3-H$. If $P,Q$ are drawn independently and uniformly among
simple 3-uniform degree-two hypergraphs on $[k]$, then
\[
\mathbb E H=\frac{8k}{3(k-1)(k-2)},\qquad
\Pr(3^q<2^k)\le
\frac{8}{3(2/3-\log_3 2)(k-1)(k-2)}=O(k^{-2}).
\]
The exact crossover is $H>(2/3-\log_3 2)k$; conditioning on the
non-fully-paired target raises failure bounds by at most a factor two.
This is a quantitative obstruction to the \emph{named} ONE-SIDED-FACTOR133
$3^q$ branch \emph{upper budget} typically beating $2^k$ exhaustive
assignment. It is \textbf{not} a time lower bound against general
uniform adaptive SAT deciders: symbolic recognition, matching, Horn,
affine, SCC, caching and direct decision can avoid both budgets.
The exact $75^2=5625$ six-variable positive/negative regular-source
census has 5625 SAT formulas and defect histogram
$q=0:75,\ 2:1260,\ 3:1680,\ 4:2610$.
Five further seeded sizes used 400 uniform samples each with
0 finite discrepancies; physical bit-work and peak-space counts
were not instrumented.
An independent source reparse confirms the published $198$-variable
D\"ocker UNSAT instance is an eligible $\mathcal C_\star$ member with
$H=0,q=132$, but its UNSAT conclusion is imported.
For one fixed adaptive decider retain
$T_A=C_A(F,R)+T_{{\rm finish},A}(F,R)$,
$S_A$ as simultaneous peak writable/index bits, and
$R_{\rm search}=k-\log_2\sum_j2^{r_j}$ only for declared
independent exhaustive residuals. All source/work traffic, unchanged
writes, control, cache, bit access, failed routes, child headers,
gluing and peak ancestors must be paid; no semantic output is free.

\paragraph{M22-WITNESS-VOLUME142 (24 October 2026, 14:20 PDT): exact negative search savings for one particular unpruned one-sided witness enumerator.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only in the declared independently replayed raw-leaf protocol, with independent review pending; COMPUTATIONAL finite checks; ARC-EDGE-90, DIRECT-POST and P versus NP OPEN.}
For canonical clausewise-monotone exact-(2,2) \(\mathcal C_\star=\mathcal C_{\rm M22}\setminus\mathcal P_{\rm pair}\), write \(k\) for active original variables, \(m=4k/3\) for clauses and \(N=\Theta(k\log(k+2))\) for physical indexed bits.
With \(q=|P\setminus Q|\), choose one TRUE variable occurrence from each unmatched positive triple, let \(d_\sigma\) count distinct selected source variables, and \emph{only for the deliberately unpruned protocol that exhaustively revisits every raw residual assignment} put
\[
\Psi_{\rm raw}=\sum_{\sigma\in[3]^q}2^{k-d_\sigma}
\ge 3^q2^{k-q}=2^k(3/2)^q,\qquad
R_{\rm search}=k-\log_2\Psi_{\rm raw}\le-q\log_2(3/2)<0.
\]
Equality holds precisely when unmatched positive supports are pairwise disjoint. Exact double counting gives \(\Psi_{\rm raw}=\sum_{a\in\{0,1\}^k}\prod_{C\in P'}|\{v\in C:a_v=1\}|\); multiplicity-weighted raw leaves are not distinct SAT witnesses or mandatory machine work.

$A\subseteq U$ has batch profile
$\phi_{\mathcal B}(A)_j={\bf1}[A\cap B_j\ne\varnothing]

a detached fixed-length summary answering this \emph{fixed menu}
for every $A$ requires at least $\lceil\log_2|\mathcal R|\rceil$
bits. New exact \emph{meet-congruence} criterion:
an operation $H$ with
$\phi(A\cap C)=H(\phi(A),\phi(C))$ for
\emph{all} $A,C\subseteq U$ exists if and only if the menu
contains every singleton $\{u\}$ for $u$ in $V=\bigcup_jB_j$.
Necessity is witnessed by $A=U$, $A'=U\setminus\{u\}$,
$C=\{u\}$ when $\{u\}$ is missing; sufficiency follows by
recovering all relevant membership coordinates. In the
meet-complete case, $|\mathcal R|=2^{|V|}$, so no
sub-$|V|$-bit universal detached interface survives arbitrary
future intersections. This refines prior CUTSET-TABLE58
and INTERFACE-ADAPTIVITY73 results; it \emph{does not}

\par\noindent\textbf{IDENTITY-SWEEP129 (25 October 2026, LSPV-2): exact source-name union sweep and low-width approximation bridge.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only on validated source-scoped streams; COMPUTATIONAL finite controls; peer review pending.}
For a valid original-source-named OPEN/CLAUSE/CLOSE stream with cut live sets $S_i$ and $W=\max_i|S_i|$, the number of consistent boundary pin pairs on a cut edge $(i,j)$ is $Q(i,j)=2^{|S_i\cup S_j|}$, as established in OVERLAP-CUT128. A new backward event sweep computes each exact $|S_i\cup S_j|$ without materializing any intermediate named live set: reversing CLOSE$(v)$ adds one to the union count and records $v$ as pending, and reversing OPEN$(v)$ subtracts one exactly when $v$ was pending. CLAUSE leaves the count unchanged. Using a $b$-window cyclic shortest-path DP and an $O(b)$-entry pending-name dictionary gives \emph{exact} minimization of $\widehat C(P)=\sum_{(i,j)\in P}2^{|S_i\cup S_j|}$ in $O(Lb\log(b+1))$ deterministic name comparisons and $O(Lb)$ bounded-bit arithmetic, with $O(b)$ value-only numerical/name entries \emph{after separately charged validation}. Dense name stamps give $O(Lb)$ word operations at the cost of $O(V+b)$ working words. Direct predecessor traceback uses additional $O(L)$ entries. Here $L$ is event count, $b$ the maximum block length, and $V$ the number of named variables.

\textbf{New exact global approximation bridge:} If $P_{\times}$ minimizes the older product proxy $C_{\times}(P)=\sum2^{|S_i|+|S_j|}$, and $P_\cup$ minimizes $\widehat C$, then
\[
\widehat C(P_\times)\le C_\times(P_\times)\le C_\times(P_\cup)\le2^W\widehat C(P_\cup).
\]
Thus in the promised $W=o(\log N)$ regime, the faster width-bucket planner of CUT126 and streamed traceback of REPLAY127 lose at most an $N^{o(1)}$ multiplicative factor in the \emph{eager compatible-pair enumeration count}, preserving that particular exponent without paying the exact named-sweep planning cost $L^{3/2+o(1)}$ at $b\asymp\sqrt L$. This does not bound \emph{unavoidable} SAT calls, provide an ordinary all-input normalizer, or give a favorable jointly achieved SAT exponent pair.

A complete standard-library extended experiment, seed 202610250112, checked 560 matched SAT/UNSAT budget cases, 900 random scoped traces, 20 persistent-width controls, 55 brute compatible-pair counts, 80 brute SAT/UNSAT labels and 1460 approximation assertions; 233060 candidate edges examined, zero mismatches. A label-blind source-aware control directly decided all 40 SAT and 40 UNSAT examples by checked witness or checked core, bypassing cut enumeration. Python counts are \emph{not} physical random-access Turing-machine bit operations, complete-state flips or peak live-bit measurements. \par\noindent\textbf{SELECTOR-TRIM121: exact nonempty witness selector and
compiler-size correction (PROVED/EXACT within declared representation;
