\paragraph{M22-ANTIPODAL-SIX103 (24 October 2026): exact six-name balanced witnesses and two independent overlap coordinates.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted; independent review pending.}
For valid six-variable clausewise-monotone exact-$(2^+,2^-)$ sources with positive and negative triple-support sets $P,Q$, put
$q=4-|P\cap Q|$ (ordinary complementary-\emph{clause} defect)
and $h=|P\cap Q^\perp|$, where $Q^\perp=\{V\setminus U:U\in Q\}$ is the \emph{antipodal-support} family. Then the exact number of satisfying three-TRUE assignments is
\[
\boxed{|M_3(F)|=12+h,\qquad h\in\{0,1,2,4\}.}
\]
Each positive clause excludes precisely its complementary TRUE triple, and each negative clause excludes precisely its own TRUE triple among the twenty balanced assignments. Overlap is exactly $h$. Both $P$ and $Q^\perp$ have vertex degree two, so three common supports force the fourth and $h\ne3$. Consequently \emph{all} six-variable $\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}$ instances are SAT, including the 5,550 valid six-name $\mathcal C_\star$ instances, with both truth values feasible for every unary source variable. This upgrades the earlier exhaustive-only all-SAT statement of MINIMAL-NAMED-JOIN102 to a short \emph{symbolic restricted proof}, not to a general complexity lower bound. Distinct sources with the same $q=2$ can have $h=0$ or $h=2$, with respectively twelve or fourteen balanced witnesses.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL finite:} a Python standard-library exhaustive audit of all $75^2=5625$ labelled six-name signed support pairs verifies a \emph{fixed source-oblivious five-witness cover}
$\{123,124,134,234,125\}$ (TRUE name sets). The cover is optimal among fixed nonadaptive balanced candidate lists: all $\binom{20}{4}=4845$ four-candidate sets fail on at least one source; 2,085 distinct balanced forbidden masks were enumerated. The two-level exact named-join control remains consistent; serializing additional ordinary unit pins produces source-scale children and leaves the target grammar. Finite catalogue counts do not force all-decider work. A recognized tensor of six-name components is decided componentwise in polynomial work despite an exponential artificial Cartesian witness list.

\paragraph{M22-MINIMAL-NAMED-JOIN102 (24 October 2026): smallest strict-target source hides a named ternary join.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted; independent peer review pending.}
The connected six-variable ordinary $\mathcal C_\star$ source has positive unsigned supports
$\{abu,abv,cuw,cvw\}$ and negative unsigned supports $\{abu,abw,cuv,cvw\}$,
so $k=6,m=8,q=2$ with two \emph{partial} complementary pairs and balanced
two-positive/two-negative occurrences per source name. Its positive and
negative halves each have constant-TRUE projection to $(a,b,c)$ separately,
but their \emph{common-source-name} join is exactly
\[
  [\exists u,v,w\,(F_+\land F_-)]
    =(a\lor b\lor\neg c)\land(\neg a\lor\neg b\lor c).
\]
It rejects boundary $001$ and $110$, has six feasible words, full unary/pairwise
projections and full affine hull, but is nonaffine. The exact two projected
clauses are \emph{mixed-sign} and leave the target grammar. Disjoint tensors
inside $\mathcal C_\star$ have $k=6r,m=8r,q=2r$, $24^r$ full models and $6^r$
feasible boundary words against $8^r$ pairwise-relaxed words. A source-readable
$O(r)$-clause predicate and polynomial-time recognition bypass explicit
enumeration of these states. The restricted independent-hidden-witness gluing
rule, pairwise-hull recovery rule and implicit-candidate-count work potential
are \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}. These are not universal SAT decision lower bounds.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} full local standard-library script audited all $75^2=5625$
six-variable M22 supports (5550 in target, all SAT), 16959 assertion groups,
zero discrepancies, two identical raw runs, and tensors through $r=3$ exhaustively.
A source-grammar check of D\"ocker U (198 variables, 264 clauses, $q=132$)
does not reprove its imported UNSAT label. Ordinary two-level source pinning
leaves the M22 target and produces $\Omega(N)$-length children for these tensors.
No physical FLMV bit operations, live-bit peaks, flips, or bit-time were measured.

\subsection*{LSPV-3: Semantic joins, shared-witness coherence and adaptive representations}

\paragraph{M22-MAXDEFECT-FACTOR101 (25 October 2026): maximally unpaired connected source with huge frontier and a constant exact boundary relation.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the explicitly declared source construction and unchanged-original-clause frontier model; independent mathematical review pending.}
Partition the edges of a connected simple four-regular bipartite graph
$G$ on $2r$ vertices into four perfect matchings. Group the two positive-factor
edges and two negative-factor edges into pairs labelled by $r$ fresh original
$A$ variables. For every factor edge $uv$, emit the proper monotone clause
$(a_i\vee u\vee v)$ or $(\neg a_i\vee\neg u\vee\neg v)$ according to its factor.
The resulting source $F_G\in\mathcal C_\star$ has
\[
 k=3r,\qquad m=4r,\qquad
 |P\cap Q|=0,\qquad q=2r=2k/3.
\]
Thus its source is connected, signed-occurrence-balanced and \emph{maximally
unpaired}. Assigning $B_L=1$, $B_R=0$ satisfies all clauses for every
assignment to the $r$ boundary $A$ names. Hence its exact projected
feasibility predicate is $Q(A)\equiv1$ even though it admits $2^r$
boundary words. A fully charged source syntax recognizer and direct
witness check therefore decide this recognized family in polynomial time.
For four-regular bipartite edge expanders, \emph{every} unchanged original
signed-clause order exposes at least $h_0(r-1)/4=\Omega(N/\log N)$ live
original $B$ source names, without forcing comparable writable bits or
post-acquisition machine work. This strengthens the earlier M22-MATCH-EXPANDER100
$q=2$ example by simultaneously taking $q$ to its maximum value.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} full independent local standard-library checks for
$r=4,5,6,8,12,16,24$, 112 shuffled clause orderings and 1940
assertion-accounted groups with zero discrepancies; $r=4,5$ full model
counts are 346 and 1304. Finite test graphs were not certified to be an
expander family and physical FLMV Turing bit operations, flips, peak live
writable bits and cumulative bit-time were not measured.
Two conventional proper-ternary forced-literal pin levels have
$k_d=3r+2d$, $m_d=4r+4d$ and \emph{growing} ordinary child encoding length;
both descendants violate the $\mathcal C_\star$ grammar. A declared
four-leaf independent exhaustive residual list fixing two source bits has
$\Psi=2^k$, $R_{\rm search}=0$; this is not a compulsory decision budget.

\paragraph{PIN-BIT-FLOOR98 (25 October 2026): ordinary independent pin-child serialization floor, restricted.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only for the fixed source-name-preserving eager
four-clause-per-pin compiler; independent mathematical review pending.}
Combine EXPANDER-STREAM-ORDER97's all-clause-order source-frontier
bound with SCOPED-BLOCK-REBUILDER92's actual proper-3CNF pin syntax.
For a cubic $h_0$-edge expander Tseitin instance with $v$ vertices
and source bit length
$N=(16v+2)\lceil\log_2(64(11v/2+1))\rceil$,
every contiguous sublinear-clause block partition, regardless of
clause permutation, has a cut with
$w\ge h_0v/2-o(v)$ live original edge names.  An eager
ordinary pinned child incident to that cut contains at least $4w$
proper clauses and $2w$ fresh auxiliary variables; hence
\[
 N_{\rm child}\ge(16w+2)
  \left\lceil\log_2\bigl(64(6w+1)\bigr)\right\rceil
 =\Omega(N).
\]
This refutes a universal \emph{power-short ordinary-child guarantee for
that specific unchanged-clause, eager-pin representation}, even after
adaptive source-clause ordering.  It does not refute a semantic
normalizer or an existential all-input achievable-pair closure.  An
independently validated affine parity recognizer solves the same
matched SAT/UNSAT Tseitin sources directly, so neither source width
nor materialization of pinned children is mandatory general SAT work.
Ordinary child bit encoding $(4m_c+2)h_c$ is distinguished from tagged
scoped-stream $(2+4n_c+4m_c)h_c$ and from indexed virtual handles.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: seven finite cubic-prism sizes, 224 shuffled
source-clause orders, 923 assertion groups with zero failures;
minimum sampled eager child/source bit ratio $>1.8$.  These finite
prisms are not an asymptotic expander family and the Python counters

\noindent\textbf{Latest Role 3: TYPED-DEGREE-BRIDGE98, PROVED/EXACT restricted (independent review pending).}
For a source containing signed exact-one ternary modules $E(\ell_1,\ell_2,\ell_3)$ and named binary equality records, define the \emph{typed} degree of a variable as the count of its original module/equality appearances, not the number of post-compilation ordinary-CNF literals. With maximum typed degree two, equality components are paths, cycles or isolated vertices. Contracting each component gives at most two exact-one occurrences per variable; repeated-coordinate constraints reduce by truth table, and the remaining signed degree-two exact-cardinality problem is solved in polynomial time by the source-matched INTERVAL-FACTOR134 graph-factor method.

For maximum typed degree three, an equality-only intermodule join of \emph{three-state} local exact-one modules remains NP-complete even with a connected constraint graph, every variable in exactly one exact-one module, and preserved source names. The new neutral two-module bridge between original disconnected components consists of $E(u',z_1,a)$, $E(v',z_2,b)$ and $u'=u,z_1=z_2,v'=v$. The assignment $z_1=z_2=0$, $a=1-u$, $b=1-v$ extends every original assignment, so bridging does not change SAT status. For an original positive 1-in-3-SAT source with $m$ triples, $n_+$ occurring variables and $c$ incidence components, the resulting connected typed source has
\[
 M=m+2(c-1),\quad L=3m-n_++3(c-1)
\]
exact-one modules and named equality links. Compiling to ordinary proper three-CNF gives
\[
 n'=4M+2L,\qquad m'=6M+4L,\qquad
 N'=(4m'+2)\left\lceil\log_2(64(n'+m'+1))\right\rceil .
\]
A connected matched SAT/UNSAT control has $M=7,L=11,n'=50,m'=86,N'=4844$ with identical unsigned support. This refines existing DEGREE-MATCH-LADDER132 and EXACTONE-JOIN-CYCLE95 rather than claiming a new original degree-three NP-completeness theorem.

\noindent\textbf{Computational and adaptive ledger.}
A full reproducible standard-library audit tested 160 typed joins (139 SAT, 21 UNSAT, including 68 originally disconnected sources) and 260 typed degree-two controls (234 SAT, 26 UNSAT). It recorded 3,896 checks, zero discrepancies, and two identical raw outputs with SHA-256 \texttt{f7e944c01d3f11618ef2a9843a6adc7b6928dea8a4d8bf5c368a6b4eb6faca4d}. Finite computation is not an asymptotic machine theorem. The uniform source-language upper bound $2^{k(I)}\operatorname{poly}(|I|)$ with $k(I)=|\{v:d_I(v)\ge3\}|$ is representation dependent. A complete BIT-RESOURCE1 ledger must charge source bits, parameter discovery, strategy selection/switching, failed alternatives, matching/factor search, name-copy equality, ordinary child serialization, caches, witness replay when used, reads and writes including unchanged values, actual state flips, peak live bits, cumulative bit-time and code size.

Neither local support three nor maximum typed degree three forces computation by every algorithm. Ordinary compilation has source-scale children, not the requisite $N^{\lambda+o(1)}$ ordinary SAT children for $\lambda<1$. The highest-value next test is SCOPED-BLOCK-REBUILDER92 and OVERLAP-CUT128 on the bridged pair and STRICT-SUBJOIN-STATUS131, over two recursion levels, with charged shared-variable compatibility and fully encoded child bits. \noindent\textbf{Earlier Role 3: EXPANDER-STREAM-ORDER97, PROVED/EXACT restricted source-frontier obstruction (independent review pending).}
For a connected cubic graph $G$ with $v$ even vertices and edge-bisection width
$B(G)=\min_{|U|=v/2}|\delta_G(U)|$, encode each degree-three vertex XOR
equation as four proper ternaries on its three incident edge variables.
Then \emph{every} permutation of the original unchanged $4v$ clauses,
with the same named source edge variables, has a cut with at least
$B(G)$ simultaneously live original variables. Stop after the first
$v/2$ distinct vertex gadgets have appeared: each crossing edge
variable occurs in a processed clause and in an unprocessed clause.
On cubic expanders, this is
\[
w\ge h_0v/2=\Omega(N/\log N),\qquad
N=(16v+2)\left\lceil\log_2(64(3v/2+4v+1))\right\rceil.
\]
The clauses are balanced, with exactly four positive and four negative
occurrences for every edge variable, and no pure-literal escape.
Yet every charge vector is decided by the source-validated affine
component-parity condition
$\operatorname{SAT}(T(G,b))\iff\bigoplus_{u\in V}b_u=0$;
SAT members have $2^{|E|-v+1}$ witnesses.
All-zero and one-twist charges give matched SAT/UNSAT sources with
identical unsigned supports, source size and live-width lower bound.
This polynomial-time semantic escape was already known in
PARITY-SHORTCUT78 and CUBIC-AFFINE-FIREWALL98; the new increment is
the exact quantifier over \emph{every original clause order}.
A block-aligned frontier refinement also holds: for any partition
of the unchanged clause order into consecutive blocks of at most
$B$ clauses, some block boundary has at least
$\max\{0,B(G)-3B\}$ live source variables. Crossing one
proper ternary changes liveness of at most three variables.
For square-root clause windows $B=\lceil\sqrt{4v}\rceil$ on
constant-expansion cubic graphs, this remains $\Omega(v)$.
This directly limits clause-reordering-only versions of the
SCOPED-BLOCK-REBUILDER92 schedule, not semantic affine
normalization or arbitrary SAT algorithms.
The theorem refutes a clause-reordering-only low-width normalization,
not arbitrary source-aware rewriting or unrestricted decision time.
\nopagebreak

\noindent\textbf{Prior Role 3 result: SCOPE-ALIAS-COMPLETENESS96, exact restricted language boundary and source-name repair.}
For any ordinary proper-3CNF with $m$ clauses, emitting one
OPEN--OPEN--OPEN--CLAUSE--CLOSE--CLOSE--CLOSE block
per clause retains the exact original global signed formula
under erasure of event tags. Each block has locally live width three
and the complete reopening stream has
\[
 N_{\rm local}=(2+28m)h\le 7(4m+2)h,
 \quad h=\lceil\log_2(64(n+m+1))\rceil.
\]
SAT on this locally well-formed but \emph{globally reopening} grammar
is therefore NP-complete by identity reduction from proper-3CNF.
A frontier DP that forgets every name on CLOSE wrongly accepts every
such stream because every detached proper ternary clause is SAT.
Globally fresh occurrence names restore syntactic validity at
width three but destroy source semantics. To repair exactly,
identify copies of the same original source name with
$r=3m-\nu$ equality links, where $\nu$ names actually occur.
An ordinary proper-3CNF realization with two fresh auxiliary
variables and four ternaries per equality has
\[
 n'=3m+2r,\qquad m'=m+4r.
\]
The alias join is source-name preserving but does not furnish
genuinely power-smaller ordinary SAT children.
Every one of the 24 individual occurrences in the complete
eight-clause three-variable UNSAT core is semantically essential:
freeing any one occurrence yields SAT. This does not imply
that one arbitrary equality-tree edge always suffices.

Role 4's global-freshness validator lower bound and Role 3/5's
three-state equality-join NP-completeness remain prior restricted
results, not new attributions. If an \emph{all-input} producer
could turn each reopening stream into a \emph{globally valid}
width-three stream of $N^{1+o(1)}$ output bits with
time $N^{1.1+o(1)}$ and space $N^{.5+o(1)}$ in the same
matched FLMV model, online frontier DP would contradict the
published strict forbidden box at $c=241/200$.
This excludes the stated complete producer contract, not all
source-aware SAT algorithms, which may decide directly,
reread, cache, propagate or change representations.
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted} for the typed semantic reductions,
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL} for 585 sources, 523 SAT and 62 UNSAT,
7,196 assertion checks and 24 occurrence release tests, zero errors.
The full BIT-RESOURCE1 vector was not measured as physical
FLMV machine traces. The universal all-input ARC successor and
cofinal charged finite route both remain \OPEN{}.

\noindent\textbf{Earlier Role 3 result: EXACTONE-JOIN-CYCLE95, restricted and independently reviewed by LSPV-5.}
An exact local relation can be expressed as
\[
 E(\ell_1,\ell_2,\ell_3)
 \iff
 [\ell_1\oplus\ell_2\oplus\ell_3=1]\land
 (\neg\ell_1\vee\neg\ell_2).
\]
It has three feasible signatures, affine hull dimension two,
and one local affine parity equation. For any positive 1-in-3-SAT
source with \(m\) triples and \(L=3m-n_+\) repeated-variable
equality links, a uniform source-name-preserving construction
makes one disjoint 3-state module per triple and \emph{only}
binary equality constraints between modules. Global named
compatibility is NP-complete by the known Schaefer exact-one theorem;
this refines the earlier TWO-EASY-INTERSECTION61 and LADDER51
results, rather than claiming new NP-completeness.

The ordinary proper-3CNF compilation has
\[
 n'=4m+2L,\qquad m'=6m+4L.
\]
A connected four-module SAT/UNSAT pair with identical unsigned
supports, the same equality interaction graph, and local domain
sizes \((3,3,3,3)\) has
\[
 n'=32,\quad m'=56,\quad N'=2938\text{ encoded bits}.
\]
Every local domain survives complete \emph{binary arc consistency}
on both labels. The UNSAT member enforces exact-one on triples
\((abc),(abd),(acd),(bcd)\), which would require
\(3(a+b+c+d)=4\); negating only \(b\) in the fourth triple
gives the SAT member \(a=1,b=c=d=0\).
The two source-named grouped relations for module pairs
\((1,2)\) and \((3,4)\) each have three assignments, but their
intersections have cardinalities zero and one respectively.
Thus detached local SAT bits and even unchanged arc-consistent
three-state supports do not determine exact global satisfiability.
Their source-name-preserving full join does determine it.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted:} local parity/binary identity,
linear metadata-counted normalization, paired opposite labels,
complete arc-consistency fixed points and two-level named gluing.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 869 assertion groups, 160 random signed
exact-one controls (103 SAT and 57 UNSAT), zero discrepancies,
two byte-identical raw results. DPLL branch counts are not
FLMV bit-TM resource measurements. Supplied, charged bounded
treewidth or feedback-cutset decompositions yield classical
\(3^{w+1}\operatorname{poly}(N)\) and
\(3^f\operatorname{poly}(N)\) upper bounds respectively.
\textbf{FALSIFIED as universal decision statistics:}
local support size, affine dimension, pairwise arc consistency,
or cycle count taken alone. Source-aware direct algorithms and
recomputation remain legitimate.

\]
is exact for validated modules with disjoint private variables.
Local lexicographically first witness lifts can be joined through two
or any fixed number of grouping levels without treating incompatible
anonymous child witnesses as compatible. This is a typed semantic
identity, not an all-input efficient SAT decomposition.
HEVALLB1 and Main 11 previously established the compact
partial-compatibility evaluator under $\SAT\in P$; the new Role 3
delta supplies fully stated typed private-variable lifts and a

all-input SAT decider $A$ has unknown polynomial exponent $K$,
the exact boundary-decision and witness-lift circuits can be uniformly

\noindent\textbf{Earlier restricted update: SCOPED-BLOCK-REBUILDER92.}
An independently rebuilt, peer-visible source-name-preserving
scoped-block compiler now generates fully serialized ordinary
proper-3CNF pinned children from \emph{supplied valid closed}
OPEN/CLAUSE/CLOSE streams. This is not the unavailable original
HEREDITARY-SCOPED10 implementation. Entry and exit pins carry
the same original variable identity; each uses four ordinary
ternary clauses with two temporary auxiliaries. The exact
parent-label equivalence is
\[
 \SAT(E)\ \Longleftrightarrow\
 \exists u_0,\ldots,u_q\;
 \bigwedge_{i=1}^q
 [u_{i-1}|_{S_{i-1}\cap S_i}=u_i|_{S_{i-1}\cap S_i}]
 \land\SAT(\mathrm{Child}_i(u_{i-1},u_i)),
 \quad u_0=u_q=\emptyset.
\]
With live width $w$, each child has width at most $w+2$,
at most $2^{2w}$ cut pairs per block, and
$N^{1/2+o(1)}$ encoded bits for $w=o(\log N)$.
For a single exact ordinary child solver with simultaneous
bounds $(t,s)$, the \emph{promised scoped-source} wrapper has
\[
 t'\le\max\{1,\tfrac12+\tfrac t2\},\qquad
 s'\le\max\{\tfrac12,\tfrac s2\},
\]

\noindent\textbf{Prior restricted result: SELECTOR-ERASURE-PRISM91 (24 October 2026); independent review pending.}

\noindent\textbf{Exact arbitrary-mask shared-selector identity.}
Adopt the source-preserving SELECTOR-SPLIT119 construction for an
ordered proper 3CNF $F(X)=\bigwedge_{i=1}^m C_i(X)$, with a fresh
selector triple $U_i=\{u_{i1},u_{i2},u_{i3}\}$ per clause.
Its affine part requires $u_{i1}\oplus u_{i2}\oplus u_{i3}=1$;
its binary part contains all three pairwise at-most-one clauses and
$u_{ij}\Rightarrow\ell_{ij}$ for each corresponding literal.
Both components are independently SAT, but
$\exists U\,[A_F(U)\land B_F(X,U)]\equiv F(X)$ when
the selectors share one consistent assignment.

For \emph{every} retained selector set $S\subseteq U$, define the
source-typed, detached projection with \emph{independent} omitted
witnesses:
\[
 J_S(X)=\exists z_S\left[
  (\exists a_{U\setminus S}\,A_F(z_S,a))
  \land(\exists b_{U\setminus S}\,B_F(X,z_S,b))
 \right].
\]
The newly proved pointwise identity is
\[
 \boxed{J_S(X)\equiv
   \bigwedge_{\{i:U_i\subseteq S\}}C_i(X).}
\]
If any selector of a clause is omitted, the affine component can
choose an omitted one while the binary component chooses no selector,
so the detached join \emph{erases exactly that source clause}. Fully
retained triples still enforce selection of one true source literal.
Keeping all original variable identifiers does not cure existentially
independent selector witnesses. This is a quantifier/coherence
obstruction, not an all-decider lower bound.

\noindent\textbf{Scalable both-label test and source attribution.}
Apply this identity to the pre-existing clause-minimally UNSAT
odd-charge cubic prism Tseitin family from
CUBIC-AFFINE-FIREWALL98, together with its even-charge SAT mate.
For $q\ge3$ there are $n=3q$ original edge variables, $m=8q$
proper ternaries and $24q$ selector bits. Because deletion of any
one original clause restores SAT, \emph{every proper} $S\subsetneq U$
makes $J_S$ SAT although the correct fully shared odd-charge join
is UNSAT. Thus every selector coordinate is indispensable only
within the declared detached coordinate-only calculus.
The parent encoded length is
$N=(32q+2)\lceil\log_2(64(11q+1))\rceil$;
ordinary properization of the affine/binary modules produces
$n'=75q$ variables and $m'=128q$ ternaries, still
$\Theta(N)$ encoded bits, not an $N^{1/2}$ ARC child.
This does not re-claim Role 2's selector compiler or Role 5's
Tseitin minimality theorem. A paid source-aware charge-parity
scan decides these matched families directly in linear record
time, so their raw interface size is not a universal work bound.

\noindent\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL hostile controls.}
Independent exhaustive edge-assignment tests for $q=3,4,5,6$
include 64 local truth/mask cases, matched SAT/UNSAT sources,
all odd-source one-clause deletions and single-selector erasures:
1,324 assertion-accounted checks and zero failures.
Even-source model counts are $16,32,64,128$;
odd-source counts zero; the per-clause deletion witness counts
are $4,8,16,32$. Two runs produced identical raw JSON.
Python logical tests do not measure FLMV elementary bit work,
complete-state flips, peak writable tape bits or cumulative
live-bit time.

Every assignment satisfying \(F_i\setminus\{C_i\}\) must falsify \emph{all} literals of its old \(C_i\); thus every permuted clause is false and the new formula is UNSAT. Moving one same-polarity literal of each chosen positive clause preserves all signed M22 degree quotas, provided unique proper supports are checked. Apply to \(p\ge2\) disjoint copies of D\"ocker's connected monotone 198-variable/264-clause UNSAT source \(U\) at clause \((x_{194}\vee x_{196}\vee x_{197})\), replacing its first literal by \(x_{194}\) of the next copy cyclically. The result \(W_p\in\mathcal C_\star\) has \(k=198p,m=264p,m_+=m_-=132p\), all \(q=132p\) positive supports unmatched, and a connected incidence graph, with each original source block's selected clause changed. Crucially, an actual checked 198-bit witness satisfies \(U\setminus\{C\}\) while making all \(194,196,197\) false, so there is \emph{no intact unsatisfiable original block}. The UNSAT proof is inherited from the published \(U\) and the exact port-permutation lemma, not independently inferred from finite enumeration.
For a selected block \(i\), exact source-coherent existential projection over its other 197 names yields the two boundary unit clauses \(\neg x_{194,i}\wedge x_{194,i+1}\). One real ordinary-CNF level replaces 264 original clause records with two units, leaving \(264(p-1)+2\) clauses. A second neighboring block yields \(264(p-2)+4\) clauses including \(x_{194,i+1}\wedge\neg x_{194,i+1}\), hence a charged unit-conflict check produces the constant empty-clause UNSAT CNF. The first descendant is still ordinary length \(\Theta(N)\), both descendants leave the strict monotone exact-$(2,2)$ target grammar, and this publicly recognisable family has a constant-size 527-clause two-block obstruction. Matched connected \(\mathcal C_\star\) SAT controls \(Z_{33p}\) from Role 1 share \(k,m,q\) and a public \(111000\) witness. This is a countercontrol against pointwise potential initialized only from these source statistics, \emph{not} a worst-case lower bound or a favorable all-input ARC successor. The fixed-template recognizer charges all indexed source reads, grammar/template checks, policy choices, fallback and program/addressing work, and can run \(O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)\) bit operations and \(O(\log N)\) work/index bits on the canonical special family; the independent executed checkers materialize formulas and do not measure FLMV physical steps. \(R_{\rm raw}=197\) names at each projection, while enumeration-specific \(R_{\rm search}\) is undefined. \paragraph{M22-GRID-WITNESS-ZERO-CHANNEL108: explicit high-treewidth easy SAT and a zero-information exact boundary (25 October 2026).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted; COMPUTATIONAL finite checks; independent peer review pending.}
An explicit connected strict nonpaired M22 family $F_r$ of $r^2$ Y cells uses four same-witness-valued positive occurrence swaps per grid cell, has $k=6r^2$, $m=8r^2$, and no complementary clause pairs. Its primal graph contains an $r\times r$ grid minor, so $\mathrm{tw}\ge r-1$, but the public word $a_i=b_i=c_i=1$, $d_i=e_i=f_i=0$ satisfies every clause. Hence a universal treewidth-exponential initial potential cannot admit polynomial-Lipschitz per-bit descent across \emph{all} adaptive deciders with terminal zero: a fixed total exact witness-first decider accepts this family in polynomial charged work. This retires the restricted treewidth-only initialization, not arbitrary complete-state potentials.

The earlier LSPV-4 SEMANTIC-INTERFACE107's two forbidden boundary words were valid for its five-variable \emph{partial} boundary, but not compulsory. Eliminating all six names in each of $q$ Y-chain prefix cells leaves a three-variable boundary $(a_q,b_q,c_q)$ with \emph{zero} forbidden rows, because the prefix word $a=1,b=0,c=1,d=1,e=f=0$ extends every interface assignment. Exact ordinary-CNF children have $8(p-q)-1$ clauses. Two real levels at $p=256$ give $2048\to127\to31$ clauses and at $p=512$ $4096\to175\to31$. At $r=128$ the separate grid audit verified $98{,}304$ variables, $131{,}072$ clauses and a grid-minor treewidth lower bound 127. D\"ocker's published fixed M22 UNSAT source appended disjointly provides a valid opposite-label control with unbounded treewidth, but is not a connected UNSAT instance.

The new exact projection is a \emph{restricted easy-family} simplification, requires paid pattern acquisition and child access, and its descendants leave $\mathcal C_\star$. It does not supply an all-input same-machine favorable ARC successor. P versus NP, ARC-EDGE-90, and DIRECT-POST remain \textbf{OPEN}. \paragraph{M22-SEMANTIC-INTERFACE107: two shrinking exact bounded-interface projections on connected M22 sources (25 October 2026).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted existential-CNF projection; COMPUTATIONAL finite verification; independent peer review pending. ARC-EDGE-90, DIRECT-POST and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
For any split $F=H(I,B)\wedge G(B,E)$, define $Q_H(B)$ as the CNF forbidding precisely those boundary assignments with no satisfying extension to $I$. Then $\exists I\,F\equiv Q_H(B)\wedge G(B,E)$, including every surviving assignment. With chosen bucket width $w$ and interface size $b$, tabulation costs $2^{O(w+b)}\operatorname{poly}(N)$ bit work and up to $O(N2^w\operatorname{polylog}N)$ writable bits; no small-space FLMV guarantee is inferred. A connected strict unpaired M22 chain of $p$ six-variable $Y$ modules joined by positive occurrence swaps has $k=6p,m=8p$ and a source-readable satisfying assignment. Two exact scoped projections retain $O(\sqrt p)$ then $O(p^{1/4})$ final cells while producing nontrivial constant-size interface constraints, yielding ordinary CNF lengths $L_1=N^{1/2+o(1)}$ and $L_2=N^{1/4+o(1)}$ on this restricted tractable source family. At $p=256$: 2048 clauses/79907 encoded bits become 133 clauses/5270 bits, then 37 clauses/1526 bits; ordinary width-at-most-three output has 41 clauses/1634 bits. This is \emph{not} an all-input favorable ARC edge: the output generally leaves $\mathcal C_\star$, the materialized implementation keeps $O(N)$ writable factors, and the exact adaptive fallback returns unchanged ordinary CNF on unsuitable inputs. \paragraph{M22-DP-COVER106: a sharp one-third original-pivot threshold is necessary but insufficient (25 October 2026, 12:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the restricted bare-Davis--Putnam original-clause model; independent mathematical review pending. COMPUTATIONAL finite both-label audits. ARC-EDGE-90, DIRECT-POST and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
Building on Role~2's prior exact $m-4d$ untouched-clause estimate, any bare DP sequence selecting $d$ distinct \emph{original} variables of a valid clausewise-monotone exact-$(2^+,2^-)$ formula with $m=4k/3$ preserves at least $m-4d$ untouched original clauses. If $d\le(1/3-\epsilon)k$, at least $4\epsilon k$ unchanged three-clauses on at least $3\epsilon k$ names survive; explicit ordinary dense indexed output must be $\Omega_\epsilon(N)$ bits. Thus fractional-power shorter \emph{ordinary clause-list} DP children require $d\ge k/3-o(k)$; the same bound holds over two actual elimination levels for total distinct original pivots. This is not an arbitrary-compressor theorem. It is also \emph{not sufficient}: a new connected eligible SAT family $F_r$, $k=3r,m=4r,q=2r$, has four strict monotone clauses per triple $(a_i,b_i,c_i)$; deleting all $r=k/3$ names $a_i$ by exact DP erases every original clause but replaces each four width-three parents with four mixed-sign width-four non-tautological resolvents. Consequently $m_r=4r$ and literal count increases $12r\to16r$, while ordinary encoded length remains $N^{1+o(1)}$ (leading ratio $4/3$). A source-readable assignment $a_i=1,b_i=c_i=0$ solves this family; no hardness follows. Source accurate \emph{COMPUTATIONAL} controls: the local Python checker passed 25,479 assertion-accounted checks; independently published JS checked 24,576 exact projected rows, both zero failures. Matched $F_{66}$ eligible SAT and D\"ocker $U_{198}$ eligible UNSAT share $k=198,m=264,q=132$, connectedness and target grammar; U's UNSAT label remains imported from publication, not recomputed. The original U greedy clause cover after $k/3=66$ selected names still leaves 40 clauses, while $F_{66}$ has an exact 66-name cover; the greedy number is not a minimum or lower bound on arbitrary SAT work. The DP descendants are \emph{ordinary mixed-sign} CNFs outside $\mathcal C_\star$ in general, need a separately paid type adapter for target-only recursion, and yield no favorable all-input same-machine exponent edge. Input reading, strategy discovery, generated child bit traffic, caches, peak live writable/index space and replay remain charged; true state flips, bit-time and FLMV physical machine metrics were not measured. The strict FLMV box is $t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$, and the cosine-sum endpoint remains retired. Both the cofinal finite-route and the complete-state post-acquisition potential are OPEN. \paragraph{M22-SPARSE-DP-PORT105: logspace two-pivot exact projection with a paid virtual output port (25 October 2026, 11:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the valid strict M22 source, bounded-two-pivot ordinary-CNF model; COMPUTATIONAL finite Y/H and imported-U198 controls; independent mathematical review pending. ARC-EDGE-90, DIRECT-POST and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
For any valid clausewise-monotone unique proper exact-$(2^+,2^-)$ source $F$, classical Davis--Putnam elimination of two fixed distinct original names $x,y$ constructs
\[
G_2(z)=\exists x\,\exists y\,F(x,y,z)
\]
for \emph{every} surviving source assignment, not merely the SAT bit. The first pivot has $(p,q)=(2,2)$ and at most four non-tautological width-four resolvents; the second has at most four occurrences per sign, hence at most sixteen width-six resolvents. Thus at most twelve original clause records are suppressed, at most twenty short generated records need retaining, and $m(G_2)\le m(F)+8$. A uniform charged source scan computes the constant-record patch in $O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)$ elementary bit work and $O(\log N)$ mutable/index bits; a virtual addressed output-bit decoder uses at most twelve suppressed-index rank comparisons, one indexed read of the original read-only source, and $\operatorname{polylog}N$ bit work per child-bit query with $O(\log N)$ auxiliary writable space. In the declared width-tagged \emph{ordinary}-CNF encoding, output length remains $N^{1+o(1)}$; it is \emph{not} an eligible $\mathcal C_\star$ child in general. Under a joint all-ordinary-input SAT solver point $(t,s)$ in the exact matching input convention, the one interpreter on valid M22 sources attains at best $(\max\{1,t\},s)$ up to $o(1)$, \emph{not} a favorable ordinary-SAT achievable-region edge. Total malformed-input handling or another input encoding requires separate paid validation/format transport.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL source-accurate controls:} eligible SAT $Y$ and paired SAT $H$ each have $k=6,m=8$, tagged original $138$ bits, exact two-pivot ordinary output $86$ bits with four clauses, and all $16$ remaining-assignment projection rows checked. The original M22 $198$-variable, $264$-clause published UNSAT source $U$ (Git blob \texttt{f9dda41}) passed the complete M22 source grammar check and produced $265$ ordinary clauses: $7944\to8082$ width-tagged bits. Independent executable source readers tested $8{,}254$ total output-bit comparisons, zero discrepancies. The U label is imported from D\"ocker, not re-proved exhaustively. A local Python audit also tested 32 deterministic source clause reorderings per $Y/H$; it did not have $U$ mounted, whereas the GitHub-published independent JS code imported U.

The result sharpens the older DP-TWOLEVEL103 potential firewall: a committed-CNF-only candidate $\Phi=2^v$ drops $3\cdot2^{k-2}$ after just two charged polynomial-time, logarithmic-workspace exact projections. It therefore cannot obey a polynomial $\Gamma(N)$ per-bitwork bounded-decrease law at all equivalent CNF checkpoints; this \emph{does not} disprove another complete-state post-acquisition potential. Exact $R_{\rm raw}=2$ names removed is not a compulsory search saving, and no independent enumerated residual-list $\Psi,R_{\rm search}$ exists. Count each source/workspace read, write including unchanged, address/control operation, generated width-tagged bit, ancestor, cache and output query; physical bit flips, cumulative bit-time and hardware switches are separate unmeasured metrics. \paragraph{M22-PC22-PORT-PROBE104: source-rank locality obstruction for one historical compiler (25 October 2026, 10:40 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the named source-bit query model; COMPUTATIONAL finite confirmation; independent mathematical review pending.}
The original \path{experiments/review/cp22_audit.py} compiler \texttt{compile22} maps a proper ordinary 3CNF with $m$ clauses to a \emph{signed}, not clausewise-monotone, proper exact-$(2^+,2^-)$ CNF with $72m$ variables and $96m$ clauses. It sorts source variable names, allocates $2d_F(x)$ consecutive clone IDs to each original source name $x$, and emits cyclic padding edges in that global order. This creates a specific source-degree prefix dependency.
For $q\ge1$, consider the ordinary positive 3CNF
\[
F_q=(1\vee3\vee4)\wedge(1\vee2\vee3)\wedge(2\vee3\vee4)
\wedge\bigwedge_{j=0}^{q-1}(1\vee3\vee(j+5)).
\]
At one fixed addressed output-padding field (zero-based clause $8(q+3)+2(q+2)-1$), its positive second literal magnitude is $1$. Changing any single trailing clause first literal from $1$ to $4$ changes the \emph{same output field} to $2q+5$, without changing source or target header/length. Since these $q$ variants differ in disjoint source variable-ID fields, deterministic stateless source-bit query evaluation of the high bit of that output field must read at least $q=\Omega(N/\log N)$ input bits on $F_q$, by exact adaptive transcript indistinguishability. Appending the eight contradictory signed clauses on three fresh variables gives an identical \emph{UNSAT} control. This proves only a \emph{named historical compiler output-bit locality floor}, not all-decider SAT time; an alternative locally ordered compiler or a paid precomputed rank directory may evade it. A source-structure recognizer solves the controls cheaply. Neither CP22LIFT1's integer Cutting Planes derivations nor this syntax-probe bound supplies an all-input ARC resource contraction or a post-acquisition universal potential.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} full independent local audit passed 3296 assertion-accounted checks with zero failures over 24 SAT/UNSAT parameter cases, all one-field changes and exact smaller compiled outputs; actual two-level controls have clause counts $4\to384\to36864$ (SAT) and $12\to1152\to110592$ (UNSAT). The standalone GitHub peer reproducer is separately published for independent execution. The historical $t+s<2\cos(\pi/7)$ universal endpoint remains \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}. ARC-EDGE-90, LOCAL-COOK-TAGGED-SIGNED22-1, DIRECT-POST and P versus NP remain \textbf{OPEN}. Canonical $L_\star$ excludes only \emph{fully} complementary-clause-paired strict monotone $(2,2)$ formulas, not individual pairs.

\paragraph{M22-DP-TWOLEVEL103: exact two-stage ordinary-CNF elimination and potential firewall (25 October 2026, 09:36 KST).}
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM} (classical Davis--Putnam existential resolution); \textbf{PROVED/EXACT} its declared M22-specific fixed-two-stage size/degree specialization, with independent mathematical review pending; \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL} finite SAT and source-dependent UNSAT controls. \textbf{ARC-EDGE-90, DIRECT-POST and P versus NP remain OPEN.} For each valid strict clausewise-monotone exact-$(2^+,2^-)$ source $F$, one pivot $x$ has precisely two positive tails $A_1,A_2$ and two negative tails $B_1,B_2$. Replace its four parent clauses by all non-tautological $A_i\lor B_j$, computing $\exists x F$ exactly on every remaining source assignment. With $h_x\le4$ surviving pairs, the new ordinary CNF has $m'=m-4+h_x\le m$ clauses and $L'=3m-12+4h_x$ literal occurrences; each new surviving clause is a proper \emph{mixed-sign} four-clause, so this transformation generally \emph{leaves} $\mathcal C_\star$. A greedy independent set in the original primal degree-eight graph eliminates $q_1\ge k/9$ names at once with disjoint parent clauses, $v_1\le8k/9$, $m_1\le m$, width at most four, and each remaining polarity-degree at most four. A second greedy set in the degree-at-most-24 intermediate primal graph eliminates $q_2\ge v_1/25$, giving $v_2\le64k/75$, $m_2\le m_1+8q_2$, width at most six. Both stages are exact full existential projections, \emph{not} merely SAT-bit-preserving neutral child repairs. A straightforward indexed, materialized two-stage machine pays $O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)$ elementary bit work and $O(N)$ writable/index bits, including source reads, pivot discovery, generated resolvents, headers, deduplication, live child buffers and ancestors. Its ordinary children have $O(N)$ encoded length, \emph{not} $N^{\lambda+o(1)}$ for $\lambda<1$, and its peak writable space is far outside the strict FLMV box $t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$. One fixed machine must attain any simultaneous exponents; the retired cosine product endpoint does not apply.

For canonical eligible SAT $Y$, the two actual projections have $(v,m,L)=(6,8,24)\to(5,5,16)\to(4,4,14)$; paired SAT $H$ is intentionally outside $\mathcal C_\star$ and has $(6,8,24)\to(4,4,16)\to(0,0,0)$. On the original eligible published UNSAT D\"ocker source $U$ (its UNSAT label imported, not exhaustively re-proved), the independently computed stages are $(198,264,792)\to(156,264,960)\to(132,330,1588)$. The independent local Python auditor checked 72 complete small-source existential projection rows; the fetched original $U$ source (blob \texttt{f9dda41}) was transformed in two actual stages by a separately coded JavaScript check. The general two-level upper bound is a useful adaptive simplifier, but the $U$ literal stream \emph{grows} despite variable-count decrease. Disjoint replicated SAT/UNSAT controls retain $\Theta(N)$ encoded output for this fixed policy and are not hard-family lower bounds.

A complete-state candidate potential required to equal $\Phi=2^{v(G)}$ at exact-CNF committed checkpoints falls by at least $2^k-2^{8k/9}$ across the \emph{charged} polynomial-work first-stage projection. Thus any universal transition inequality $\Phi(C)-\Phi(C')\le\Gamma(N)\cdot\mathrm{bitwork}(C,C')$ for this specific checkpoint potential requires exponentially large $\Gamma(N)$: the naive $2^v$ all-policy post-acquisition potential with polynomial $\Gamma$ is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}. This does not eliminate other noncircular structural potentials; it does not count input acquisition twice or turn an optional $2^{v}$ enumeration budget into necessary work. \paragraph{M22-QUAD-NEUTRAL102: sharp balanced four-clause neutral port repair and its semantic firewall (25 October 2026, 08:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT within the add-only independently assignable universal-port M22 clause-deletion model; COMPUTATIONAL two independently executed finite auditors and a cross-language raw-results check; mathematical peer review pending. ARC-EDGE-90, DIRECT-POST and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
For any valid clausewise-monotone unique proper signed $(2^+,2^-)$ formula $F$, delete two positive and two negative original triples with old ports $x_1,\ldots,x_6$ and $y_1,\ldots,y_6$. Introduce six fresh $a,b,c,d,e,f$ and add the positive triples $(a,d,x_1),(a,e,x_2),(b,e,x_3),(b,f,x_4),(c,f,x_5),(c,d,x_6)$ and negative triples $(-a,-d,-y_1),(-a,-f,-y_2),(-b,-d,-y_3),(-b,-e,-y_4),(-c,-e,-y_5),(-c,-f,-y_6)$. All old assignments extend to the added clauses using $a=b=c=1,d=e=f=0$, even with overlapping old names; each source signed incidence is repaired, all fresh degrees are $(2,2)$, every new triple is proper and unique, and the positive fresh pair $(a,e)$ has no negative counterpart. Thus
\[
 D_4(F)\in\mathcal C_\star,\quad
 \operatorname{SAT}(D_4(F))\iff\operatorname{SAT}(F\setminus D),\quad
 (k',m')=(k+6,m+8).
\]
The result improves NEUTRAL-PAIR101's two separate opposite-sign repairs (twelve auxiliaries and twenty inserted clauses) to one six-auxiliary twelve-clause repair for four deleted clauses. Signed quota conservation yields $p=d_++2h/3$, $q=d_-+2h/3$. For a universal independent-port interface, all old literals can be simultaneously falsified; with $t$ fresh true names each new positive clause needs a fresh true and each negative a fresh false, giving $2t\ge p$, $2(h-t)\ge q$, hence $h\ge3(d_++d_-)/2$. Balanced $2r+2r$ deletions therefore require $h\ge6r$, achieved by $r$ quad gadgets, with $12r$ inserted clauses. This sharp auxiliary floor does \emph{not} extend to old-name elimination, context-specialized ports, or every SAT algorithm. Type restoration also does not reconstruct the deleted original constraints: since every M22 source has even positive and negative clause counts, deleting \emph{all} source clauses in balanced quads always produces an eligible SAT formula, even when the parent is D\"ocker's published UNSAT $U$ (old $(198,264,7154)$, new $(594,792,26168)$ in $k,m,N$). Genuine source-compatible parent gluing remains necessary.
The dense indexed bit length increases by $3(m+1)(w'-w)+24(w'+1)>0$, with $w'=\lceil\log_2(k+7)\rceil$; two actual $Y$ repair levels have $(k,m,N,\#\mathrm{models})=(6,8,107,20)\to(12,16,254,314)\to(18,24,449,5936)$. The local full auditor passed 9,460 assertion-accounted checks and the published portable peer reproducer passed 9,303, with zero discrepancies; an independently generated raw table covers all 72 selected four-clause repairs on $Y$ and fully paired $H$. These are finite grammar/projection/model-count claims, not FLMV physical time or space measurements. Eager child bytes, source reads and output, address/index generation, policy acquisition/switching, replay, caches, ancestors and actual live writable/index bits remain charged; no $R_{\rm search}$ or direct superpolynomial post-acquisition $\Phi$ is proved. The FLMV region still concerns one fixed total uniform \emph{ordinary}-SAT machine simultaneously attaining both resources on all inputs, with corrected strict forbidden box $t<241/200$, $s<11642479/23232400$; the old cosine endpoint is retired. The missing all-input ARC successor and the direct post-acquisition lower-bound arrow remain OPEN. Exact report, peer script, machine-readable 72-case raw table and five-role ledger:

\paragraph{M22-NEUTRAL-PAIR101: shared opposite-sign clause-deletion repair and universal-port floor (25 October 2026, 07:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the declared add-only six-independent-port interface, COMPUTATIONAL 382,721 local finite checks; independent review pending. ARC-EDGE-90, DIRECT-POST and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
For any valid $\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}$ source $F$ and a removed positive triple $(x,y,z)$ together with a removed negative triple $(-u,-v,-w)$, a ten-clause six-fresh-name gadget gives an exact $\mathcal C_\star$ type-restored child whose label is the label of \emph{the two-clause deletion}, not automatically of $F$. Five positives are $(a,b,x),(a,d,y),(b,e,z),(c,d,f),(c,e,f)$; five negatives are $(-a,-d,-u),(-a,-d,-v),(-b,-e,-w),(-b,-c,-f),(-c,-e,-f)$. The one fixed assignment $a=b=c=1$, $d=e=f=0$ satisfies every added clause independently of all old port values, and the fresh-only positive $(c,d,f)$ is unpaired. Both signs have exactly two fresh occurrences per fresh variable, so $(k',m')=(k+6,m+8)$. This improves on using two independent NEUTRAL-PORT100 gadgets (twelve new variables and eighteen added clauses), without claiming size contraction. For any add-only signed quota restorer deleting $d_+,d_-$ clauses, with $h$ fresh names and \emph{no lost original active name}, the incidence conservation identity forces $h\equiv0\pmod3$, $p=d_++2h/3$, $q=d_-+2h/3$, and $m'=m+4h/3$. For one opposite-sign pair on six independent old ports, universal extension is impossible at $h=0$ or $h=3$: on all-zero positive/all-one negative ports, three positive added clauses require at least two fresh true names and three negative require at least two fresh false names. Hence the six-fresh construction has a sharp local floor. If $z$ original names disappear, the more general exact equation is $m'=m+4(h-z)/3$, so the old-name-elimination escape is \emph{not} excluded. The actual indexed length increases by $3(m+1)(w'-w)+24(w'+1)>0$; two genuine $Y$ levels have $(k,m,N)=(6,8,107)\to(12,16,254)\to(18,24,449)$, and both remain SAT. \paragraph{M22-COMPONENT-CLOSURE97: exact original-clause deletion grammar and safe paired-component erasure (25 Oct 2026, 04:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT for the declared source-preserving component/deletion model, with independent peer checking pending. ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
Let $F\in\mathcal C_{\rm M22}$ be an exact clausewise-monotone signed $(2^+,2^-)$ proper ternary CNF, with $c$ connected clause--variable incidence components, of which $d$ are not fully complementary paired. A subformula retaining an arbitrary subset of \emph{unchanged source clause records} remains in $\mathcal C_{\rm M22}$ if and only if its clauses are a union of whole incidence components. Indeed, keeping any occurrence of variable $x$ requires keeping all four original occurrences to retain both exact sign quotas, forcing every source clause in its component. Consequently exactly $2^c$ original-clause subsets remain valid $\mathcal C_{\rm M22}$ instances, and exactly $2^c-2^{c-d}$ belong to $\mathcal C_\star$, with the empty subset convention outside $\mathcal C_\star$. A connected nonpaired input has no strict in-target deletion child. This does not restrict semantic rewriting or paid type-restoring reductions.
A source-aware projection $\Pi(F)$ erases all fully complementary-paired incidence components and preserves the complete parent SAT label, by D\"ocker's paired-SAT theorem and disjoint-component factorization. It is idempotent and stays in $\mathcal C_\star$, but $\Pi(F)=F$ for every connected nonpaired $F$, preventing a universal power-shrinking successor from this route. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: $66{,}048$ exhaustive clause-index masks of $Y,H,Y\dot\cup H$, $66{,}062$ assertion-accounted checks, no failures; valid $\mathcal C_{\rm M22}$ subset counts $2,2,4$, valid $\mathcal C_\star$ counts $1,0,2$, while $Y$ is SAT (20 models) and $H$ is fully paired SAT (18 models). The original connected 198-variable/264-clause D\"ocker target-UNSAT fixture had every single deletion exit the grammar, checked against blob \texttt{f9dda41a}; its UNSAT label is imported from the published theorem.
All source access, parser/recognizer work, component checks, clause headers/bodies, cache/replay, joint peak writable/index storage and fallback remain chargeable. This positive restricted component simplification supplies neither a noncircular post-acquisition potential nor a jointly favorable all-input ARC edge. In the FLMV-matched ordinary-SAT random-access model the valid forbidden box remains $t<241/200$ and $s<11642479/23232400$; the historical universal cosine-product landing remains \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}.

\paragraph{READ-LAST-SPACE-GAUGE95: logspace source cofactors preserve the joint machine bounds and defeat compulsory post-coverage work (25 Oct, 02:55 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the indexed read-only-input model; independent peer review pending. ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain OPEN.}
COVERCOF1 and PHASEBRIDGE1 previously showed that an arbitrary fixed
uniform total deterministic exact decider $A$ may compute both last-bit
cofactors before physically reading the last real input bit; their
particular compiler copied an $N$-bit prefix to writable memory.
LOGCOF1 further generalizes their copied-prefix form to
$2^{O(\log N)}$ precomputed cofactors while retaining an $N$-bit
writable source cache; neither earlier form attains the new
$O(\log N)$ incremental space overhead.

The new source-indexed compiler \emph{does not copy that prefix}:
scan $N-1$ source bits without retaining them, simulate $A$ on
the two complete virtual last-bit variants with charged indexed
reads of the same read-only source, reuse $A$'s writable tapes, then
read the last actual source bit and select the cached correct answer.
Both SAT and UNSAT, malformed bitstrings, controller work and all
source-address accesses are covered. For every all-input joint machine
pair $(t,s)$ with $t\ge1$, its read-last successor has
\[
 T_B(N)=O((N+T_A(N))\operatorname{polylog}N),\qquad
 S_B(N)=O(S_A(N)+\log(N+2)),
\]
and so merely \emph{preserves}, rather than improves, the same joint
exponents. On the exact 224-bit proper-3CNF opposite-label pair,
223 source bits and all metadata agree; the two charged virtual
answers are $(0,1)$ before reading the final sign bit.
The complete-state checkpoint after that last read has only $O(1)$
remaining charged work. Hence a terminal-zero potential whose every
legal bit transition decreases by at most $L(N)$ times its charged
work cannot take superpolynomial value at this checkpoint on
\emph{every} decider when $L$ is polynomial, by Role~5's exact
telescoping lemma. This is a \emph{phase-gauge counterexample},
\textbf{not} a whole-execution lower-bound theorem; a universal
all-input favorable ARC successor remains \textbf{OPEN}.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: exact local full auditor 1,723 checks (172
inputs, 125 SAT/47 UNSAT) and independently written peer auditor
2,058 checks (514 finite source cases), with zero failures; these
are not physical FLMV switching/bit-time measurements.
GitHub-published report, executable full/peer checkers, machine-readable
summary, and source-coverage handoff:

\paragraph{INTERVAL-PIN-COVER90: exact pinned-interval OR join and tight adaptive acquisition cover (23:38 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only in a restricted source-linked interval model; P versus NP and ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN.}
For the properized implication-chain promise
$P_q(b)$, which is SAT iff some of its $q$ orientation fields is zero,
an interval of $h$ consecutive orientations defines an
\emph{ordinary proper-3CNF child} $P_h(b|_I)$ with new endpoint pins,

recursively remain in the same easy recognizable promise.

adaptive controller on all-one UNSAT with $r$ directly read
orientations and $Q$ SAT-status queries supported on intervals
of length at most $K$ obeys the \emph{tight} bound
$r+KQ\ge q$.

ordinary serialization and virtual dereferencing are separately charged.
With $K=\lceil\sqrt q\rceil$ and one fixed exact all-input child
decider $(t,s)$, the \emph{restricted} eager wrapper has
$t'\le\max\{1,(1+t)/2\}$ and
$s'\le\max\{1/2,s/2\}$, including paid child headers and
one readable buffer. Generic-source fallback retains the old bound,
so this is \emph{not} an all-input achievable-pair successor.
The matched controls
$A=(x\vee a)\wedge(x\vee\neg a)$,
$B_U=(\neg x\vee b)\wedge(\neg x\vee\neg b)$,
$B_S=(x\vee b)\wedge(x\vee\neg b)$
show that separately SAT blocks can join into either SAT or UNSAT
with identical metadata and SAT-status transcripts; source
boundary compatibility, not status aggregation, is indispensable.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 18,795 full local Python assertion-accounted
checks, zero failures, including orientation sequences through
$q=1024$. Publication and peer review status: standalone
short LaTeX statement, detailed additive handoff, concise
reproducer and raw summary are verified in GitHub; the
locally tested full auditor is in the run package and is not
asserted to have been peer-executed.

\paragraph{MINCORE-ADAPTIVE-ORACLE89: unlimited proper-deletion SAT statuses have zero discriminating power (23:13 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted indexed source/deletion-SAT-oracle model; peer audit pending.}
Lift the scalable \(q\)-orientation 2-SAT chain \(F_b\) clausewise to proper
ternary \(P_q(b)\) by replacing each binary \(D_j\) with
\((D_j\vee t_j)\wedge(D_j\vee\neg t_j)\).
Then \(n=2q+7\), \(m=2q+8\), \(m-n=1\), and
\(N=(8q+34)\lceil\log_2(64(4q+16))\rceil=\Theta(q\log q)\).
The all-one member is \emph{minimally UNSAT}; every other orientation is SAT.
Every strict clause-deletion subformula is SAT on \emph{every} member.
Thus even arbitrarily many \emph{adaptively selected} exact SAT-status calls
on strict deletion children give an all-YES transcript for both labels.
When actual signed input fields are acquired only through charged bit/word
queries, every exact deterministic controller must still inspect one
coordinate in each of \(q\) disjoint orientation groups on the all-one UNSAT
source: \(R_{\mathrm{src}}\ge q=\Omega(N/\log N)\).
A paid direct scan/SCC matches this input order, so this is
\emph{not} a post-acquisition time lower bound and gives no positive charged
transport remainder \(L_{\mathrm{old}}-R-H\).
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} independent full Python auditor, 87,375
assertion-accounted checks, zero failures; source grammar, single deletions,
all strict subsets for small \(q\), signs, and source model counts.
This extends the earlier MINIMAL-UNSAT-PROJECTION20 and
ADAPTIVE-COVER75 \emph{within a stronger restricted oracle test}; it does
not replace compatibility-aware pinned children, source computation,
all-input gluing, or an arbitrary algorithm.
The original HEREDITARY-SCOPED10 executable remains unrecovered.
\textbf{OPEN:} all-input ARC-EDGE-90, matched FLMV successor, and P versus NP.

\paragraph{SCOPE-CLIQUE-FIREWALL88: connected opposite-label source width and paid direct decision (21:34 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in source-name/clause-preserving scoped event streams; independent review pending.}
For $p\ge2$ put $C_p=\bigwedge_{i<j}(x_i\vee x_j\vee a)$, and append
all eight signed clauses on $a,b,c$ for the UNSAT member $U_p$.
Replace $(a\vee b\vee c)$ by a duplicate of $(\neg a\vee b\vee c)$
for the SAT member $V_p$. Both have $n=p+3$,
$m=\binom p2+8$, and the same encoded length
$N=(4m+2)\lceil\log_2(64(n+m+1))\rceil=\Theta(p^2\log p)$,
with $\#\mathrm{SAT}(U_p)=0$ and $\#\mathrm{SAT}(V_p)=p+1$.
Every two variables in $\{x_1,\dots,x_p,a\}$ share a source clause,
hence their contiguous scoped live intervals intersect pairwise.
The interval Helly property forces one event with at least $p+1$
simultaneously live source variables. An explicit closed stream attains
$p+1$, so the exact minimum is $w=p+1$ under \emph{any} original-clause
reordering and independent satisfying pad blocks. Any fixed-polynomial
padding length $N'\le N^C$ retains
$w/\log N'=\Omega(p/\log p)\to\infty$: PAD-FLOOR-SCOPED87 does
\emph{not} supply a general all-input sublog-width normalizer.
\textbf{Restricted-model firewall:} source-preserving clause replay
is not arbitrary SAT decision. One uniform paid canonical recognizer
decides both labeled inputs in $O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)$ elementary
bit work and $O(\log N)$ writable bits, then uses an exact (potentially
exponential) fallback on other inputs. High syntactic live width does
not force a large machine state or compulsory boundary-table enumeration.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} deterministic full local audit, 254
assertion-accounted checks, no failures, 22 matched instances;
independent GitHub mini reproducer, 180 checks, no failures.
Prior LSPV-3 SCOPED-BLOCK-REBUILDER92 gives exact pinned children only on
valid promised scoped streams; the original HEREDITARY-SCOPED10
executable is still unavailable. The FLMV matched-machine forbidden box
remains \textbf{KNOWN THEOREM}, but no favorable all-input
jointly charged achievable SAT pair or cofinal route is established.

\paragraph{REPLAY-ENTROPY86: compressed-state source replay versus one-bit SAT decision (20:06 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in a restricted indexed source-bit service, independent peer review pending.}
For the connected ordinary proper-3CNF parity-cycle family from BLOCK-CLOSED-ORACLE85,
with $q$ independent orientation bits, $n=3q$, $m=4q$ and
$N=(16q+2)\lceil\log_2(64(7q+1))\rceil$, even parity is SAT and
odd parity UNSAT, with matched source metadata.
An exact deterministic controller asked to \emph{replay all $q$ orientation bits
in order on each of $R$ rounds}, whose complete usable state at every
round boundary has at most $B$ bits (including working caches, control,
addresses and head position), must perform expected original-source
bit probes at least $R(q-B)_+$ for uniform orientations.
Even \emph{separately conditioned on either SAT or UNSAT}, it must perform
expected probes at least $R(q-1-B)_+$. Thus at least one input of
each label attains the latter total source-read floor.
The proof conditions on the current adaptive machine state and uses
a binary source-probe decision-tree/Kraft entropy bound on the remaining
source strings; the expected \emph{actual} reads are summed, not
hypothetical residual potentials.
One paid scan followed by caching $C$ orientation bits uses exactly
$q+(R-1)(q-C)$ addressed source reads,
with $C+O(\log(N+R))$ live control bits.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} the independent full Python audit checked
541 assertion-accounted tests with zero failures, including exhaustive
proper-CNF model counts for $q=3,4$, all 256 and 65,536 one-bit cache
maps, and both parity-label controls.
\textbf{STRICT SCOPE FIREWALL:} a one-bit SAT decider can instead
compute global parity after one charged scan in linear source work,
without any repeated full replay, all child bitstrings or model counts.
The source-read lower bound applies only to the typed complete replay
interface; it does not prove a post-acquisition deterministic SAT
time bound or a favorable successor in the jointly achievable
ARC/FLMV region. The original full HEREDITARY-SCOPED10 executable
remains unavailable for a necessity-of-replay audit.
The FLMV matched-model forbidden endpoint remains a KNOWN THEOREM;
the all-input charged favorable ARC edge and $P\ne NP$ remain \OPEN{}.

\paragraph{NESTED-VIRTUAL-TRANSCRIPT84: two-level exact indexed children, adaptive model-count firewall (18:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent review pending:}
For an ordinary proper-3CNF parent with \(M=8d\) clauses, two sequential cyclic selectors
\(s_1(j_1,u)=(j_1K_1+u)\bmod M\) and
\(s_2(j_1,j_2,i)=(j_1K_1+((j_2K_2+i)\bmod K_1))\bmod M\)
define exact self-contained ordinary \(K_2\)-clause children, including proper
headers and fresh fixed-width literal words. A uniform controller computes any
second-level bit with \(O(\log^2 N)\) elementary bit work and \(O(\log N)\)
counted writable bits, including addressing and read-only parent dereferences,
without materializing either level.
For the explicit \(n=6\) matched pair \(U_d,V_d\), all eight signed
core clauses at positions \(rd\) make \(U_d\) UNSAT, while replacing
the eighth by a duplicate makes \(V_d\) SAT with exactly seven models.
Every cyclic source window of length \(<d\), and every nested leaf
with \(K_1<d\), has the \emph{same} SAT label and exact model count under
both opposite-label parents. Even arbitrarily adaptive, source-blind
SAT/\(\#\)SAT-only window-query protocols therefore have identical transcripts
and cannot reconstruct the parent label. For odd \(\gcd(K_1,M)=1\),
the \(MK_1\) nested index pairs yield exactly \(1+8K_2\) distinct
UNSAT-parent leaf bitstrings and \(1+7K_2\) SAT-parent leaf bitstrings.
Source-aware canonical recognition instead decides both parent labels
in \(O(N)\) bit work and \(O(\log N)\) live writable bits; all
recognition, failed attempts and source reads are charged.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} standard-library full audit, deterministic seed
202610241836, 269,564 assertion-accounted checks and zero failures,
all 52,608 two-level leaf pairs per parent over \(d=16,32,64,128,256\),
independent six-variable enumeration, exact or sampled child bits, adaptive
30-query controls and Horn/affine/padded/random hostile families.
\textbf{CONDITIONAL ONLY:} a separately correct all-input source-coherent
gluing rule could use the virtual two-level solver term
\(t_2\le\max\{r_0,a(1+\lambda)+\lambda^2t\}\),
\(s_2\le\max\{p_0,\lambda^2s\}\); no such gluing rule is proved.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as general inferences:}
mandatory intermediate materialization, all-level distinct child contents,
or parent SAT reconstruction from isolated small-child SAT or exact counts.

\paragraph{PERIODIC-CHILD-COLLISION83: source-index versus literal-content orbits (17:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent review pending:}
For ordinary six-variable proper-3CNF parents with \(M=2^h\) repeated
records and a cyclic \(K\)-clause selector \(s(j,i)=(jK+i)\bmod M\),
choose odd \(K=\Theta(N^{1/2}/\log N)\),
\(p=2^{\lceil\log_2(8K)\rceil}\),
\(Q=\min\{M,\lfloor N^{3/5}\rfloor\}\).
An UNSAT source containing all eight signed clauses on a disjoint
core triple and its SAT sibling omitting one distinct core clause
have model counts \(0\) and \(7\), while \emph{every} indexed child
of either source has the same complete six-variable model count
\(49\) or \(56\), determined only by the source position.
The \(Q\) clause-index tuples are pairwise distinct but their ordinary
serialized child bitstrings occur in only \(1+8K\) or \(1+7K\)
distinct forms, respectively. Thus distinct index selections
do \emph{not} imply distinct child inputs when parent records repeat.
For these parents, two adjacent flat child arrays differ in at most
six literal-code fields, so \emph{changed-value} flat rewrites are
at most \(6(Q-1)\), rather than being forced to scale as \(QK\).
\textbf{Correction to CYCLIC-VIRTUAL-SELECTOR82:} its unqualified
claim of \(Q\) distinct children on every valid parent means
distinct \emph{index selections}; its flat-buffer
\(\Omega((Q-1)K)\) theorem remains valid with its stated premise
that \emph{all parent clause records are pairwise distinct}.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} deterministic exhaustive \(2^6\)-assignment
verification for every selected child at \(M=256,512,1024,4096\),
7098 child counts and 8 parent counts, zero failures;
at \(M=4096\), \(N=311334\), \(K=29\), \(Q=1976\), there
are only 233 and 204 distinct child encodings.
A charged \(O(N\operatorname{polylog} N)\)-bit-work,
\(O(\log N)\)-space recognizer directly decides either canonical
label, falling back to a total exact solver on other sources.
This is not an all-input polynomial SAT algorithm.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as inferences:}
child-input uniqueness from selector-index uniqueness, or parent
SAT from individual child model counts alone.
\textbf{OPEN:} all-input charged semantic reconstruction and any
favorable ARC/FLMV simultaneous time-space successor; P versus NP.

\paragraph{CYCLIC-VIRTUAL-SELECTOR82: logarithmic-state cyclic child bit service (16:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent review pending:}
For canonical proper-3CNF input length
$N=(4M+2)\lceil\log_2(64(n+M+1))\rceil$,
$M=2^h$, $n=M-3$, odd $K=\Theta(N^{1/2}/\log N)$ and
$Q=\min\{M,\lfloor N^{3/5}\rfloor\}$,
the $j$th ordered clause-deletion child uses indices
$s(j,i)=(jK+i)\bmod M$. The $Q$ distinct ordered
index selections define ordinary children of length
$L=\Theta(N^{1/2})$ bits; child serializations need not be
distinct when source clause records repeat. Adjacent selectors
are disjoint when $2K\le M$.
A fixed uniform $O(\log N)$-writable-bit controller supplies
each ordinary child bit using charged $\polylog N$ bit work
and read-only parent accesses. When all parent clause records are pairwise distinct, a flat
materialized same-address buffer needs at least $(Q-1)K$
changed literal-code fields across the \emph{same} schedule;
without that premise the bound need not hold. Virtual access
does not incur this materialization requirement.
The $N^{1/2}$ all-selector index-array capacity theorem
remains valid under its stronger full-service quantifiers.
\textbf{CONDITIONAL ONLY:} with a separately proved all-input
both-label gluing algorithm using additional time
$N^{r+o(1)}$ and space $N^{\sigma+o(1)}$, the virtual
service would allow
$t'\le\max\{1,r,3/5+t/2\}$,
$s'\le\max\{0,\sigma,s/2\}$ for one simultaneous
fixed-machine pair. No such SAT-correct gluing exists here.
The archived minimally-UNSAT $F_m$ and SAT $G_m$ have
identical all-YES labels on all $K<M$ deletion children,
so this is a hostile \emph{semantic} control.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 53,005 assertion-accounted checks,
302,812 complete child-bit comparisons, 39,324 selected
checks, zero failures. At $M=4096$, $Q=1976$, $K=31$,
the flat representation changes 183,675 word fields;
its virtual index counter flips 3,941 bits, not a
measurement of all elementary work.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} mandatory $QK$ materialization
writes or $\Theta(K\log M)$ descriptor storage for every
selector schedule, and status-only strict-child gluing.
\textbf{OPEN:} the all-input favorable ARC successor;

\paragraph{SELECTOR-CAPACITY-BIT-LEDGER81: full-child service versus one-bit decision (15:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, independent review pending:}
For a fixed proper-3CNF parent containing all eight sign patterns
on one core triple and $M-8$ disjoint positive fillers, every $K$-clause
deletion child with $r$ selected core clauses has exactly
$(8-r)7^{K-r}8^{M-8-K+r}$ models. The parent is UNSAT,
every $K<8$ child is SAT, and for $K\ge8$ exactly

A fixed stateless full-child-bit decoder for \emph{every} $K$-subset

selector bits, whereas a streaming source-aware child-\emph{label}
algorithm uses an eight-flag mask and $O(K\log M)$ bit work.
The selector-capacity inequality and array upper bound already
occurred in SELECTOR-VIRTUAL-CHILD62; the new matched-label
family and service-separation hostile control do not imply
arbitrary-decider SAT hardness.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 273719 audit checks, including 129030 exact

\paragraph{DUAL-PARITY-JOINT77: two parity checks create a rank-invisible, cheaply decidable compatibility hole (11:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT on the explicit canonical mixed-CNF family, independent
Role~5 review pending:} For $q\ge1$ and $e\in\{0,1\}^q$, let binary $B_e$
enforce $w_j=z_j\oplus e_j$, $(x\vee z_j)$ and $(y\vee\neg z_j)$
for every $j$, and encode both long affine checks
$x\oplus\bigoplus_j(z_j\oplus w_j)=0$ and
$y\oplus\bigoplus_j(z_j\oplus w_j)=0$ using separate functional
XOR3-prefix chains. The explicit full formula has
\[
n=6q,\quad m_2=4q+4,\quad k=16q-8,\quad M=20q-4,\quad
h_2^{\mathrm{raw}}=2q-1,\qquad
N=(80q-14)\lceil\log_2(64(26q-3))\rceil.
\]
The minimum raw strong syntactic 2-CNF backdoor is sharp: there are
$4q-2$ distinct ternary supports, each variable hits at most two,
and the $2q-1$ shared source operands $z_1,w_1,\ldots,z_q$ (excluding
$w_q$) hit every support. Every auxiliary is functional, the primal
graph is connected, and there are no unit clauses.
With $p=\bigoplus_je_j$, joint SAT holds iff $p=1$, with
$2^q$ witnesses if SAT and none otherwise; $B_e$ has $2^q+2$
models and each long check alone is compatible with $B_e$.
The joint syndrome image on $B_e$ is exactly
$\mathbb F_2^2\setminus\{(p,p)\}$: all individual parity marginals
and its affine hull are full, while one non-affine compatibility
point is absent.

A \emph{total uniform exact} policy pays for canonical grammar
recognition, complete template regeneration and verification, binary
SCC, reading all signed orientations, XOR cancellation and binary
compatibility, and then reconstructs one SAT witness or derives
an observed contradiction, with generic DPLL fallback off grammar.
The canonical trajectory changes
$(k,h_2)=(16q-8,2q-1)\to(0,0)$ in $O(q)$ word time
and $O(q)$ peak words. No true label is provided. The all-zero-$e$
UNSAT input requires $q=\Omega(N/\log N)$ independent addressed-word
reads for any exact deterministic decider because each sign flip
alone produces SAT; this is \emph{only} input acquisition.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 986 assertion-accounted check groups and
64 raw records, zero failures; matched SAT/UNSAT through $q=2048$,
small exhaustive counts, full target DPLL counts, generic fallback,
a three-syndrome pairwise projection firewall and complete restricted
fixed-backdoor comparisons. At $q=6$ the nonpruning enumerator
makes 2048 leaf checks; the canonical adaptive policy does linear
work. At $q=1$, recognition overhead makes adaptation more expensive.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} separate feasibility or full affine
syndrome hull proves joint parity feasibility, or a linear initial
backdoor forces exponential work against every adaptive SAT algorithm.
The single-global-parity polynomial theorem from SINGLE-PARITY-SCC76
remains valid. TWO-EASY-INTERSECTION61 already supplies the
unrestricted affine/2-CNF NP-complete endpoint; no NP-completeness
for \emph{exactly two} global checks is claimed.
\textbf{OPEN:} an independently measured post-acquisition
superpolynomial work obstruction covering every total uniform
deterministic exact decider; hence P versus NP.
Verified role report, runnable mini-reproducer, raw summary, bibliography
and five-role handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-24_1136_LSPV-4_DUAL_PARITY_JOINT77.tex}.



\paragraph{SINGLE-PARITY-SCC76: one global parity plus arbitrary 2-CNF remains polynomial (10:46 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT within the declared mixed 2-CNF--one-XOR model; independent Role~5 review pending:}
For any explicitly encoded satisfiable 2-CNF $B$ and one global
linear check $\bigoplus_{i\in S}x_i=b$, group variables by complementary
signed implication-SCC pairs $V_g$ and define their parity weights
$\omega_g=|S\cap V_g|\bmod2$. Each SCC pair's truth orientation is either
forced or free, recognizable by paid unit-augmented SCC calls.
The image of the parity check on all $B$-witnesses is a singleton
if every free $V_g$ has $\omega_g=0$ and otherwise is $\{0,1\}$.
Indeed, any two $B$-witnesses are joined by a sequence of satisfying
assignments changing one SCC pair at a time, using a topological
order on the changing quotient blocks. This yields a uniform exact
SAT/UNSAT decider and SAT witness constructor in conservative
$O(n^2(n+m))$ logical-word time and $O(n+m)$ live words,
charging discovery, all failed tests, switching and source replay.
The affine hull of a bijunctive relation is therefore described by
its forced literals and signed within-SCC-block equality/disequality
relations. The theorem does \emph{not} extend by separately testing
multiple simultaneous global equations.

For even $q=2t\ge4$, a fully explicit source-encoded family
$P_{q,c,b}$ couples $t$ binary XOR pairs
$x_{2j+1}\oplus x_{2j+2}=c_j$ with a functional prefix-XOR
chain and final XOR output $b$. Its exact ledger is
\[
n=2q-2,\quad m_2=q+2,\quad k=4q-8,\quad M=5q-6,\quad
N=(20q-22)\lceil\log_2(64(7q-7))\rceil.
\]
There are $q$ original source variables, $q-2$ functional
auxiliaries, and no free auxiliaries.
The formula is SAT iff $b=\bigoplus_jc_j$; its SAT model count is
$2^{q/2}$, otherwise zero. Its minimum raw strong syntactic
2-CNF backdoor is $(q-2)/2$. Its \emph{single connected} affine
prefix component has nullity $q-1$ and excess $K=q-2$.
Nevertheless a charged adaptive solver recognizes the source,
eliminates the functional prefix variables, uses SCC to discover
even-weight free binary blocks, switches to parity cancellation
and verifies a witness or algebraic contradiction in $O(q)$
word operations and $O(q)$ live words. The designated all-zero-$c$,
$b=1$ UNSAT input has $q/2$ private orientation fields, each
single flip producing SAT, proving only
$\Omega(N/\log N)$ addressed-word acquisition on that input,
tight for the canonical scanner.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} six independent stdlib audit suites, seed
202610240136, 283,242 assertion-accounted checks and zero failures.
They include exhaustive three-/four-variable bijunctive relations
(166 and 4170), 5010 randomized parity masks, 2062 genuine
witness-switch cases, matched SAT/UNSAT controls through $q=4096$,
and restricted nonpruning-policy comparisons. These finite tests
do not prove asymptotic lower bounds.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as an inference:}
large initial excess nullity, one connected affine component,
many ternaries, or a large raw backdoor forces exponential work
from \emph{every} adaptive exact SAT decider. The older
$2^K\operatorname{poly}(N)$ and forest-joining results remain
correct upper bounds; both may be pessimistic.
\textbf{OPEN:} universal superpolynomial post-acquisition lower bound
for any fixed NP-complete SAT language, hence $P$ versus $NP$.

\section{LSPV-4 (independent ladder falsification)}

\paragraph{Current reconciled ladder: AFFINE-BIJUNCTIVE-GATE75 (09:58 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT structural classification; independent Role~5
review pending.}  For any consistent projected affine relation
$R=\{c+At\}$ on a 2-CNF boundary, with $\operatorname{rank}(A)=d$ and
$s$ \emph{distinct nonzero coordinate covectors} (rows of $A$),
$R$ is equivalent to a 2-CNF iff $s=d$. When $s=d$ a charged
Gaussian-to-binary conversion emits at most $2|X|$ unit/equality
clauses; when $s>d$, the unary/pairwise closure has $2^s$ possible
tuples versus $2^d$ in $R$, precluding any exact 2-CNF on the same
boundary. This is a representation criterion, not a time lower bound.
The exact uniform algorithm first computes paid binary SCC equivalences,
substitutes them into its affine subsystem, projects via GF(2), and
switches to SCC when the updated excess vanishes; complete exact
fallback remains available.

For even $q\ge4$, use cyclic affine equations
$y_{i-1}\oplus y_i\oplus x_i=\beta_i$ and binary links
$x_i\leftrightarrow x_{i+1}$ for $i<q-1$. Encode each XOR3
with four proper ternaries. The resulting $H_{q,\beta}$ has
\[
n=2q,\quad m_2=2q-2,\quad k=4q,\quad M=6q-2,\quad
N=(24q-6)\lceil\log_2(64(8q-1))\rceil.
\]
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT:}
$H_{q,\beta}$ is SAT iff $\bigoplus_i\beta_i=0$; its model count
is exactly four or zero. Initially the raw strong syntactic 2-CNF
backdoor is $q/2$, and the existentially projected parity relation
has $d=q-1,s=q,\epsilon=s-d=1$ with minimum strong
\emph{semantic} 2-CNF variable-conditioning backdoor $q-2$.
A charged SCC discovers every $x_i=t$, after which the affine
relation becomes TRUE or FALSE by parity cancellation.
A uniform exact source-recognizing policy handles both labels in
$O(q)$ word time and $O(q)$ peak words, without initial units or
label advice. No mandatory enumeration follows.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted input acquisition:} flipping any one
$\beta_i$ changes four private signed target literal words and
reverses the SAT label on either a SAT or UNSAT member. Thus every
deterministic exact addressed-word decider reads $q=
\Omega(N/\log N)$ word groups on those designated sources,
matched by an $O(q)$ recognition scan. Deleting one cross-block
equality link at position $r$ produces
$(r\bmod 2)t_L\oplus((q-r)\bmod2)t_R=\bigoplus_i\beta_i$;
the new quotient can change UNSAT to SAT even though both
individual subsystems were already SAT.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 4,736 assertion-accounted tests,
zero failures; complete small model counts and $q=2560$
SAT/UNSAT scaling, one-link controls, and a restricted fixed-rule
enumerator. At $q=14$, that enumerator checks 4,096 leaves
(778,240 abstract events), versus adaptive 578 SAT and 510
UNSAT events. This is not a lower bound on arbitrary DPLL/CDCL.
\textbf{KNOWN NP-complete endpoint:} AFFINE-NULLITY-BOUNDARY73's
graph-3-colorability-to-mixed-XOR3/NAND reduction remains unchanged.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} inferring mandatory superlinear work
from initial $k$, a fixed backdoor, affine covector excess or
one chosen representation's branching depth.
\textbf{OPEN:} all-decider post-acquisition superpolynomial cost.
P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.
