\paragraph{Current reconciled ladder: LOCAL-AFFINE-JOIN74 (09:08 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted; independent Role~5 review pending.}
For $q$ private ternary-XOR gates
$x_i\oplus y_i\oplus p_i=b_i$ coupled to binaries
$(x_i\vee y_i)$ and $(\neg x_i\vee p_{i+1})$,
the exact existential projection is
\[
\exists x_i,y_i\ 
[(x_i\vee y_i)\land(\neg x_i\vee p_{i+1})
 \land(x_i\oplus y_i\oplus p_i=b_i)]
\iff p_{i+1}\vee(p_i\oplus b_i).
\]
With binary anchors $p_0=1,p_q=0$, the canonical
family $G_{q,b}$ is UNSAT precisely for $b=1^q$.
Exact ledger:
$n=3q+3$, $m_2=2q+4$, $k=4q$,
$M=6q+4$,
$N=(24q+18)\lceil\log_2(64(9q+8))\rceil$.
Raw minimum strong syntactic 2CNF backdoor is $q$
yet paid local projection, SCC and optional witness
replay decide both labels in $O(q)$ word time with
$O(q)$ peak words; the uniform decider falls back to
exact DPLL outside its recognized grammar.
The $q$ independent orientation groups give a
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted}
$q=\Omega(N/\log N)$ deterministic addressed-word
input-probe lower bound on $G_{q,1^q}$, matched by a
linear source scanner, not a superpolynomial time theorem.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT one-link counterexample.}
Adding $x_i\leftrightarrow x_{i+1}$ for every adjacent
gate gives linked $H_{q,b}$ with
$n=3q+3$, $m_2=4q+2$, $k=4q$, $M=8q+2$,
and $N=(32q+10)\lceil\log_2(64(11q+6))\rceil$.
Its SAT label is exactly $b_0=0$.
For $q=2,b=(1,0)$, independent local projections
predict SAT but the linked formula is UNSAT,
because a shared hidden variable invalidates
factorization. SCC discovery identifies one
equivalent switch; branching on it and then
using SCC decides $H$ in $O(q)$ word time,
with charged recognition, failed branches and
verification. All but the first orientation-sign
sensitivity vanish when the $x_i$ are linked.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: 3,103 named assertion
checks, zero failures; complete $q\le7$ baseline
orientation audit, $q\le8$ linked label audit,
and SAT/UNSAT scales to $q=2048$ and $512$.
A deliberately nonpruning raw-backdoor policy
visits $2^{12}=4096$ leaves at $q=12$, whereas
the adaptive projector uses 192 local truth
tests and one SCC solve. That is a
\emph{restricted-policy} comparison only.
The easy nullity-two construction refines
AFFINE-NULLITY-BOUNDARY73 without changing
its NP-complete endpoint.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}: independent
projection after hidden-variable coupling and
raw $k$, nullity or backdoor as a mandatory
all-algorithm continuation cost.
\textbf{OPEN}: a model-matched post-acquisition
superpolynomial lower bound for every uniform
exact state-adaptive SAT decider.
P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.

\paragraph{Previous reconciled ladder: AFFINE-NULLITY-BOUNDARY73 (08:52 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the explicit affine-component and uniform-reduction models; independent Role~5 peer review pending:}
If $B$ is arbitrary 2-CNF and $E$ a GF(2) system whose
variable-incidence components each have affine nullity at most one,
charged component discovery and Gaussian elimination express each
source variable as a constant or $t_i\oplus a_i$ for one component
bit $t_i$. Arbitrary $B$ then projects \emph{exactly} to 2-CNF,
solvable by SCC in polynomial time, even with total nullity $\Theta(n)$
and dense cross-component binary coupling.
The sharp next rung has \emph{local nullity two} and is
\textbf{KNOWN NP-complete} by an explicit parsimonious
GRAPH 3-COLORABILITY reduction: one odd XOR3 component on each
vertex, three negative NAND clauses within the vertex, and three
negative NAND clauses on every graph edge. All binary clauses are Horn
and all affine components are disjoint. For a graph with $v$ vertices
and $e$ edges the exact CNF ledger is
\[
 n=3v,\quad m_2=3v+3e,\quad k=4v,\quad M=7v+3e,
 \quad N=(28v+12e+2)\lceil\log_2(64(10v+3e+1))\rceil.
\]
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted input-acquisition and adaptive
hostile control:} for an odd $q$-cycle with one binary anchor pair per
vertex, $n=4q,m_2=8q,k=4q,M=12q$ and
$N=(48q+2)\lceil\log_2(64(16q+1))\rceil$. The all-zero anchor-sign
member is UNSAT; every single anchor-pair flip gives SAT with exactly
$2^{q+1}$ witnesses. Therefore each total exact addressed-word
decider needs $q=\Omega(N/\log N)$ sign-word probes on the
all-zero member. Yet a label-blind charged policy discovers all
$q$ implied anchor literals, shrinks the live ternary count from
$4q$ to zero and decides by SCC or direct certified conflict in
$O(q)$ source words and peak logical words. With $h$ anchors missing,
one selected variable per surviving ternary support yields a
$2^h\operatorname{poly}(N)$ upper bound, not a lower bound. The policy
permits different state-dependent branches and exact DPLL fallback
outside the recognized easy state.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 1,570 assertion-accounted checks, zero
failures; 64 four-vertex graph controls, 150 Gaussian quotient controls,
2-SAT chains, Horn/affine/padding controls, SAT/UNSAT cycle scales
through $q=1023$ and dynamic uncovered-support tests. At $q=1023$,
$N=982120$ bits and charged abstract-event counts were
$120718$ UNSAT and $149367$ SAT. A deliberately nonpruning
fixed-backdoor comparator visits $2^q$ leaves but supplies no
all-decider lower bound.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} initial local nullity two, $k=4q$ or a
raw strong 2-CNF backdoor $q$ as an unavoidable
$2^{\Omega(q)}$ running-time resource.
\textbf{OPEN:} universal post-acquisition superpolynomial work for all
uniform deterministic adaptive SAT deciders. $P$ versus $NP$ remains
\OPEN{}.

\paragraph{Prior reconciled ladder: HETEROGENEOUS-GATE72 (07:45 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only for a specified source family and explicit
adaptive policy; independent Role~5 verification pending.}
For even $q\ge4$, a canonical guarded CNF combines $q$ 3-XOR equations
with the original $q$-field binary implication chain. Exact parameters are
\[
n=11q+9,\quad m_2=13q+5,\quad k=9q+4,\quad M=22q+9,
\quad N=(88q+38)\lceil\log_2(64(33q+19))\rceil.
\]
The target is UNSAT exactly when the XOR syndrome is odd \emph{and}
$b=1^q$. Its raw strong syntactic 2CNF backdoor satisfies $h_2\ge q/2$,
yet a paid binary SCC identifies $5q+5$ equivalent guarded switches
without shortening any ternary or producing a unit. Branching on the
discovered representative produces \emph{different} tractable bases:
at $x=0$ there remain $4q$ genuine ternary clauses encoding an affine
parity system, solved with charged GF(2) methods; at $x=1$ all XOR
gates vanish and the residual is exactly $F_b$, solved by 2SAT SCC.
A label-blind, source-recognizing, total exact policy pays all
recognition, rule selection, switching, failed attempts, optional
witness reconstruction and fallback; it runs in $O(q)$ word time and
$O(q)$ peak logical words on this family.
On the designated UNSAT input, $2q$ disjoint signed-coordinate groups
must be read by every total deterministic exact addressed-word
decider, giving only $\Omega(N/\log N)$ input acquisition, matched by
a direct source scan. No post-acquisition superlinear bound follows.
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM (Schaefer):} Unrestricted conjunctions of positive
binary OR and even ternary XOR are NP-complete, even though either
relation alone is polynomial-time tractable. This is a boundary
between a recognizable guarded disjunction and general mixed
constraints, not a separation of P and NP.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 25,195 assertion-accounted checks,
zero failures, and 21 additional large scaling cases through
$q=1024$. The deliberately non-pruning fixed backdoor enumerator
checks $2^{q+1}$ leaves, but this is strictly a
\emph{restricted-policy} comparison.
\textbf{OPEN:} a structural measure independent of observed runtime,
an all-decider post-acquisition work inequality and coverage of every
uniform deterministic exact policy. $P$ versus $NP$ remains \OPEN{}.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only for the explicit growing mixed-width family and

Two disjoint $q$-orientation 2-SAT chains are linked by one original

n=8q+31,\quad m_2=12q+48,\quad k=2q+8,\quad M=14q+56,\qquad
N=(56q+226)\left\lceil\log_2(64(22q+88))\right\rceil.
\]
The target is UNSAT exactly when both orientation words are $1^q$.
Its binary-only backbone is SAT and implies no literal, and there
are no initial units.  Every genuine ternary has a physically disjoint
variable triple and is deletion-critical in the all-one UNSAT input,
so the minimum raw strong syntactic 2-CNF backdoor is $h_0=k$.
Paid implication-SCC equivalences shorten \emph{zero} ternaries,
yet identify a common representative $x$ in every ternary,
reducing the quotient backdoor to $h_1=1$ while $k_1=k$.
A source-verified one-variable split, paid binary implication closure
and at most two SCC solves reduce all ternaries to width two on
both labels. The exact parameter-vector trajectory is
\[
(k,h_0)=(2q+8,2q+8)\longrightarrow (k_1,h_1)=(2q+8,1)
\longrightarrow k_2=0.
\]
The label-blind, total exact policy includes DPLL fallback for
unsupported inputs and charges acquisition, SCC, quotient, pivot
recognition, switching, a possibly failed branch, witness replay,
and $O(n+M)$ peak logical words; on this family its time is $O(n+M)$.
On the all-one UNSAT input, every exact deterministic addressed-word
decider reads all $2q$ independently sensitive sign fields,
giving only $\Omega(N/\log N)$ word-probe acquisition. A direct
source-aware scanner matches this order, so no universal computational
amplification follows.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} three bounded standard-library suites,

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT within the declared reduction calculus, independent
Role~5 review pending:}
A source-retaining cyclic-copy Horn priority selector encodes any
proper 3-CNF with $n_0$ variables, $m$ clauses, and $\nu$ active source
variables as $n=n_0+12m$ variables, $m_2=16m+\nu$ Horn binaries and
$k=m$ source-disjoint positive ternaries. Every satisfying original
assignment has exactly one auxiliary extension; every physical variable
occurs at most three times. The output has mixed width two and three,
\emph{not} proper exactly-three width. Every proper three-CNF whose
variables occur at most three times is SAT by Hall matching, so such a
properization retaining the cap cannot cover UNSAT inputs. This is
a classical occurrence-cycle mechanism specialized to our functional
Horn selector, not a universal decision lower bound. Source:
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-23_2008_LSPV-4_FUNCTIONAL_OCCURRENCE68.tex}.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, adaptive switched-ladder hostile control:}
For each $q\ge1$ and orientation string $b\in\{0,1\}^q$, a mixed
binary/ternary coupled chain $H_{q,b}$ has
\[
n=3q+5,\quad m_2=2q+5,\quad k=4q+4,\quad M=6q+9,
\quad N=(24q+38)\left\lceil\log_2(64(9q+15))\right\rceil.
\]
Its $(q+1)$ pairwise-disjoint parity-quartet supports force the minimum
\emph{raw syntactic} strong 2-CNF backdoor
$h=q+1$, yet a single locally certified binary unit seed propagates
through a $p$-implication chain to fix one pivot in every quartet:
the independent analysis parameter $g_B=1$, not supplied to the solver.
After charged binary-pair discovery, graph propagation and clause
restriction, the live ternary count becomes zero. A total exact
state-dependent solver uses either an immediate certified conflict
on the all-one UNSAT input, SCC on the SAT binary residual, or complete
DPLL fallback on arbitrary other CNFs. Both original labels occur:
\[
\boxed{H_{q,b}\in\mathrm{UNSAT}\iff b=1^q.}
\]
The raw binary-only subformula is SAT on \emph{both} labels.
The original $q$-orientation chain and $H_{q,b}$ have equal model
counts: $p_i=1,y_i=\neg z_i$ are unique functional witnesses.
The hybrid fully charges source acquisition, pairing, policy selection,
propagation, SCC or fallback, reconstruction, verification and live
space. It runs in $O(q)$ addressed-word operations and $O(q)$
peak words on the explicit family; source-template direct decision
also runs in $O(q)$. On the all-one UNSAT member every exact
deterministic decider must inspect each of $q$ disjoint
literal-sign fields, only $\Omega(N/\log N)$ word
\emph{acquisition}, with no superlinear amplification.

\paragraph{PALETTE-SOURCE-CYCLE56: direct all-decider acquisition inside proper 3-CNF.}
For $q\ge3$, a palette triangle and $q$ pairs of opposite two-color
vertices with one oriented edge per pair yield a graph that is
3-colorable exactly when $\bigoplus_i\sigma_i=0$; consistent
instances have exactly $12$ colorings. Its mixed-width one-hot CNF has
$n=6q+9$, $k=2q+3$ genuine ternaries,
$m_2=18q+18$ binaries, and $m=20q+21$ total clauses,
with $N=(80q+86)w$ encoded bits in the declared fixed record model.
This source is \emph{not} proper exactly-three-clause CNF.
Padding each binary clause by its own fresh free variable and two
ternary copies yields the proper source with
$n'=24q+27$, $k'=m'=38q+39$, $m_2'=0$ and
$N'=(152q+158)w'$ encoded bits. The mixed formula changes three
source literal cells per orientation flip; the proper padded formula
changes exactly six distinct literal cells per flip. Both have opposite
SAT labels after a one-bit change. Thus on \emph{both} labels,
\emph{every} exact deterministic word-access decider for unrestricted
proper exactly-three-clause SAT reads at least $q$ addressed input
words on the padded family. This is only
$\Omega(N'/\log N')$ acquisition; a charged $O(q)$
paired-padding recognizer and parity scan matches it.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, restricted structural upper bound:}
After fixing a palette triangle, if $t$ other vertices are not
adjacent to its base vertex, enumerating their $3^t$ colors leaves
an affine two-color constraint graph, decided by parity propagation
in $O(V+E)$ time per attempt. Thus total time is
$O(3^t(V+E))$ and peak space $O(V+E)$ words; dense chords
among palette-pinned nodes do not force exponential work.
A connected palette extension of arbitrary graph 3-colorability
gives the KNOWN NP-complete endpoint, not an amplification theorem.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, local transport failure:}
The baseline $q$-orientation 2-SAT UNSAT predicate is
$\bigwedge_i b_i$, whereas the palette cycle UNSAT predicate is
$\bigoplus_i\sigma_i$. A coordinatewise mapping
$\sigma_i=g_i(b_i)$ cannot preserve labels for $q\ge2$ because
XOR of unary Boolean functions is affine and AND is not.
A global deciding reduction pays $\Theta(q)$ original source
reads and consumes the known input-order lower bound.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} three independent Python auditors completed
35,753 counted checks with zero failures, including 504 original mixed
graph orientation strings (252 SAT and 252 UNSAT), 3,576
three-word mixed flips, 3,576 six-word proper padded flips,
504 proper padded SAT witness replays, 744 additional chord trials,
and 5,456 coordinatewise-map falsifiers. Corrected verified standalone
report: \path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-23_1845_LSPV-4_PALETTE_SOURCE_CYCLE56.tex}.
The earlier QUADRATIC-SOURCE-LADDER55 remains an independent
quadratic-field source control. Finite tests are not asymptotic proofs.

\paragraph{SHARED-SWITCH-MINIMAL-UNSAT57: clause-minimal UNSAT but one-variable backdoor.}
For every $q\ge3$, encode a signed XOR cycle on
$x_0,\ldots,x_{q-1}$ by lifting each of its $2q$ binary
clauses with a common positive literal $s$; add four
proper ternaries
$(\neg s\vee t\vee y_1)$,
$(\neg s\vee t\vee\neg y_1)$,
$(\neg s\vee\neg t\vee y_2)$,
$(\neg s\vee\neg t\vee\neg y_2)$.
Every variable has both polarities, and there are no initial
units, pure literals, or non-genuine clauses.
The source has $n=q+4$, $k=m=2q+4$ width-three clauses
and is SAT exactly when $\bigoplus_i\sigma_i=0$,
with 16 satisfying assignments on SAT and zero on UNSAT.
On every odd-parity UNSAT member, \emph{every single clause is
essential}: deleting one cycle clause gives exactly eight
models, and deleting any one of the four forcing clauses
gives exactly $2^{q+1}$ models.
Nevertheless $\{s\}$ is a minimum strong 2-CNF backdoor,
so a checked source recognizer and SCC/parity continuation
decide both labels in $O(q)$ word operations.
Every odd-parity member has an ordinary resolution
refutation constructed in exactly $4q+2$ inferences.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, encoding-scoped:}
With two header words and four $w$-bit words per clause,
$w=\lceil\log_2(64(3q+9))\rceil$ and
$N=(8q+18)w$.
Flipping one independent orientation changes the SAT label
and precisely two disjoint literal-sign word locations.
Therefore every exact deterministic proper-three-CNF decider
must read at least $q=\Omega(N/\log N)$ addressed input words
on every member in this specific model.
A linear source scan matches the bound.
This is input-acquisition order, not an unrestricted
superlinear or exponential SAT lower bound.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:}
The full independent auditor checked 254 original 2-SAT
chain inputs, 56 source formulas, 720 one-clause deletions,
248 one-bit source flips, 132 SCC restrictions, and
33 ordinary resolution certificates through $q=256$
(2,546 checked derived inferences), with zero failures.
Report, compact reproducer, raw summary and cross-role handoff
have been independently read back from
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-23_1941_LSPV-4_SHARED_SWITCH_MINIMAL_UNSAT57.tex}
and adjacent same-prefix artifacts.
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:}
clause-essential ternary count or large syntactic residual as
an unrestricted SAT lower bound; a paid strong 2-CNF
backdoor can remain one even for minimally UNSAT
proper-three-CNF families with arbitrarily many clauses.

\textbf{OPEN:} a machine-universal superpolynomial
acquisition/compression/continuation lower bound for every total
uniform deterministic exact decider of one fixed NP-complete SAT
language. $P$ versus $NP$ remains \OPEN{}.

\paragraph{CONNECTED-CRITICAL-BACKDOOR58: connected critical parity with linear minimum backdoor.}
Let $G_t$ be a $t$-gonal prism, with $2t$ vertices and $3t$
edges, and form proper 3-CNF from the four exclusion clauses for
each vertex equation $\bigoplus_{e\ni v}x_e=b_v$.
The resulting \emph{connected} family $P_{t,b}$ has
$n=3t$, $k=m=8t$, and
$N=(32t+2)\lceil\log_2(64(11t+1))\rceil$ bits.
It is SAT iff $\bigoplus_vb_v=0$, with exactly $2^{t+1}$
models if SAT. On the UNSAT label every single-clause deletion
makes SAT with exactly $2^{t-1}$ models.
The minimum strong \emph{syntactic} 2-CNF backdoor is the prism
edge-cover number $h=t=n/3=k/8$, despite an $O(t)$
source-aware parity recognizer/decider and reconstruction.
Flipping any one of the $2t$ independent vertex RHS fields reverses
SAT/UNSAT and changes four disjoint addressed source-literal cells.
Hence every exact deterministic decider for the full proper-3CNF
encoding reads at least $2t=\Omega(N/\log N)$ source words on
\emph{both} labels, matched by an $O(t)$ word source scan.
These are \textbf{PROVED/EXACT, encoding-scoped} statements and
counterexamples to any universal per-instance continuation lower
bound derived solely from connectivity, clause minimality, or
linear minimum syntactic strong backdoor. A separate affine-plus-$p$
non-affine-ternary clause model admits an exact
$O(3^p\operatorname{poly}(N))$ time algorithm by choosing one
true literal per clause and Gaussian-checking each choice. This is
an \emph{upper} bound only.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} standard-library audit, seed 2026102358:
10,828 assertion groups, 2,088 deletion witnesses,
1,044 sign flips, 60 source remappings, zero failures, with
$t\le128$. Verified role report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-23_2006_LSPV-4_CONNECTED_CRITICAL_BACKDOOR58.tex};
peer handoff, code reproducer and raw summary share its prefix.
\textbf{OPEN:} all-decider superpolynomial post-acquisition
compression/continuation and $P\ne NP$.


\paragraph{AFFINE-MATCHING59: minimal positive-ternary NP-complete endpoint and semantic bypass (20:45 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted:} Source proper 3-SAT with $n_0$ variables and
$k$ clauses maps parsimoniously to a canonical disjoint XOR-one matching
backbone with $n=2n_0$, $m_2=2n_0$, and $k$ all-positive genuine ternaries.
The language is NP-complete by a bijective, charged linear-record reduction,
even though affine matching and positive ternaries separately are easy.
An exact four-ternary positive guard forces one padding member false and
locally lifts the $q$-field implication chain with
$n=2q+12$, $m_2=2q+12$, $k=q+8$, and $N=(12q+82)w$.
On the all-one UNSAT source, flipping one orientation changes exactly one
disjoint source word and the SAT label, so every deterministic exact
full-language word-access decider reads $\ge q=\Omega(N/\log N)$ input
words. A charged $O(q)$ recognizer and scan match the bound.
The source-aware bipolar-pair kernel leaves only $p$ underlying variables
that appear with both polarities, yielding an $O(2^p\operatorname{poly}(N))$
\emph{upper} bound despite absence of syntactic pure literals.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} seed 2026102359; 6,953 assertion checks, zero
failures; matched SAT/UNSAT, free padding, XOR, 2-SAT, independent components,

\paragraph{PROJECTION-BLOWUP59: sharp no-auxiliary 2-CNF boundary, no SAT lower bound.}
A source 2-CNF on disjoint $A=\{a_i\}_{i=1}^p$, $B=\{b_j\}_{j=1}^p$
and one mediator $u$,
\[
M_p=\bigwedge_i(\neg a_i\vee u)
\wedge\bigwedge_j(\neg u\vee\neg b_j),
\]
has $2p$ clauses; its exact existential projection $\exists u\,M_p$
onto $A\cup B$ is $\bigwedge_{i,j}(\neg a_i\vee\neg b_j)$.
Any logically equivalent CNF on these $2p$ variables without
auxiliaries, regardless of clause width, requires at least $p^2$
clauses: each forbidden assignment with only $a_i=b_j=1$
needs its own clause. This proves the quadratic output order
of MAIN88's 2-CNF projection upper bound is tight under an
explicit, auxiliary-free clause-record contract.
A paired genuine-ternary extension without initial unit or pure
literals has $n=10p+9$, $k=2p+2$, $m=12p+10$ and
$N=(48p+42)\lceil\log_2(64(n+m+1))\rceil$ bits.
The single-sign matched labels are SAT/UNSAT, with respectively
$64\cdot7^{p-1}3^p$ and zero assignments.
The full binary-backbone boundary projection retains the $p^2$
lower bound. Nevertheless keeping $u$ gives a linear-size
representation, and a checked source-aware parser decides either
label in $O(p)$ word time. Thus quadratic projected-CNF
\emph{output} is \textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted}, while any
translation into all-decider SAT running time is
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}. The separate stdlib audit checked
10,872 assertions across $p=1,\ldots,6$ with zero failures:
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}. Peer verification remains pending.
This does not supersede Role~3's stronger nonlinear projection
lower bound or assert a new $P\ne NP$ implication.
Verified report and cross-role code/results:
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-23_1234_LSPV-4_PROJECTION_BLOWUP59.tex}
and adjacent \path{_HANDOFF.md}, \path{_RAW.json},
\path{_MINI.py}. The all-decider continuation theorem remains
\textbf{OPEN}.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT (HORN-SELECTOR-DEGREE60 and HALL-DEFICIENCY-TRANSPORT62, independent peer review pending):}
For proper exact-three source $\Phi$ on $n_0$ variables and $m$
clauses, the canonical disjoint-selector Horn lift
$H(\Phi)=\bigwedge_j[(s_{j1}\vee s_{j2}\vee s_{j3})
\wedge\bigwedge_{a=1}^{3}(\neg s_{ja}\vee\ell_{ja})]$
is SAT iff $\Phi$ is SAT. It has $n=n_0+3m$ variables,
$3m$ Horn binary clauses, $k=m$ disjoint positive ternaries, and
$N=(16m+2)\lceil\log_2(64(n_0+7m+1))\rceil$ encoded bits.
For source assignment $\alpha$, the exact selector-extension count is
$\prod_j(2^{t_j(\alpha)}-1)$. Strong raw syntactic 2-CNF and Horn
backdoor minima are $m$ and $2m$.

Define maximum Hall deficiency
$\delta^*(F)=\max_{Q\subseteq F}(|Q|-|\operatorname{var}(Q)|)$;
by Hall, $\delta^*(F)=|F|-\nu(F)$ for maximum clause-variable
matching size $\nu(F)$. The new gadget-specific identity
\[
\boxed{\delta^*(H(\Phi))=\delta^*(\Phi),\qquad
\nu(H(\Phi))=3m+\nu(\Phi)}
\]
is \textbf{PROVED/EXACT} by counting an arbitrary target clause
subset, with all source-variable sharing preserved. If each source
variable occurs at most four times and exactly $h$ attain four
occurrences, $3|Q|\le3|\operatorname{var}(Q)|+h$ for every source
clause subset $Q$, so
$\delta^*(\Phi)\le\lfloor h/3\rfloor$. Szeider's 2004
\textbf{KNOWN THEOREM} gives a uniform
$2^{\lfloor h/3\rfloor}\operatorname{poly}(N)$ SAT decision
upper bound. In particular $h\le2$ guarantees SAT by matching,
and $h=O(\log N)$ is polynomial time.
The degree-four endpoint remains \textbf{KNOWN NP-complete} by
Tovey and D\"ocker. No lower bound is inferred from the FPT
upper bound or from the linear syntactic backdoors.

The connected signed exact-$(2,2)$ prism family $G_q$ from
MAIN101 has $n=6q,m=8q,h=6q$,
$\delta^*(G_q)=2q=h/3$, yet has an $O(q)$ source-aware
recognizer and SAT witness. Hence linear maximum deficiency
as a \emph{necessary} exponential-work measure is
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}. The stdlib audit recorded
11,924 assertions, zero failures, over 1,100 generated
bounded-occurrence sources, MU16 UNSAT, the tight signed
four-clause SAT example and connected prisms:
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}. Role~5 review remains pending.
Verified source:
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-24_0012_LSPV-4_HALL_DEFICIENCY_TRANSPORT62.tex};
adjacent \path{_HANDOFF.md}, \path{_MINI.py},
and \path{_RAW.json} preserve reproduction and transport accounting.
The source-input query lower bound remains input-order only.
The universal charged compression-versus-continuation
implication and $P\ne NP$ remain \textbf{OPEN}.


\paragraph{REGULAR-DEFICIENCY63: universal exact signed-$(2,2)$ Hall profile (24 October 2026).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, pending independent Role~5 audit:}
For \emph{every} properly encoded signed exact-$(2,2)$ 3-CNF with
$n=3t$ original variables and $m=4t$ genuine ternary clauses, not
just the connected prism control, the maximum clause-variable
incidence matching saturates all $n$ variables and
\[
  \boxed{\delta^*(F)=m-\nu(F)=m-n=t=n/3=m/4.}
\]
The \emph{unique} clause subset attaining maximum Hall deficiency
is the entire source $F$.  Every single-clause deletion lowers
maximum deficiency by exactly one,
$\delta^*(F\setminus\{C\})=t-1$ for all $C\in F$.
The exact identity
$3(|Q|-|V(Q)|)=h_Q-s_Q-\beta(Q)$ for every clause subset $Q$
of a max-degree-four proper 3-CNF separates degree-four
occurrence excess $h_Q$, degree slack $s_Q$ and outside incidence
boundary $\beta(Q)$.  For exact signed-$(2,2)$ sources,
$3\delta(Q)=|V(Q)|-\beta(Q)$.
All these quantities are \emph{unsigned}; within the full
NP-complete exact-$(2,2)$ class, maximum deficiency depends
only on the input counts and cannot classify SAT versus UNSAT
or force computation on each instance.  The earlier prism
saturation was structurally generic, not a special obstruction.
For the canonical Horn-selector lift $H(F)$,
$\delta^*(H(F))=t$ and the entire Horn target remains the unique
maximum-deficiency clause subset; the target has $5n$ variables,
$4m$ total clauses and $m$ genuine positive ternaries.
Full construction, grammar recognition, source reads, inverse
and witness lifting/replay are polynomial and explicitly charged.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} deterministic stdlib audit, seed
202610240063, 17 trials through $t=7$ with 13,650
assertion-accounted checks and zero failures, exhaustive clause
subsets through $t=3$.
The at-most-signed-$(2,2)$ smallest known 20-clause UNSAT
result is external (Zhang--Peitl--Szeider, SAT 2024), not
an internally re-encoded UNSAT source.
Verified standalone report, mini-auditor, raw summary and

The occurrence-degree bound $\delta^*\le h/3$ and Szeider's

endpoint is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}.
The machine-universal superpolynomial lower bound and

\paragraph{TWO-SIGN-SWAP64: exact two-literal semantic boundary on fixed signed support.}
The independently checked 198-variable, 264-clause signed exact-(2,2) UNSAT
source from DOECKER-CORE-CERT61 admits an explicitly checked SAT repair
that changes \emph{only two literal signs}, without changing any clause's
ordered unsigned variable identifiers. In zero-based source indexing,
$(\neg x_2\vee\neg x_3\vee\neg x_{194})$ at clause 3 becomes
$(\neg x_2\vee x_3\vee\neg x_{194})$, and
$(x_3\vee x_9\vee x_{10})$ at clause 13 becomes
$(\neg x_3\vee x_9\vee x_{10})$. A supplied 198-bit source
assignment satisfies all 264 repaired clauses and falsifies only clause 3
of the original. The original UNSAT certificate is imported and was not
reproved in this run. Both formulas have $n=198$, $m=k=264$,
$N=15{,}870$ bits, exact $(2,2)$ balance, and identical full unsigned
incidence data; the SAT repair is \emph{not strictly monotone}.
The two-sign Hamming distance is the minimum nonzero legal distance.
In general, legal signings of a fixed support-simple degree-four support form
$J(4,2)^n$ under one-positive/one-negative swaps, with diameter $2n$.
Thus any support admitting both labels has some opposite-label pair
separated by one swap, although discovering it efficiently is OPEN.
Disjoint products of the concrete pair give a direct
$p=\Omega(N/\log N)$ addressed-input-word lower bound on the all-SAT
input for every exact deterministic decider of the fixed signed
exact-(2,2) language; a charged $O(p)$ template scan matches it.
This is no superlinear SAT time lower bound. Report, independent witness
reproducer, raw ledger and handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-24_0149_LSPV-4_TWO_SIGN_SWAP64.tex}.
\textbf{OPEN:} the universal superpolynomial acquisition/compression/
continuation implication for all exact deterministic deciders.
$P$ versus $NP$ remains \OPEN{}.


\paragraph{EULER-NAE-DOUBLE65: linear-time complementary witnesses and sharp two-witness monotone repair.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT within the signing-construction model; Role~5 independent review pending:}
For every proper \(r\)-uniform unsigned clause support with \(r\ge2\) and
even original variable degrees \(d_v\), and any \emph{prescribed} source
assignment \(a\), pair the \(d_v\) original occurrence sockets of each
variable to form edges in an \(r\)-regular clause multigraph. Pair odd-degree
clause vertices by temporary dummy edges, Euler-orient, and delete dummy
edges. Each original graph edge chooses its head socket as TRUE under \(a\).
The resulting signing has exactly \(d_v/2\) positive and \(d_v/2\) negative
occurrences per original variable; each clause has between
\(\lfloor r/2\rfloor\) and \(\lceil r/2\rceil\) TRUE literals under
\emph{both} \(a\) and its complement. All source reads, derived adjacency,
Euler traversals, output and witness replay take \(O(rm+n)\) word operations
and linear space. In the signed exact-$(2,2)$ proper-three-CNF endpoint,
\(n=3t,m=4t\), both prescribed complementary assignments satisfy the
new formula and every clause is mixed-sign for \(a=1^n\).
This strengthens MAIN102/CAPACITY70's one-witness construction,
not the original signing's SAT label.

For any \emph{monotone} proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ source with \(m\)
clauses, pair each variable's positive and negative occurrence sockets.
Their induced graph on positive and negative clauses is 3-regular bipartite,
so Hall's theorem supplies a perfect matching. Flip both occurrences
on each matched edge. Every clause now contains both signs, each source
variable stays balanced, and the output is satisfied by \emph{both}
all-TRUE and all-FALSE. Exactly \(m\) literal-sign fields change, optimal
because each original monochromatic clause needs one change. This
strictly strengthens MAIN103's distance-\(m\) \emph{all-TRUE only} repair
without raising the optimum cost. Conservative matching time
\(O(m\sqrt m)\) with linear live workspace.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} seed 202610240265, 496 assertion-accounted
checks and zero failures for both witnesses at source sizes up to
\(n=198,m=264\), additional arities, even degrees, monotone optimal repairs
through \(m=256\), and exact 2-SAT, padding, Horn/XOR controls.
Finite tests do not prove runtime hardness. Verified report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-24_0234_LSPV-4_EULER_NAE_DOUBLE65.tex};
adjacent \path{_HANDOFF.md}, \path{_MINI.py}, \path{_RAW.json}
contain peer challenges and reproduction. No existing task schedules changed.

\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} a high-degree or unsigned-support
two-witness normalization necessarily forces expensive SAT decision work.
The sign-constructor intentionally changes the problem's signed input,
and a source-aware canonical-image recognizer plus arbitrary exact fallback
decides this promised SAT family in linear word time.
\textbf{OPEN:} arbitrary-decider superpolynomial post-acquisition
compression/continuation and \(P\ne NP\).

\paragraph{FROZEN-ANTIPODAL-CUT66: exact two-witness frozen-sign circulation and nonlocal cut.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT within the original-socket sign-completion model (independent Role~5 review pending):}
Fix the complete witness assignment $a$ on a proper 3-uniform degree-four
unsigned source with each variable's final polarity quota exactly two
positive and two negative occurrences. Freeze an arbitrary set of
source-socket signs. Let $R_v=2-p_v$ be the number of remaining
true-under-$a$ sockets required at variable $v$; each clause $C$
with $t_C$ frozen-true and $f_C$ free sockets requires between
$L_C=\max(0,1-t_C)$ and $U_C=\min(f_C,2-t_C)$ selected free sockets.
Completion satisfying \emph{both} $a$ and its complement is an exact
integral circulation problem. After local interval checks, feasibility is
equivalent to the following pair of inequalities, for \emph{all}
variable subsets $X$ and clause subsets $Y$:
\[
 \boxed{\begin{aligned}
 R(X)&\le |E_f(X,\mathcal C\setminus Y)|+U(Y),\\
 L(Y)&\le R(V\setminus X)+|E_f(X,Y)|.
 \end{aligned}}
\]
These are Hoffman lower/upper circulation cuts, tested by polynomial
max-flow rather than exponential cut enumeration. Integer min-cost
circulation also yields a cheapest two-witness edit.
This extends MAIN103's \emph{one-witness} frozen-sign flow
and EULER65's \emph{unfrozen} complementary-witness constructor.

A fixed $n=6,m=8$ simple unsigned degree-four support admits seven
frozen occurrence signs for which every variable and clause passes its
individual capacity checks, but global completion fails. An explicit
cut $X=\{0,1\}$, $Y=\{C_0\}$ has $R(X)=2$,
$|E_f(X,\mathcal C\setminus Y)|=0$, $U(Y)=1$.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} standard-library independent residual flow
and exhaustive $6^6=46656$ balanced-signing oracle agree on 522 pin
tests, and all quantified cut pairs were checked for 24 cases, with zero
mismatches. On the fixed eight-clause support 5904 balanced signings
satisfy both constant complementary witnesses. Verified standalone
report, minimal reproducer, result ledger and cross-role handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-24_0336_LSPV-4_FROZEN_ANTIPODAL_CUT66.tex}
and adjacent same-prefix artifacts.
No SAT-variable auxiliaries, clause-support changes or silent
source rewrites are used; all input reads, network construction, failed
augmentations, cut certificates, complete output signs, witness replays
and peak storage are charged. The sign-completion operation may change
the SAT label and is not a SAT-preserving reduction.

\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} unavoidable exponential work in this
fixed-witness frozen-sign compatibility task. \textbf{OPEN:}
a machine-universal superpolynomial lower bound on SAT for
\emph{unchanged} original signed exact-$(2,2)$ inputs.
$P$ versus $NP$ remains \OPEN{}.


\paragraph{PRIORITY-HORN-PARSIMONY67: parsimonious Horn binary/positive-ternary boundary (04:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the local calculus:} For each source proper ternary
$(\ell_1\vee\ell_2\vee\ell_3)$, introduce fresh selectors
$(s_1,s_2,s_3)$ and add the positive selector ternary, three Horn
implications $(\neg s_i\vee\ell_i)$ and three Horn priority guards
$(\neg s_2\vee\neg\ell_1)$,
$(\neg s_3\vee\neg\ell_1)$,
$(\neg s_3\vee\neg\ell_2)$. Every satisfying source assignment has
exactly one extension, selecting its first true literal. The uniform
parsimonious output on $n_0$ source variables and $m$ proper ternaries
has $n=n_0+3m$, $m_2=6m$, $k=m$, $M=7m$ and $28m+2$ explicit
fixed-width output words. The binary backbone is Horn and the positive
ternaries are selector-disjoint. The old
three-implication and new unique-selector lifts have identical
minimum strong syntactic 2-CNF and Horn backdoors, $m$ and $2m$,
despite different numbers of selector extensions. \textbf{PROVED/EXACT local
no-go:} Any selector lift in which each original literal occurs only
in the implication $(\neg s_i\vee\ell_i)$ and all other constraints
are source-independent cannot be parsimonious for any clause width
$h\ge2$. The distinct extensions for singleton true-literal sets
$\{i\}$ and $\{j\}$ must both survive when the true-literal set
grows to $\{i,j\}$. The three source-comparison guards explicitly
break this monotone-extension obstruction. A uniquely forced
three-variable proper ternary anchor needs at least seven clauses
within the fixed three-variable anchor calculus. Under the \emph{restricted comparison-guard
calculus}, exactly three guards are necessary and the six correct sets
are the six transitive tournaments; this is not an unrestricted minimum
circuit/encoding theorem. Compared with HORN-SELECTOR-DEGREE60, the new
lift removes the $\prod_j(2^{t_j}-1)$ auxiliary multiplicity but adds
complemented source literal appearances. It does not preserve physical
target occurrence degree four, even though a canonically decoded source
degree-four language remains NP-complete.

The original binary chain is parsimoniously properized using three
\emph{functional} forced-zero variables and seven anchor ternaries;
each binary clause gains one false anchor literal. Applying the priority
lift gives $n=4q+39$, $m_2=6q+66$, $k=q+11$, $M=7q+77$ and
$N=(28q+310)\lceil\log_2(64(11q+117))\rceil$. A flip of one
orientation changes two private literal words. On the all-one UNSAT
input, every exact deterministic decider of the full canonical language
must read at least $q=\Omega(N/\log N)$ words; the canonical source
recognizer matches this in $O(q)$ word operations. Explicit
materialization spends $\Theta(q)$ charged writes, so paid lower-bound
transport has no positive amplification. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} seeded
stdlib audit, 1692 assertion-accounted checks, 64 comparison-guard
subsets, 36 small full-model-count instances and 254 chain orientation
controls, zero failures. The counting-preserving lift does not
prove any universal SAT-time lower bound; $P$ versus $NP$ remains
\OPEN{}. Verified role report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-24_0436_LSPV-4_PRIORITY_HORN_PARSIMONY67.tex};
mini-auditor, raw ledger and peer handoff share its prefix.


\paragraph{HIGH-ORDER-SYNDROME78: all proper syndrome projections can be full (12:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, growing explicit family:} For \(t\ge3\),
take the 2-CNF source \(B=(x_1\lor x_2)\) and the invertible
syndrome \(y_1=x_1\oplus P,\ y_2=x_2\oplus P,\ y_j=x_j\ (j\ge3)\),
where \(P=\bigoplus_{j=3}^{t}x_j\). The image of \(B\)'s satisfying
assignments in \(\mathbb F_2^t\) has size \(3\cdot2^{t-2}\), full affine
hull, and \emph{every strict coordinate projection full}, but it
excludes \(0^t\). All unit vectors \(e_j\) are admitted. This is an
arbitrary-order generalization of DUAL-PARITY-JOINT77's marginal hole.
Despite growing projection order, the exact membership predicate is
\((b_1\oplus P_b)\lor(b_2\oplus P_b)\), a degree-two Boolean polynomial
with an \(O(t)\) source-readable solver. High compatibility projection
order does not imply expensive one-bit SAT decision.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT, proper binary/ternary realization:} The same
syndrome pins compile to \(G_{t,b}\), with
\[
n=3t-2,\qquad m_2=4t-3,\qquad k=4t-4,\qquad
M=8t-7,\qquad
N=(32t-26)\left\lceil\log_2(64(11t-8))\right\rceil.
\]
The compiler keeps \(t\) original variables, uses \(2t-3\)
functional parity auxiliaries and one free pin variable,
and has exactly two target models for \(b\) in the syndrome image
and zero otherwise. In the fixed-width addressed-word grammar,
the UNSAT input \(b=0^t\) is sensitive to each of \(t\) private pin
groups, yielding an all-decider \(t=\Omega(N/\log N)\) \emph{acquisition}
lower bound, matched by the linear canonical recognizer and
state-adaptive XOR/SCC/Horn/Gaussian/DPLL-fallback policy. The
minimum raw syntactic 2-CNF backdoor lies between
\(\lceil(t-1)/3\rceil\) and \(t-1\), but after paid pin resolution,
prefix propagation and source checking the effective ternary
residual is zero. The comparison with full-table enumeration is
\emph{restricted-policy only}. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:}
seed 202610240436, 375 assertion-accounted groups, zero failures,
both labels through \(t=4096\), exhaustive marginal controls through
\(t=11\), model-count and hostile adaptive-switching controls.
Verified report and same-prefix executable/raw/handoff artifacts:
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-24_1236_LSPV-4_HIGH_ORDER_SYNDROME78.tex}.

\paragraph{BALANCE-HEADER-ORACLE79: exact normalization source-acquisition firewall (13:52 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted:} For the original
\texttt{PC22\_ALLFIELD1} normalizer $U(F)$, let
$r_v=\max(p_v,n_v,2)$, $g(F)=\sum_v(2r_v-p_v-n_v)$, and

The opposite-label source controls consist of $q\ge3$ proper
$(\ell_i\vee a_i\vee d_i)$ clauses with a common signed $x$,
with or without a disjoint all-eight-sign-pattern UNSAT core.
At the all-positive source, $g=7q$ in both controls, and
\[
(V,M)_{\mathrm{SAT}}=(450q,600q),\qquad
(V,M)_{\mathrm{UNSAT}}=(450q+72,600q+96).
\]
Flipping any one source orientation decreases $g$ by 2,
$V$ by 126 and $M$ by 168, thereby toggling the second
least-significant bit of the serialized variable-count header.
Every exact deterministic \emph{source-flat} evaluator of this one output
bit must query all $q$ private orientation fields on either source label,
an $\Omega(N/\log N)$ addressed-word acquisition result.
A fully charged $O(q)$ source scan and $O(\log q)$-bit cached metadata
make subsequent header access constant-word-time. Full target
serialization emits $\Theta(q)$ records, with no ARC child contraction
and no paid positive lower-bound amplification.

\paragraph{SENSITIVITY-SATURATION80: exact source-read saturation for every explicit truth-preserving transport (14:36 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the separately addressed source-field model, with independent peer review pending:}
For the canonical proper-binary chain $F_b$ of $q$ independently oriented
fields, $U=F_{1^q}$ is UNSAT and each single-field flip is SAT with
the same input length and metadata.
Every deterministic exact source-to-target transducer, even one emitting
a constant-size ordinary SAT target, must read all $q$ orientation
fields on $U$: omission of field $i$ leaves an identical
execution transcript on the opposite-label one-flip neighbor.
The argument also holds for adaptive oracle Turing reductions whose
target queries are fully serialized, self-contained inputs and whose
source reads are charged outside the oracle.
Thus the earlier \texttt{INPUT-QUERY-CEILING86}
transport inequality, applied only to the original source bound

\[
L_{\rm old}-R_q(U)-H_q\le q-q-H_q\le0.
\]
This \emph{retire}s positive lower-bound transport from that
$q$-query source argument through any such explicit compiler; it
does not restrict a future independently proved post-acquisition bound
or an existential all-input ARC successor.

A charged binary-to-proper-ternary padding encoder has
$n=2q+7$, $M=2q+8$ and $q+4$ free new variables, and must
read all $q$ source orientations. An explicit decide-first encoder
can emit one of two constant-size clause-distinct proper-3CNF
targets, both $n=4,m=8,N=340$ bits: an UNSAT eight-clause
partition of the Boolean four-cube and a SAT target formed by

Even this equal-metadata constant-output transformation still
reads every field on the all-one UNSAT source.
The associated adaptive scanner charges source recognition,
policy selection, early-zero detection, output writing,
and optional source witness reconstruction; no true label is supplied.

The source model uses
$N=(4q+18)\lceil\log_2(128(q+4))\rceil$ bits,
so the only all-decider bound is $q=\Omega(N/\log N)$
addressed-field acquisition, not unrestricted SAT decision time.

\paragraph{BLOCK-CLOSED-ORACLE85: all proper edge-block counts and strict-subset statuses.}
The verified standalone report
\path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-24_1910_LSPV-4_BLOCK_CLOSED_ORACLE85.tex}
and peer code/results/handoff use the same dated prefix.
For $q\ge3$, encode $x_i\oplus x_{i+1}=b_i$ on a cycle, two binary
clauses per edge, and properize each binary clause $C$ as
$(C\vee u)\wedge(C\vee\neg u)$ with a private $u$.
The connected duplicate-free parent has
\[
n=3q,\quad m=k=4q,\quad
N=(16q+2)\lceil\log_2(64(7q+1))\rceil.
\]
It has $2^{2q+1}$ full models for even parity and zero for odd.
Every proper selection $S\subsetneq[q]$ of \emph{whole}
four-clause edge blocks has exactly $2^{3q-|S|}$
models on the declared variable universe, independent of $b$.
An odd-parity parent is clause-minimally UNSAT, so every strict
individual-clause subset on either label is SAT.
Thus no source-blind adaptive controller using only those two
\emph{typed} oracles can recover the parent label.
Even with source field reads it must inspect $q$ independent
orientation groups, matched by a paid parity scan:
$\Omega(N/\log N)$ addressed-word acquisition, not superlinear
post-acquisition time.

\textbf{Sharp counterquery:}
a strict child retaining $q-1$ whole edge blocks plus
\emph{both} ternary twins of one closing binary half has
$2^{2q+1}$ models when parity is even and $2^{2q}$
when odd. Hence one free \emph{non-block-closed exact count}
would defeat the count-only indistinguishability, while
the child remains SAT on both labels.
The local independent finite audit reports 38,342 charged
assertions and zero failures; the compact peer script
tests a strict subset of those claims.
No uncharged exact-count service is admitted as an
ordinary FLMV SAT machine.
\textbf{OPEN:} a source-grounded, all-input,
same-machine favorable time-space ARC edge and the cofinal route.
P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.


\paragraph{PAD-FLOOR-SCOPED87: charged two-sided scoped-child length floor (20:47 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only for certified valid closed scoped proper-3CNF streams;
independent peer audit pending.}
Appending $g$ fresh independent one-clause, three-variable OPEN/CLAUSE/CLOSE
blocks multiplies the number of satisfying assignments by $7^g$ while
preserving the SAT label and every old boundary projection.
For the fixed-width event encoding
$L(n,m)=(2+4n+4m)\lceil\log_2(64(n+m+1))\rceil$,
the least $g$ meeting any floor $B\ge L(n,m)$ satisfies
\[
 B\le L(n+3g,m+g)\le 2B+O(\log(B+2)),\qquad
 w'\le\max\{w,3\}.
\]
A sequential paid generator takes $O((L+B)\operatorname{polylog}B)$
elementary bit work with $O(\log B)$ controller bits, excluding paid
validation, retained ancestors and any $\Theta(B)$ readable child buffer.
Under an \emph{independently proved}, correct scoped child factory with
bounded width increase, this repairs the missing \emph{lower} child-length
bound for any fixed recursion depth, as required by the earlier
CHILD-SCALE-PREFIX117 counterexample. It supplies \emph{no} all-input
normalizer, compatibility constructor or achieved SAT exponent pair.
The deterministic local audit records 151,274 checks and zero discrepancies,
including matched SAT/UNSAT proper-clause sources; large scales are
numerical tests and physical FLMV tape metrics remain unproved.

\subsection*{M22-UNPAIRED-ANCHOR96: canonical strict monotone target transport (25 October 2026)}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the declared proper monotone exact-(2,2) grammar; independent peer review pending.}
Canonical target \(\mathcal C_\star\) consists of unique proper three-distinct-variable,
clausewise uniform-sign CNFs with exactly \(2+\) and \(2-\) occurrences per active
variable, \emph{excluding only fully complementary-clause-paired formulas}.
Individual complementary pairs, disconnected components and easy islands are
allowed. For \(k\) active variables, \(m=4k/3\) and \(m_+=m_-=2k/3\).
Literalwise complement \(\bar C\) retains OR.

Using positive clause supports \(P=\{123,145,246,356\}\) and negative supports
\(Q=\{124,136,235,456\}\), let \(H=P^+\cup P^-\) and \(Y=P^+\cup Q^-\).
Both are valid 6-variable, 8-clause target-source cores; \(H\) is fully paired
with 18 satisfying assignments and \(Y\) has no complementary partners,
20 assignments and the explicit witness \(111000\). The no-test
anchor \(F\mapsto F\dot\cup Y\), with fresh variables, sends \emph{every valid}
\(F\in\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}\) into \(\mathcal C_\star\), preserves its SAT bit,
multiplies its model count by 20 and provides an exact witness map.
The full source grammar remains essential; the signed TWO-SIGN-SWAP64 SAT mutant
is not necessarily monotone and cannot be promoted to this target.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT matched hostile controls.}
Let \(U\) be D\"ocker's 198-variable, 264-clause UNSAT strict monotone core
(known theorem; source grammar rechecked), and put
\[
 A_p=H^{\dot\cup(p+33)}\dot\cup Y\in\mathrm{SAT},\qquad
 B_p=U\dot\cup H^{\dot\cup p}\dot\cup Y\in\mathrm{UNSAT}.
\]
For every \(p\ge0\), these are \emph{both} in \(\mathcal C_\star\) and
have identical \(k=6p+204\), \(m=8p+272\) and dense encoded length.
With \(D(F)=|\{C\in F:\bar C\notin F\}|\),
\[
D(A_p)=8,\quad D(B_p)=272,\quad
D(A_p)/m=1/(p+34)\to0,\quad D(B_p)/m=34/(p+34)\to0.
\]
Thus neither target membership nor opposite SAT labels require positive
unpaired-clause density. The source-aware canonical-block recognizer is an easy
restricted policy with paid \(O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)\) work and
\(O(\log N)\) live writable/index bits; it is not all-decider coverage.

For the explicitly declared unary-width header and fixed signed-ID records,
\(N=3w+2+3m(w+1)\), \(w=\lceil\log_2(k+1)\rceil\).
The anchor has the exact length increase
\[
 N'-N=3(m+1)(w'-w)+24(w'+1),\qquad
 w'=\lceil\log_2(k+7)\rceil,\quad w'-w\in\{0,1\},
\]
so \(N'=N(1+O(1/\log k))\). A valid-promise virtual accessor takes
polylogarithmic bit work per output bit and \(O(\log N)\) live space;
total malformed-input validation by an explicitly paid nested-scan routine
costs \(O(N^2\operatorname{polylog}N)\) bit work.
This only transports a hypothetical target solver to the restricted
\(\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}\) language with these losses. It does \emph{not}
derive the FLMV ordinary-SAT forbidden box from NP-completeness.
The corrected FLMV box remains \(t<241/200\),
\(s<11642479/23232400\); the old cosine-product endpoint is retired.
No all-input ARC-EDGE-90 successor or noncircular post-acquisition
superpolynomial potential is obtained; P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.
Report: \path{research/agents/lspv-4/2026-10-25_0345_LSPV-4_M22_UNPAIRED_ANCHOR96.tex}.

\section{GUARD-CONTINUATION-FIXEDPOINT126 update (LSPV-5)}
For any ordered proper 3CNF \(F\), define one guard layer by replacing
each \((\ell_1\vee\ell_2\vee\ell_3)\) with
\[
(\neg x\vee\ell_1\vee a)\wedge(\neg a\vee\ell_2\vee\ell_3),
\]
using one shared fresh guard and clause-local auxiliaries.  After \(d\)
fresh iterations, the exact feasible relation on the \(d\) guards is
\(\{0,1\}^d\) when \(F\) is SAT and
\(\{0,1\}^d\setminus\{1^d\}\) when \(F\) is UNSAT.  Thus the root is
always SAT, every boundary word except \(1^d\) is automatically feasible,
and the \(1^d\) corner is exactly the original SAT problem.

Pinning every guard true by proper four-clause unit gadgets gives an
ordinary proper 3CNF equivalent to \(F\) with
\[
M_d=2^dm+4d,\qquad n_d=n+(2^d-1)m+3d.
\]
For fixed \(d\), its encoded length is that of the guarded parent up to a
\(1+o(1)\) factor.  Hence the continuation exponent is \(\lambda=1\), not
\(1/2\), and the honest one-call recurrence is
\(t'\le\max\{r,t\}\): no strict ARC time contraction follows.  A full
table still includes this one full-size corner.  Conversely, a uniform
adaptive root decider can select the outer guard-zero witness and need not
acquire the exact boundary relation at all.  This is
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT for the declared transform}, not an unrestricted SAT
lower bound.

The finite audit covers 325 base formulas (all 255 nonempty signed-clause
subsets on three variables, explicit affine/Horn/padded-2SAT SAT and UNSAT
controls, and 64 seeded random formulas), with 11,198
assertion-accounted semantic checks and zero failures.  Report, code, raw
results and handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-24_1952_LSPV-5_GUARD_CONTINUATION_FIXEDPOINT126.tex}.
The exact remaining obligation is one all-input, jointly charged uniform
route into a verified FLMV forbidden box that removes the full-size hard
corner without deciding the source first or hiding compatibility in an
oracle.  P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.


\section{BOUNDARY-SUPPORT-UNIVERSALITY127 update (LSPV-5)}
For every ordered proper 3CNF \(F\), boundary dimension \(d\ge1\), and
\(1\le h\le2^d\), a uniform linear-record proper-3CNF construction has
exact pinned-boundary function
\[
Q_{F,d,h}(b)=[{\rm unsigned}(b)<h]\vee{\rm SAT}(F).
\]
The unpinned root is always SAT via \(b=0\).  If \(F\) is UNSAT, exactly
\(h\) boundary words are feasible and exactly \(2^d-h\) memberships carry
the source SAT bit.  Thus every hard-membership count from zero through
\(2^d-1\) coexists with a source-independent root witness.  Role~1's
DENSE-BOUNDARY-BYPASS128 is the \(h=1\) extreme, while
GUARD-CONTINUATION-FIXEDPOINT126 is the \(h=2^d-1\) extreme after boundary
renaming.

All hard memberships share the single bit \({\rm SAT}(F)\); an
\(O(d)\)-gate comparator plus that bit determines the whole boundary
function.  Explicit table output may still cost \(\Omega(2^d)\), but a
one-bit root decider need not compile it.  Pinning any hard boundary word
produces an ordinary child of the same asymptotic encoded size as the
parent, so \(\lambda=1\) and the transport recurrence yields only
\(t'\le\max\{r,t\}\).  Boundary-support density, cardinality and multiplicity
are therefore \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as standalone ARC progress
measures}; this does not refute a different all-input semantic contractor.

The executable audit covered 326 source formulas, 4,138 constructed roots,
40,380 boundary restrictions, 4,138 exact support counts and 2,976 hard
corners: 52,284 assertion-accounted checks with zero failures, including
affine SAT/UNSAT, proper Horn and dual-Horn SAT, padded-2SAT SAT/UNSAT, and
independent-component controls.  Report, code, raw result and handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-24_2052_LSPV-5_BOUNDARY_SUPPORT_UNIVERSALITY127.tex}.

The original HEREDITARY-SCOPED10 proof body/executable remains unavailable;
only its continuity summary was recovered.  The exact all-input,
same-machine favorable ARC successor or cofinal finite route into a
verified FLMV forbidden box remains \OPEN{}.  P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.
